<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：open-vocabulary segmentation（开放词汇分割），属于稠密预测任务。场景：通用视觉场景，非遥感。传感器类型：摘要未说明（基于 CLIP 视觉特征，通常为自然图像）。目标类别：开放词汇，未限定具体类别。

Model architecture: SCI-CLIP 推理框架：1) 在冻结视觉 token 上诱导 region-consistent interaction graph（区域一致交互图）；2) 通过在该图上传播值来重建稠密特征，并在局部证据不足时选择性加入 cross-window support（跨窗口支持）；3) 用同一 segment 抽象构建并查询 offline reference memory（离线参考记忆），使示例检索与预测单元对齐。全程基于冻结的 CLIP-style 特征，无训练。

Lightweight method: 核心卖点是 training-free（无需训练），即推理阶段不更新参数，直接利用冻结的 CLIP 特征。但摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标，也未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等经典轻量化手段。因此轻量化信息不足。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等星上/边缘部署相关信息。虽然 training-free 在概念上有利于部署（无需训练开销），但推理阶段构建交互图、跨窗口传播、参考记忆检索的计算成本未知，无法判断在轨可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 在八个 benchmark 上评估（摘要未列出具体数据集名称）。评价指标聚焦稠密预测的结构质量、上下文推理鲁棒性、示例校正对齐度，具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明 SCI-CLIP 在八个 benchmark 上一致提升了稠密预测的结构质量、上下文推理鲁棒性以及基于示例校正的对齐度。未给出具体数值、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，场景不匹配；2) 轻量化信息严重不足，无任何部署指标；3) 推理阶段图构建与参考记忆检索的计算开销未知，可能不满足实时/在轨约束；4) 摘要未给出具体数据集、指标数值与对比基线，难以量化其优势；5) 开放词汇分割与遥感目标检测（尤其是小目标、旋转框检测）任务形式差异大。

Ideas for my research: 1) "training-free" 思路可借鉴：在遥感在轨场景中，若能用冻结的预训练特征配合轻量推理模块完成检测，可避免星上训练开销；2) region-consistent interaction graph 与 cross-window support 的"局部证据不足时选择性补充上下文"思想，可迁移到遥感小目标检测中，用于在有限感受野内增强上下文；3) offline reference memory 的检索校正机制，可类比遥感目标检测中的模板/示例匹配，但需评估星上存储与检索成本。总体借鉴价值有限，需自行验证在遥感数据上的适用性。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary segmentation remains limited by a missing inference abstraction. Frozen vision-language features are produced at patch level, yet dense prediction requires a unit that simultaneously governs feature interaction, spatial support, contextual recovery, and retrieval-based correction. We present SCI-CLIP, a segment-centric inference framework built around the principle that the same region abstraction should organize all stages of dense open-vocabulary prediction. SCI-CLIP first induces a region-consistent interaction graph over frozen visual tokens, then reconstructs dense features by propagating values over this graph, augmenting them with selective cross-window support only where local evidence is insufficient. The same segment abstraction is subsequently used to construct and query an offline reference memory, aligning exemplar retrieval with the units on which prediction is made. SCI-CLIP turns frozen CLIP-style features into spatially coherent, context-aware, and retrieval-compatible dense predictions without any training. SCI-CLIP consistently improves the structural quality of dense predictions, the robustness of contextual reasoning, and the alignment of exemplar-based correction, yielding stronger open-vocabulary segmentation across eight benchmarks. Project code is available at: https://github.com/mzamini92/SCICLIP.

</details>


### [34] [ChronoVision: Temporal Reasoning via Latent State Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05631)
*Yifan Shen,Jian Xu,Boyi Li,Yuner Zhang,Tianjiao Yu,Bingxuan Li,Houze Yang,Rushi Wang,Xu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChronoVision多模态大语言模型框架，通过重构视觉头、ROI注意力定位和强化学习解决多步时序视觉推理中的语言歧义问题，在Vbvr-VQA和IntPhys2基准上取得SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多步时序视觉推理（multi-step temporal reasoning）、视觉问答(VQA)；应用场景：通用视频/图像时序理解；传感器类型：自然图像/视频；目标类别：无特定目标类别。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: ChronoVision框架包含：Reconstructive Visual Head（重构视觉头，预测最终变换状态的潜在表示）、ROI Attention Locating module（ROI注意力定位模块，通过语义跨度查询聚焦关键视觉证据）、强化学习后训练阶段（implicit process grounding mechanism，隐式过程对齐机制）。基础架构为多模态大语言模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署相关内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。多模态大语言模型通常参数量巨大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Vbvr-VQA（新提出的视频推理数据集，将视频推理重构为严格图像排序任务）、IntPhys2（跨域基准）；评价指标：准确率（accuracy）；实验结果：Vbvr-VQA上in-domain 74.8%、out-of-domain 71.6%，IntPhys2上55.0%准确率。

Experiments: 在Vbvr-VQA和IntPhys2上达到SOTA。摘要未说明与哪些基线方法对比，未提供消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关；2. 多模态大语言模型计算开销极大，不适合边缘/星上部署；3. 未提供模型参数量、推理速度等效率指标；4. 仅在通用视觉推理数据集上验证，未在遥感场景下测试。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) ROI Attention Locating模块的语义跨度查询机制，可类比遥感图像中基于语义引导的感兴趣区域聚焦，用于大场景遥感图像中的小目标检测；2) 隐式过程对齐的强化学习奖励设计思路，可用于遥感目标检测中的多阶段检测流程优化。但整体而言，该论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接关联性很低。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models excel at passive perception but struggle with complex visual cognitive tasks requiring multi-step temporal reasoning. This degradation largely stems from the inherent ambiguity of language-based reasoning, which often fails to accurately articulate continuous visual transformations. To address this, we propose ChronoVision, a multimodal framework designed to align visual logic with latent imagery. During supervised fine-tuning, a Reconstructive Visual Head predicts the latent representation of the final transformed state, while an ROI Attention Locating module focuses the model on key visual evidence via semantic span queries. In post-training, we apply reinforcement learning with an implicit process grounding mechanism, guided by a composite reward function that evaluates outcome correctness, latent process alignment, and unsupervised visual focus. Furthermore, we introduce Vbvr-VQA, a novel dataset that evaluates temporal tracking by reformulating video reasoning into a strict image-ordering task. Experiments demonstrate that ChronoVision achieves state-of-the-art performance on Vbvr-VQA with 74.8% in-domain and 71.6% out-of-domain accuracy, alongside a strong 55.0% accuracy on IntPhys2, a highly challenging cross-domain benchmark.

</details>


### [35] [Vorch-IR: Long-Form Unified Multimodal Identity Replacement Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05648)
*Yaole Wang,Xiaoyu Chen,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Jingjing Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vorch-IR，一个统一框架，支持单人和双人视频身份替换及背景替换，基于LTX2架构，通过自注意力融合视觉条件、跨注意力建立语义对应，并设计了自动数据构建管线。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于视频身份替换（video identity replacement），属于视频生成/编辑领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视频身份替换（single- and dual-person identity replacement），背景替换；场景：视频编辑/生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：人物身份。

Model architecture: 基于LTX2架构，通过自注意力（self-attention）融合密集视觉条件，通过跨注意力（cross-attention）建立视觉-语言语义对应。支持驱动视频、索引参考图像和文本编辑指令作为条件输入。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Experiments: 实验表明在身份保持（identity preservation）、运动保真度（motion fidelity）和时间一致性（temporal coherence）方面表现良好。提出时间重叠推理策略（temporal overlapping inference strategy）将短片段模型扩展到分钟级生成。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于视频生成/编辑领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Video identity replacement seeks to transfer the identities of one or more subjects while preserving the motion, expressions, and temporal structure of a driving video. Existing methods largely target single-person settings and often require task-specific structural controls, such as masks or pose representations, limiting their flexibility in general multimodal editing systems. Progress on multi-person replacement is further constrained by the scarcity of paired training data. We present Vorch-IR, a unified framework that supports single- and dual-person identity replacement, with optional background replacement, in a single model. Built on LTX2, Vorch-IR jointly conditions on a driving video, indexed reference images, and a textual editing instruction. The reference images need not match the pose, layout, or spatial configuration of the driving video: their roles as subject or background references are specified through the instruction. Dense visual conditions are fused through self-attention, while a vision-language context establishes semantic correspondence through cross-attention. We further develop an automatic data construction pipeline that synthesizes paired supervision for all four editing settings. Experiments using automatic metrics and pairwise human evaluation demonstrate strong identity preservation, motion fidelity, and temporal coherence across diverse scenarios. A temporal overlapping inference strategy additionally extends the short-clip model to minute-long generation without autoregressive continuation.

</details>


### [36] [Vorch-Streamer: Extending Human Audio-Visual Generation to Real-Time Long-Form Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05663)
*Menglin Han,Yang Ding,Yulei Lu,Haoran Yu,Xin Ma,Junyi Chen,Zhangkai Ni,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Vorch-Streamer，一个后训练框架，通过混合 Teacher Forcing 与 Diffusion Forcing 训练因果生成器，并采用长时程 Self Forcing 与 DMD 蒸馏，实现实时长时程文本到音视频（T2AV）流式生成，达到 27.12 FPS 的实时播放速度。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，核心是"文本到音视频生成"（Text-to-Audio-Video, T2AV），涉及数字人/头像（avatar）的音频-视频同步生成，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测或遥感场景理解。虽然涉及"蒸馏（distillation）"和"实时推理（real-time, FPS）"等与轻量化/部署相关的概念，但应用场景是音视频生成而非遥感目标检测，因此仅作为泛机器学习方法弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于音视频生成领域。虽然涉及蒸馏和实时推理概念，但应用场景差异过大，对遥感目标检测轻量化课题的借鉴价值很低，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：实时长时程文本到音视频生成（T2AV streaming），即数字人/头像（avatar）的音频-视频联合生成。应用场景：虚拟数字人、音视频同步生成，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 采用预训练双向模型（bidirectional teacher）作为教师，通过后训练（post-training）适配为因果生成器（causal generator）。关键模块包括：混合 Teacher Forcing 与 Diffusion Forcing 训练、长时程 Self Forcing、DMD 蒸馏、外部语言模型预测 25-Hz 离散语音规划 token、音频扩散分支、四步去噪（four-step denoising）。

Lightweight method: 涉及 DMD 蒸馏（distillation）将预训练双向教师模型蒸馏到因果生成器，以及四步去噪（four-step denoising）加速采样。这些属于模型压缩/加速范畴，但面向的是音视频生成模型而非遥感目标检测模型。未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、NAS、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到实时性指标：27.12 FPS，超过 24-FPS 实时播放速率。但未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标，也未涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明具体硬件平台。

Datasets and metrics: 构建了 80K 个 avatar 视频片段（时长 12-21 秒）的合成语料库。评价指标包括：音频-唇形同步（audio-lip synchronization）、身份保持（identity preservation）、FPS（27.12 FPS）。未涉及遥感数据集或目标检测指标（如 mAP）。

Experiments: 摘要仅给出整体结果：Vorch-Streamer 以 27.12 FPS 联合生成音频和视频，超过 24-FPS 实时播放速率，同时保持有竞争力的音频-唇形同步和强身份保持。未给出与基线方法的详细对比、消融实验细节或具体数值表格。

Limitations: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标；未给出与基线方法的详细定量对比；未说明在真实硬件上的部署验证；未涉及遥感场景。摘要未说明模型在长时程生成中的误差累积上限、身份漂移的具体量化结果。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，但可借鉴以下方法论：1) Self Forcing（让模型暴露于自身 rollout 分布）与 DMD 蒸馏结合的思想，可用于遥感目标检测模型的蒸馏训练，缓解教师-学生分布偏移；2) 混合 Teacher Forcing 与 Diffusion Forcing 的训练策略，可类比于检测模型训练中的多阶段/多尺度训练；3) 外部语言模型预测离散 token 来控制生成进度的思路，可类比于在轨检测中利用外部先验（如轨道/姿态信息）引导检测；4) 四步去噪加速采样的思想，可类比于检测模型推理加速。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为核心任务（音视频生成）与遥感目标检测差异较大。

Abstract: Real-time long-form avatar audio--video generation requires causal, continuous synthesis while maintaining audiovisual synchronization and visual consistency. Adapting a pretrained bidirectional model to this setting presents two key dilemmas. First, autoregressively reusing generated blocks as context creates exposure bias, causing errors and visual drift to accumulate over long rollouts. Second, a global speech utterance does not indicates a causal generator which portion should be spoken next when only limited local audio--video context is available. We present \textbf{Vorch-Streamer}, a post-training framework that addresses these challenges and enables real-time long-form Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) streaming. We construct a synthetic corpus of 80K avatar clips spanning 12--21 seconds and first train a causal generator with mixed Teacher Forcing and Diffusion Forcing. We then apply long-horizon Self Forcing with DMD distillation, exposing the model to its own rollout distribution while preserving the quality of the pretrained bidirectional teacher. To explicitly control speech progression, an external language model predicts discrete 25-Hz speech-planning tokens, whose continuous features condition the audio diffusion branch and align each causal block with the content it should speak. With bounded causal context and four-step denoising, Vorch-Streamer jointly generates audio and video from text at 27.12 FPS, exceeding the 24-FPS real-time playback rate while maintaining competitive audio--lip synchronization and strong identity preservation over long-form generation.

</details>

Abstract: Millimeter-wave human activity recognition suffers significant performance degradation when the user's orientation changes relative to the sensing system, yet collecting labeled multi-orientation data is labor-intensive and costly. To eliminate the need for exhaustive multi-orientation measured data, we develop a physics-guided simulator that synthesizes orientation-diverse wireless training data from single-orientation motion. Specifically, to suppress orientation-induced feature variations, we propose a dual-attention network that extracts activity-discriminative and orientation-robust representations from dual-link Doppler spectrograms. To bridge the simulation-to-reality gap, we introduce an adversarial unsupervised transfer learning mechanism that aligns feature distributions using only a small number of unlabeled target-domain samples. The S2M-Sense platform shows high fidelity in reproducing real-world signatures, validated against 60.48 GHz mmWave measured data with an average structural similarity index measure (SSIM) of 0.84 between simulated and measured Doppler spectrograms across all 4 activities and 4 orientations. Experimental results show that S2M-Sense achieves 88.33% recognition accuracy using only the dual-link multi-orientation simulated dataset, which improves to 95% after simulation-to-reality transfer learning with as few as 16 unlabeled measured samples. Both cases with and without transfer learning outperform state-of-the-art cross-domain sensing methods.

</details>

Ideas for my research: 1. 重参数化（Reparameterization）策略可借鉴：在遥感目标检测模型中，可将训练时的复杂结构（如多分支）通过重参数化合并为推理时的单分支结构，兼顾训练精度与推理速度；2. 单编码器统一多模态特征提取与融合的思路，可迁移到遥感多模态（如光学+SAR、多光谱+高光谱）目标检测中，减少双分支冗余；3. Linear Gated Attention (LGA) 模块的设计思路可用于遥感多模态特征融合；4. 但需注意：该论文是语义分割任务，迁移到目标检测需重新设计检测头；且需在遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、SSDD等）上验证。

Abstract: Previous RGB-D semantic segmentation methods commonly employ dual encoders to separately process RGB and depth inputs, followed by dedicated modules for cross-modal feature fusion. However, such designs often inadequately capture depth representations and consequently limit effective cross-modal interaction, while the additional encoder branch introduces redundant computation that hinders lightweight execution. To tackle these challenges, we propose URNet, a Unified Reparameterized RGB-D Network that performs simultaneous multi-modal feature extraction and cross-modal fusion within a single encoder. Specifically, we adopt a reparameterization strategy to compact the network architecture and facilitate fast inference. Within each Reparameterized Block (RepBlock), a Linear Gated Attention (LGA) module is introduced to fully exploit complementary RGB and depth cues across different feature scales. Furthermore, considering that decoder design has been relatively underexplored in existing RGB-D segmentation models, we develop a concise yet effective universal decoder, termed the Pyramid Merging Decoder (PMD). Extensive experiments on multiple RGB-D segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that URNet achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining high efficiency. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wild-Stephen/URNet.

</details>

*Jiaxuan Li,Qing Xu,Xiangjian He,Yue Li,Daokun Zhang,Fiseha B. Tesema,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DistMedVL，一个面向多模态医学图像分割的概率视觉-语言框架，通过轻量级 Probabilistic Cross-Modal Adapter（PCM-Adapter）在冻结编码器上显式建模表征不确定性，仅用 6.3M 可训练参数在八个医学分割基准上超越 SOTA。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2 分）。该论文是医学图像（medical image）领域的视觉-语言分割方法，与遥感/卫星/在轨部署场景无关，也不涉及目标检测。虽然其"轻量级 adapter + 冻结编码器"的少参数设计思路对轻量化有间接借鉴意义，但任务（分割而非检测）、场景（医学而非遥感）均不匹配，属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法。

Reading priority: 2 分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，任务和场景均不匹配，仅"冻结编码器+轻量 adapter"的参数高效思路和概率不确定性建模有间接借鉴价值，优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态医学图像分割（vision-language segmentation）。场景：临床医学影像，非遥感/卫星。传感器类型：医学影像（摘要未说明具体模态，如 CT/MRI/超声等）。目标类别：医学解剖结构/病灶（摘要未具体说明）。

Model architecture: DistMedVL 框架：冻结的视觉和文本编码器 + 轻量级 Probabilistic Cross-Modal Adapter（PCM-Adapter）。PCM-Adapter 由两个顺序模块组成：(1) Mahalanobis Alignment Module (MAM)，将文本 token 建模为高斯分布，用 Mahalanobis 距离计算 patch-text 兼容性，实现方差条件匹配；(2) Distribution Flow Module (DFM)，估计模态级置信度参数，进行视觉引导的文本分布精炼。

Lightweight method: 采用"冻结编码器 + 轻量级 adapter"的参数高效范式，仅训练 PCM-Adapter，总可训练参数仅 6.3M。属于参数高效微调（PEFT）思路，而非剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS 等传统压缩方法。摘要未说明 FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。仅给出可训练参数 6.3M，未提供 FLOPs、推理延迟、显存占用、能耗或实时性等星上/边缘部署相关指标。且任务为医学分割，与在轨遥感目标检测场景不匹配。

Datasets and metrics: 八个医学分割基准（eight medical segmentation benchmarks），摘要未列出具体数据集名称。评价指标摘要未具体说明（通常为 Dice 等分割指标，但摘要未明确）。

Experiments: 在八个医学分割基准上，DistMedVL 以 6.3M 可训练参数超越 SOTA 方法，表现出更好的数据效率（data efficiency）、扰动鲁棒性（perturbation robustness）和跨数据集泛化（cross-dataset generalization）。摘要未给出具体数值对比和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。主要缺口：无 FLOPs/延迟/显存/能耗等部署指标；无具体数据集名称与指标数值；任务为医学分割而非遥感目标检测；未涉及旋转框、小目标、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感特性；未涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏等压缩手段。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不匹配，仍有两点可借鉴：(1) "冻结骨干 + 轻量 adapter"的参数高效范式，可迁移到遥感在轨检测中——冻结大模型骨干、仅训练轻量检测头/adapter，以极低参数量实现部署；(2) 概率建模思想（将特征建模为分布、用 Mahalanobis 距离做方差条件匹配）可用于遥感小目标/模糊边界的不确定性建模，提升域迁移鲁棒性。但需注意其任务为分割，需自行改造为检测框架。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment of visual and textual representations is fundamental to multimodal medical image understanding, yet remains hindered by uncertainty in both modalities under real-world clinical conditions. Existing vision-language segmentation methods rely on deterministic cross-modal matching, which overlooks aleatoric uncertainty from ambiguous boundaries and epistemic uncertainty from limited training data, leading to fragile performance under domain shift. To address this issue, we propose DistMedVL, a probabilistic vision-language framework that introduces a lightweight Probabilistic Cross-Modal Adapter (PCM-Adapter) upon frozen encoders to explicitly model representational uncertainty. Specifically, the PCM-Adapter comprises two sequential modules for progressive probabilistic alignment. We first devise a Mahalanobis Alignment Module (MAM) that models textual tokens as Gaussian distributions and computes patch-text compatibility via Mahalanobis distance, yielding variance-conditioned matching that downweights unreliable feature dimensions. Moreover, we devise a Distribution Flow Module (DFM) that estimates modality-wise confidence parameters and performs vision-guided refinement of textual distributions, accommodating distributional variation across imaging modalities. Extensive experiments across eight medical segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that DistMedVL outperforms state-of-the-art methods with only 6.3M trainable parameters, exhibiting superior data efficiency, perturbation robustness and cross-dataset generalization.

</details>

Abstract: Scientific images are essential for communicating experimental observations, quantitative evidence and conceptual knowledge. Unlike natural images, their quality depends on both visual clarity and scientific informativeness, making assessment challenging. In this work, we present SciQNet, a two-stage multimodal adaptation framework for scientific image quality assessment. The first stage performs domain-adaptive pretraining on scientific document images and the second stage conducts task-specific fine-tuning with joint scoring and understanding supervision. For scoring-oriented supervision, we combine instruction tuning with a Huber loss derived from rating-word logits, while understanding-oriented supervision is formulated as multiple-choice visual question answering. Experiments show that using a 40% stratified subset of the domain-adaptive data gives the best performance among the evaluated pretraining fractions, suggesting that pretraining-data relevance may be as important as pretraining-data scale. The final model achieves an SIQA-S score of 92.21, an SIQA-U score of 47.38 and a combined score of 69.80. This work presents our solution to the ICME 2026 Scientific Image Quality Assessment Challenge, which ranked 2nd in the scoring track.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate object detection in aerial and satellite imagery is dependent upon the bounding box representation. This is especially true for spatially oriented objects such as ships or aircrafts. Oriented Bounding Boxes (OBB) have a tighter fit and more robust non-max suppression compared to Horizontal Bounding Boxes (HBB), any current post-processing conversion from OBB to HBB either introduces excess empty and background space or removes data from the detection. This paper introduces a novel approach for a shape-aware OBB-to-HBB conversion for ship detection in remote sensing imagery. It leverages hull shape, hull fullness, and the bounding box orientation to produce a tighter axis-aligned HBB representation. The proposed method is benchmarked against three baselines methods for OBBto-HBB conversion, Outer HBB which uses minimum and maximum, Area Equivalent HBB and GBB Marginalized HBB.

</details>


### [42] [TAU-Bench: From Anomaly Instance Tracking to Fine-Grained Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05699)
*Kepeng Yang,Dongxuan Liu,Rongxin Gao,Zixin Su,Rui Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Chenxin Li,Panwang Pan,Yuzhi Huang,Yue Huang,Jingyan Jiang*

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。该工作聚焦于通用视频异常理解评估，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于视频异常理解领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向在任务场景、传感器类型、技术方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Humans understand anomalous events through a coherent perceptual process in which they identify the focal instance, follow its behavior as the event unfolds, and interpret why it violates the expectations of the surrounding scene. Video anomaly understanding (VAU) seeks to endow models with a similar capability, moving beyond deciding whether a video is anomalous toward explaining how the event develops and why it matters. Although recent vision--language models (VLMs) can generate detailed and plausible anomaly descriptions, their semantic fluency does not ensure that these interpretations remain grounded in the correct anomaly instance over time. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate tracking and semantic understanding through separate protocols, leaving such instance--semantic inconsistency largely unmeasured. We therefore introduce TAU-Bench, a track-centric benchmark for jointly evaluating anomaly instance tracking and fine-grained anomaly understanding. TAU-Bench contains 1,118 videos, 1,454 tracks, and 202,438 pixel-level masks spanning 49 event and 45 scene categories, together with track-centric annotations that connect instance-level identification, event-level understanding, and scene-level reasoning. To build TAU-Bench at scale, we developed an automated data engine integrating anomaly suitability filtering, anomaly instance track construction, hierarchical caption annotation, and human quality control. Evaluations across representative VLM families show that models producing plausible anomaly interpretations may still fail to localize and track the correct instance reliably, revealing a persistent gap between semantic reasoning and visual grounding. These findings therefore highlight instance-grounded evaluation as an important step toward more faithful and reliable VAU systems.

</details>


### [43] [Robust-WAM: Bridging Generative Pretraining and Semantic Foresight in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05903)
*Haodong Yan,Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,MingMing Yu,Wenxuan Song,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Yuqiao Du,Jiadi You,Yingjie Cai,Xu Yan,Guanyi Zhao,Bingbing Liu,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Robust-WAM，一种后训练方法，通过在动作流中引入轻量级语义预见对齐目标，使基于视频生成的世界-动作模型在保持VAE空间大规模预训练的同时，提升对光照等视觉分布外变化的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人控制领域的World-Action Models，解决的是视频生成模型在动作预测中对视觉分布外变化的鲁棒性问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人动作预测/控制；应用场景：机器人操作、仿真与真实机器人部署；传感器类型：视觉相机（RGB视频）；目标类别：机器人动作/操作对象。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 基于视频生成模型（VGM）的世界-动作模型（WAM），包含VAE潜在空间生成路径和动作流。Robust-WAM在动作流上添加了轻量级语义预见对齐模块，使用可学习查询令牌（learnable query tokens）和位置编码来建立时序对应关系。

Lightweight method: 论文提到"lightweight semantic foresight alignment objective"，即轻量级的语义预见对齐目标，但这是一种后训练微调方法，并非模型压缩/加速技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及轻量化核心方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在分布外泛化仿真基准（out-of-distribution generalization simulation benchmarks）和真实机器人平台（real-robot setup）上进行。评价指标：成功率（success rates）。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在多个WAM基线上，Robust-WAM一致提升了成功率，且不牺牲分布内性能（in-distribution performance）。摘要未给出具体数值。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对机器人控制领域的动作预测任务，与遥感目标检测无关；2. 轻量级仅指新增模块参数量小，并非模型压缩/加速；3. 未讨论在轨部署、实时推理等约束。

Ideas for my research: 1. 语义空间对齐的思想：在遥感目标检测中，可借鉴将特征从像素重建空间对齐到语义不变空间的方法，提升模型对光照、天气等视觉变化的鲁棒性；2. 轻量级后训练对齐模块的设计思路，可在不改变原模型结构的前提下提升泛化能力，这对星上模型更新有参考价值；3. 但整体相关性极低，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Mainstream World-Action Models (WAMs) adapt pretrained video generation models (VGMs) for robot control, transferring their learned dynamics prior for action prediction. These VGMs are typically trained in a variational autoencoder (VAE) latent space. However, the VAE latent space is optimized for pixel reconstruction, which rewards fine appearance detail and leaves the action prediction fragile under visual shifts. Recent works build WAMs in semantic latent space, which are more robust to appearance shifts. However, these models cannot leverage the large-scale VGM pretraining that exists only in VAE space. To overcome this dilemma, we propose Robust-WAM, a general post-training method for video-generation-based WAMs that preserves the VAE-based generative path and adds a lightweight semantic foresight alignment objective on the action stream. This retains the large-scale VGM pretraining while grounding actions in appearance-invariant dynamics that stay reliable under illumination shifts and other visual out-of-distribution conditions. Specifically, we employ learnable query tokens to bring future-scene semantics into the action stream by aligning their output hidden states with the semantic foresight of future ground-truth frames. To establish the temporal correspondence between each query and the future step it describes, we give it the positional encoding of the matching action tokens. Experiments on out-of-distribution generalization simulation benchmarks and a real-robot setup show that our Robust-WAM consistently improves the success rates of multiple WAM baselines without sacrificing in-distribution performance.

</details>

*Xichen Zhang,Guankai Li,Yinghao Zhu,Shijian Wang,Sitong Wu,Shaozuo Yu,Meng Chu,Yuan Lu,Jiaya Jia*

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态流式视频理解（streaming video understanding）；应用场景：通用环境中的自主多模态智能体；传感器类型：音频-视觉流（audio-visual streams）；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。摘要未说明遥感相关任务。

Model architecture: StreamMind：双架构（two-tier architecture），前端工作者（frontend workers）负责延迟关键交互和主动监控，后端工作者（backend workers）异步构建持久多模态记忆并进行历史回忆和外部搜索。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是长视频流理解中的记忆与延迟问题，而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: StreamArena基准：243个全长视频（平均88.8分钟），3,646个开放问答对；评估能力：实时感知、历史回顾、主动交互、多模态工具利用。摘要未说明遥感数据集。

Experiments: StreamMind在四个能力维度上优于现有流式基线，并通过重用持久状态降低查询到回答的延迟。摘要未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性信息。摘要未说明模型在资源受限设备上的表现。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴点有限：StreamMind的前端/后端分离架构思想（将延迟敏感任务与持久化记忆分离）在星上场景中可能有启发意义——例如将实时目标检测（前端）与历史信息融合（后端）分离设计。但论文本身不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化，借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous multimodal agents in continuous, real-world environments requires them to ingest unbounded audio-visual streams and maintain hour-scale memory. However, current evaluations predominantly rely on brief clips and multiple-choice formats. This design allows minimal baselines that process only the last four frames to match or surpass complex streaming models, while answer options also expose language shortcuts. We introduce StreamArena, a benchmark for hour-scale, interactive streaming video understanding. StreamArena contains 243 full-length videos averaging 88.8 minutes and 3,646 rigorously annotated, open-ended question-answer pairs that evaluate real-time perception, historical retrospection, proactive interaction, and multimodal tool utilization. Evaluation across diverse systems exposes a tension between continuous interaction and long-horizon multimodal comprehension. Methods that retain only recent frames cannot recover distant events, methods that convert past observations into text lose visual evidence, and methods that repeatedly compress visual memory struggle to preserve fine-grained details over time. We address this tension with StreamMind, a two-tier architecture that assigns latency-critical interaction and proactive monitoring to independently scheduled frontend workers, while backend workers asynchronously construct persistent multimodal memory and perform historical recall and external search. StreamMind outperforms existing streaming baselines across all four capabilities and reduces query-to-answer latency by reusing persistent state.

</details>

*Puyuan Zhang,Jianming Huang,Wenkai Ye,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G²ARD-GS，一种几何引导的蒸馏方法，将稠密彩色LiDAR点云转化为紧凑的3D高斯表示，实现5-30倍压缩并保持高质量新视角合成。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于城市级稠密LiDAR点云的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）压缩与渲染，属于计算机图形学/新视角合成领域，与遥感卫星、在轨目标检测、轻量化模型部署基本无关。虽然涉及模型压缩（蒸馏、primitive reduction），但应用场景（城市LiDAR地图渲染）和任务（novel-view synthesis）与遥感在轨目标检测完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：3D高斯泼溅的模型压缩与新视角合成；场景：城市级稠密LiDAR地图；传感器：LiDAR；目标类别：无（场景级重建）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)架构，引入几何引导蒸馏（geometry-guided distillation），将稠密高斯先验转化为表面感知的代表性基元，并在固定拓扑下恢复外观。

Lightweight method: 涉及primitive reduction（基元缩减）和知识蒸馏（distillation），将稠密高斯先验压缩为紧凑表示。但方法针对的是3DGS渲染场景，非神经网络模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等星上部署相关指标。仅提及压缩倍数（5×-30×）和PSNR指标，未涉及实时推理或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MatrixCity（城市级场景）、Cambridge KingsCollege；评价指标：PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS（图像质量指标），与目标检测的mAP等指标无关。

Experiments: 在MatrixCity上，5×-30×压缩下PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS均优于PUP方法，PSNR提升3.2-6.8 dB；在Cambridge KingsCollege上30×压缩下保持图像到模型配准精度。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关：1）任务为3D场景渲染而非目标检测；2）传感器为LiDAR而非遥感卫星光学/SAR/高光谱；3）无轻量化模型部署指标；4）无目标检测相关实验。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1）蒸馏方法用于模型压缩的思路（将大模型知识迁移到紧凑表示）；2）几何引导的基元选择策略可能启发遥感检测中的特征筛选。但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Dense colored LiDAR maps provide accurate city-scale geometry, but lifting them into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) retains millions of primitives, making the resulting models costly to store, transmit, render, and adapt. Aggressive primitive reduction alleviates this burden, but can remove the local surface support needed for stable novel-view synthesis and downstream geometric use. We introduce G$^2$ARD-GS, a geometry-guided distillation method that converts a dense Gaussian prior instantiated either as a training-free point-cloud lift or a trained GS model into a compact, reusable representation. G$^2$ARD-GS progressively consolidates the prior into surface-aware representatives, then recovers appearance on the resulting fixed topology under construction-time anchor constraints, with no primitives added or removed during recovery. Under limited supervision, geometry-aware view selection allocates the available view budget. On MatrixCity, G$^2$ARD-GS achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS across matched $5\times$--$30\times$ compression budgets, outperforming PUP by $3.2$--$6.8$,dB in PSNR. When reused as frozen geometry, the compact model improves off-trajectory appearance adaptation by $3.7$--$4.9$,dB over PUP 3D-GS and preserves image-to-model registration accuracy on Cambridge KingsCollege at $30\times$ compression. Project page: https://patrick1159.github.io/gardGS-page/.

</details>

*Jiarui Yang,Jiale Zhange,Jiawei Li,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Jinpeng Wang,Peidong Liu,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LAWM-3D，通过多视图不变统一动作分词、几何对齐约束和RGB-D联合重建目标，使潜在动作模型学习3D感知的潜在动作，提升世界模型在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力上的表现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于世界模型（world model）中的潜在动作学习（latent action learning），面向机器人/具身智能（embodied intelligence）场景，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文聚焦世界模型与潜在动作学习，面向机器人/具身智能，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：潜在动作学习（latent action learning）与世界模型（world model）前向推演；应用场景：机器人/具身智能（embodied intelligence）；传感器类型：多视图RGB-D视频；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: LAWM-3D，包含三个关键设计：(1) 多视图不变统一动作分词方案（multi-view invariant unified action tokenization）；(2) 几何对齐约束（geometric alignment constraint），将中间编码器特征锚定到预训练3D基础模型；(3) 非注入式RGB-D联合重建目标（non-injective RGB-D joint reconstruction objective）。采用大规模人类视频预训练+机器人微调的两阶段范式。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标涉及生成质量（generation quality）、物理一致性（physical consistency）、泛化能力（generalization ability），但未给出具体指标名称。

Experiments: 摘要声称在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力上达到SOTA结果，但未给出具体数值、对比方法名称或消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/在轨场景；(2) 不涉及目标检测任务；(3) 不涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；(4) 实验细节缺失，无法评估方法有效性；(5) 依赖多视图RGB-D输入，在遥感卫星场景下难以获取。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的思路是：其"几何对齐约束"将编码器特征锚定到预训练3D基础模型的思想，或许可类比为遥感目标检测中利用预训练遥感基础模型（如SatMAE、SpectralGPT等）进行特征对齐，但与本课题的轻量化核心目标关联微弱。

Abstract: World models enable agents to perform forward rollout and planning without real-world interaction. However, their application in open-world embodied intelligence remains limited by the high cost of action annotations and the heterogeneity of action spaces across platforms. Recently, latent action models (LAMs) have alleviated this bottleneck by learning action representations directly from unlabeled human videos in a self-supervised manner. Nevertheless, most existing LAMs rely on single-view inputs and operate primarily in 2D pixel space, raising a fundamental question: can simply incorporating multi-view videos into LAM training endow the learned latent actions with 3D-aware perception? Our study shows that the answer is negative. The primary reasons lie in future-frame appearance leakage as well as inter-camera appearance discrepancies and viewpoint variations. To address these issues, we propose LAWM-3D, which introduces three tightly coupled key designs: (1) a multi-view invariant unified action tokenization scheme for learning 3D-aware latent actions; (2) a geometric alignment constraint that anchors intermediate encoder features to a pretrained 3D foundation model, thereby explicitly providing cross-view geometric correspondences; and (3) a non-injective RGB-D joint reconstruction objective that prevents shortcut learning from future-frame appearance information, forcing the LAM to focus supervision on motion cues with geometric significance. Importantly, these components are not simply stacked but are tightly coupled through a unified motivation. Built upon a two-stage paradigm of large-scale human video pretraining followed by robot fine-tuning, extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed 3D-aware latent actions significantly improve world model performance, achieving SOTA results in generation quality, physical consistency, and generalization ability.

</details>


### [47] [One Ranking, Any Budget: Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context Frame Selection for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05707)
*Wang Chen,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Jinfa Huang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MEC (Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context) 帧选择方法，将长视频帧选择建模为 Matryoshka ranking 问题，构建单一可复用的优先级序列，使任意预算下截断即可复用前缀，无需重跑选择器，在四个基准和六个帧预算上提升平均准确率 3.77 个百分点并降低端到端选择延迟 47.37-51.19%。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2 分）。论文面向 Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) 的长视频帧选择任务，属于泛视觉/多模态视频理解方向，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱等遥感场景无关，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）。其核心是视频帧冗余下的高效选择与延迟优化，与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题仅存在间接的"延迟优化/稀疏采样"方法论借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2 分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向弱相关，仅"稀疏采样/延迟优化/免训练"方法论有间接借鉴价值，不建议作为核心参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：长视频帧选择（frame selection）用于 Large Multimodal Models (LMMs)。应用场景为通用视频理解，非遥感场景。传感器类型：摘要未说明（视频帧）。目标类别：摘要未说明（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: MEC (Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context) Frame Selection，一个 training-free（免训练）框架。核心组件：可复用的稀疏视频索引（reusable sparse video index）、稀疏探测与局部缩放（sparse probing and local zooming）用于发现候选帧、贪心构建位置自适应排序（position-adaptive ranking）。早期位置强调证据（evidence），后期位置渐进偏向时间覆盖（temporal coverage）并保持视觉多样性（visual diversity）。

Lightweight method: 非传统模型压缩方法。本文的"轻量化"体现在：免训练（training-free）设计、构建可复用稀疏视频索引避免重复密集采样、稀疏探测（sparse probing）降低帧-查询相关性评估开销，从而降低端到端选择延迟。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 或轻量网络结构设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告端到端选择延迟降低 47.37-51.19%，以及免训练特性带来的计算开销优势。未涉及星上/边缘部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 四个基准（four benchmarks）和六个帧预算（six frame budgets）。评价指标为平均准确率（average accuracy），相对均匀采样（uniform sampling）提升 3.77 个百分点，并与强 SOTA 选择器对比。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在四个基准、六个帧预算上，MEC 相比均匀采样平均准确率提升 3.77 个百分点，匹配强 SOTA 选择器，端到端选择延迟降低 47.37-51.19%。消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向基本无关，属于通用视频理解。摘要未说明具体数据集、模型规模、参数量、FLOPs、能耗等部署指标。方法针对 LMM 长视频帧选择，未涉及目标检测、遥感图像或星上硬件约束。训练-free 的稀疏索引构建在遥感连续观测/视频流场景下的适用性未验证。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，MEC 的"单一优先级序列 + 任意预算截断复用"思想对在轨遥感目标检测有间接借鉴：1) 遥感视频/连续观测流中，可借鉴"稀疏探测 + 局部缩放"策略，在星上算力受限时先粗筛候选区域再精细检测，降低计算开销；2) "位置自适应排序"思想可类比于在轨多目标检测中按重要性/置信度渐进处理候选框，实现延迟-精度权衡；3) 免训练（training-free）思路契合星上无法在线重训的约束。但需注意其面向 LMM 帧选择，与目标检测模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）无直接关系。

Abstract: Frame selection is essential for applying Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to long videos due to severe frame redundancy and limited context windows. Since the appropriate frame budget varies with the downstream LMM, reasoning demands, and latency constraints, a practical selector should serve multiple budgets. However, existing methods typically optimize an isolated frame subset for each predefined budget: when the budget changes, previously selected evidence may be replaced rather than progressively augmented. Ranking frames by a fixed score would allow prefix reuse across budgets, but it ignores the distinct roles of different ranking positions. In this paper, we formulate long-video frame selection as a Matryoshka ranking problem: constructing a single priority sequence whose small prefixes concentrate query-conditioned evidence, while progressively larger prefixes preserve this evidence and add broader temporal context. Efficiently constructing such a ranking is itself challenging, as densely sampling long videos and evaluating frame-query relevance incurs substantial overhead. We therefore introduce Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context (MEC) Frame Selection, a training-free framework that builds a reusable sparse video index, discovers candidates through sparse probing and local zooming, and greedily constructs a position-adaptive ranking: early positions emphasize evidence; later positions progressively favor temporal coverage while preserving visual diversity. A single ranking can thus be truncated to any target budget without rerunning the selector. Across four benchmarks and six frame budgets, MEC improves average accuracy over uniform sampling by 3.77 percentage points, matches strong state-of-the-art selectors, and reduces end-to-end selection latency by 47.37-51.19%.

</details>

*Alan Grech,Daniel Pisani,Andre Grima,Carl James Debono,Saviour Formosa,Dylan Seychell*

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无人机摄影测量的迭代混合离散-连续视点规划方法，通过光度测量启发式评分和CMA-ES优化来提升三维重建质量。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry requires camera networks that provide sufficient surface coverage, image overlap, parallax, and resolution, yet conventional flight patterns are often poorly adapted to scene geometry resulting in local reconstruction errors. This paper proposes an iterative hybrid discrete-continuous viewpoint planning method for targeted UAV photogrammetry from a proxy reconstruction. The method scores sampled surface points using photogrammetric heuristics based on frontality, imaging distance, parallax, and multi-view observation count, while also evaluating the full viewpoint set in terms of visibility, pairwise overlap, and graph connectivity. Candidate viewpoints are generated around weakly observed regions, refined using clustered Covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy (CMA-ES) optimisation, and removed when redundant. The final flight path combines close-range detail viewpoints with wider model-coverage viewpoints, balancing local reconstruction quality with global image-network robustness. Evaluation on three synthetic scenes shows that the proposed method improves both reconstruction accuracy and completeness compared with prior UAV path-planning methods.

</details>

*Xi Zeng,Haojie Ren,Ziying Song*

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: We propose PhyLatent, a dynamics-relevant training objective for JointEmbedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models. Our key observation is that preventing global latent collapse does not ensure that a representation preserves physical states and action consequences. We identify three failure modes in JEPA world models: physical invariance collapse, physical identifiability collapse, and counterfactual dynamics collapse. PhyLatent addresses them through three training pathways: physical invariance, physical identifiability, and counterfactual dynamics, implemented with physical state grounding, future representation alignment, static visual invariance, counterfactual branch separation, and latent denoising. On OGBench-Cube, PhyLatent reduces the three failure rates from 15.60%, 6.71%, and 8.41% to 7.53%, 0.95%, and 4.62%, respectively, and improves model predictive control (MPC) success from 70.0% to 78.1%. With the same architecture and planner, it further improves success from 81.0% to 98.0% on TwoRooms and remains competitive on Reacher and PushT. These results show that global non-collapse alone is insufficient for learning a reliable JEPA worldmodel state space.

</details>


### [50] [Engram-E2VID: Reference-Based Event-to-Video Reconstruction via Generative Activation of Appearance Engrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05728)
*Feiyu Ji,Xiang Li,Hao Ma,Tianxiang Huang,Qingxin Lu,Mengqi Ji,Lei Han,Xiaokang Yang,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Engram-E2VID，一种基于结构引导的扩散框架，通过外观记忆印记与事件流运动结构支架的跨层交互，实现参考帧到目标帧的事件驱动视频重建。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于事件相机(event camera)的参考帧到目标帧视频重建(event-to-video reconstruction)，属于底层视觉/视频重建任务，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测(object detection)或轻量化(lightweight)方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：事件驱动视频重建(event-to-video reconstruction)；应用场景：事件相机(e.g., DVS)的帧重建；传感器类型：事件相机(event camera)；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: Engram-E2VID框架：将参考帧编码为token空间的外观记忆印记(appearance engrams)；将事件流与参考上下文转换为目标时刻运动结构支架(motion-structure scaffold)；基于一步扩散骨干(one-step diffusion backbone)，结构token跨层渐进式激活相关外观印记。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝(pruning)、量化(quantization)、知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。扩散模型通常计算开销较大，不适用于星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 三个基准数据集(three benchmarks)；评价指标：PSNR（最高提升3.29 dB）、LPIPS（最高降低0.08）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在三个基准上，Engram-E2VID相比最强同输入基线，PSNR提升最高3.29 dB，LPIPS降低最高0.08，且随重建间隔增大性能下降更慢。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 基于扩散模型(diffusion backbone)，计算开销大，不适合在轨部署；3. 仅针对事件相机数据，不适用于光学/SAR/高光谱遥感图像；4. 未提供任何模型效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接借鉴意义。唯一的间接启发可能是：其"结构引导的token空间关联"思想如果迁移到遥感目标检测中，或许可用于处理时序遥感图像中的运动目标关联，但需要大幅改造且不涉及轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Reference-based event-to-video reconstruction aims to recover target RGB frames from a reference frame and the event stream captured over the reference-to-target interval. Although events provide fine-grained temporal cues, they encode sparse and asynchronous log-intensity changes rather than absolute appearance, making faithful reconstruction intrinsically challenging. The central challenge lies in associating event-derived target-time structures with relevant appearance information from the reference frame, especially under complex motion and long temporal intervals. In this work, we propose Engram-E2VID, a structure-guided framework that reconstructs target frames through the generative activation of appearance engrams. Specifically, the reference frame is encoded into token-space appearance engrams, while the event stream and reference context are transformed into a target-time motion-structure scaffold that captures motion boundaries and event-induced structural changes. Within a one-step diffusion backbone, scaffold-derived structural tokens progressively interact with and activate relevant appearance engrams across layers. This token-space association allows target structures to access reference appearance without relying on direct pixel-wise correspondence, while the diffusion prior complements uncertain or newly revealed regions. Across three benchmarks, Engram-E2VID improves PSNR by up to 3.29 dB and reduces LPIPS by up to 0.08 over the strongest same-input baseline, while degrading more slowly as the reconstruction interval increases.

</details>


### [51] [LiteKD-Net: Lightweight Knowledge-Distilled Network for Mobile Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05739)
*Zhou Zhiyi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiteKD-Net，一种基于知识蒸馏和轻量RRDB模块的移动端图像去噪网络，通过物理引导的噪声模拟和特征级蒸馏实现高质量与低计算成本的平衡。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文聚焦移动端图像去噪，属于底层视觉任务，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或遥感场景理解。轻量化方法（知识蒸馏、深度可分离卷积）对模型压缩有借鉴意义，但场景和任务完全不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅知识蒸馏和深度可分离卷积的设计思路有微弱借鉴意义，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像去噪（image denoising）；场景：移动端摄影（mobile image）；传感器类型：普通相机（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Teacher：Real-ESRGAN改编为恒分辨率去噪；Student：基于Lite-RRDB（轻量残差密集块）的轻量网络，使用depthwise separable convolutions（深度可分离卷积）。

Lightweight method: 知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation, KD）：特征级蒸馏将Teacher的恢复能力迁移至Student，不增加推理开销；轻量模块设计：Lite-RRDB基于深度可分离卷积。未涉及剪枝、量化、NAS。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提到runtime reduction和inference rate提升，但无量化数据。未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实世界数据集（real-world datasets），通过物理引导噪声模拟生成LQ-GT配对数据；评价指标：与SwinIR对比，在所有指标上最优，但摘要未说明具体指标名称（如PSNR/SSIM等）。

Experiments: 在真实数据集上，LiteKD-Net相比SwinIR在运行时间和推理速度上有显著提升，且恢复质量指标最优。摘要未提供消融实验或与其他轻量方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 任务为图像去噪，非目标检测，与遥感在轨检测方向不直接相关；2. 摘要未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化指标，难以评估轻量化程度；3. 未在遥感图像（如SAR、多光谱）上验证；4. 知识蒸馏仅涉及特征级蒸馏，未探讨logit蒸馏或结构化剪枝等更通用的压缩方法。

Ideas for my research: 1. 知识蒸馏（KD）的思路可迁移：在遥感目标检测中，可用大模型（如YOLOv8-L）作为Teacher蒸馏轻量Student（如YOLOv8-n），实现精度-速度权衡；2. 深度可分离卷积（depthwise separable conv）可引入遥感轻量检测骨干设计；3. 物理引导的噪声模拟思路对遥感图像去噪/增强有参考价值，但需适配遥感传感器特性（如SAR speckle噪声、多光谱通道相关性）。

Abstract: Mobile image denoising requires both good restoration quality and low computational cost. In addition, it's annoying to collect large-scale LQ-GT clean pairs. As a result, we propose LiteKD-Net, a lightweight knowledge-distilled network for mobile image denoising. First, a physics-guided noise simulation pipeline generates paired training data by adding pixel crosstalk compared with pipelines applied to cameras. Next, we adapt the Real-ESRGAN to identity-resolution denoising and construct a lightweight Student using Lite-RRDB blocks based on depthwise separable convolutions. Third, feature-level knowledge distillation is applied to transfer the Teacher's restoration capability to the Student without introducing additional inference cost. Experiments on real-world datasets show that our model reaches great reduction in runtime and increase in the inference rate with good restoration quality. Our model also reaches the best in all metrics compared with SwinIR. These results indicate that LiteKD-Net provides a great trade-off between restoration quality and computational efficiency.

</details>

*Yufei Li,Yicheng Ruan,Long Tian,Dongsheng Wang,Liang Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 基于预训练基础模型（foundation-model）的特征提取，结合概念引导的自适应特征重建模块（concept-guided adaptive feature reconstruction），动态注意力机制（dynamic attention mechanism），稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoders），以及LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）注意力模块。

Lightweight method: 仅提及LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）用于注意力模块，引入少量可更新参数（minimal updating parameters），实现推理时的快速适应（fast adaptation）。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及LoRA使模型轻量化（lightweight），但未给出具体数值，无法判断星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MVTec-AD、VisA、MPDD（均为工业异常检测基准数据集）。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称（通常为AUROC、AUPR等）。实验设置：少样本（few-shot）设置，多种shot设置（various shot settings）。

Experiments: 在三个基准数据集上，ConceptADapt在检测和定位任务上均一致优于SOTA方法，在不同shot设置下均有显著提升。摘要未提供具体数值。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，场景差异巨大；2. 轻量化仅依赖LoRA，未涉及星上部署所需的参数量/FLOPs/延迟等关键指标；3. 方法针对的是正常样本极少的异常检测，而非遥感目标检测任务；4. 未在遥感数据集上验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. LoRA作为一种参数高效微调方法，可借鉴用于遥感目标检测模型的轻量化微调，但需注意LoRA主要减少可训练参数量而非推理参数量；2. 概念引导的特征重建思路可能对遥感小目标检测中的特征增强有启发，但需大幅改造；3. 整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Few-shot industrial anomaly detection (FS-IAD) focuses on detecting and localizing visual defects in industrial inspection during the cold-start phase, where only a limited number of normal training samples are available per category. Recent advances in this field predominantly leverage visual features from foundation-model and have achieved promising performance. Despite the strong representational power of foundation-model features, the model generalization remains fragile due to the extreme scarcity of normal training data.To address this pivotal issue, we propose ConceptADapt, a concept-guided adaptive feature reconstruction model with dynamic attention. Specifically, our model pre-learns a set of fixed normal concepts from the limited support features and leverages them to mine relationships with query features, thereby recalibrating their statistics for improved anomaly detection at test time. To mitigate the prevalent feature shortcut problem, which is particularly severe under low-data regimes, we further develop a dynamic attention mechanism integrated with sparse autoencoders to learn robust normal concepts during training. Moreover, to enable fast adaptation during inference, our model remains lightweight by incorporating LoRA into the attention module, which introduces only minimal updating parameters.Extensive experiments on three widely adopted FS-IAD benchmarks, including MVTec-AD, VisA, and MPDD, demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches across both detection and localization tasks, achieving significant improvements under various shot settings.

</details>


### [53] [UniVVT: A Unified End-to-End Framework for High-Fidelity Video Virtual Try-on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05745)
*Yushe Cao,Shikun Feng,Fei Shen,Haikuo Peng,Jianqiang Xia,Yiheng Zhu,Dianxi Shi,Chun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniVVT，一个端到端的视频虚拟试穿框架，用多模态大语言模型替代传统多阶段几何预处理流程。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究视频虚拟试穿（VVT），属于计算机视觉/图形学中的时尚与人体相关任务，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及任何遥感场景理解、目标检测或轻量化部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频虚拟试穿（Video Virtual Try-On）；应用场景：电商、时尚、虚拟现实；传感器类型：RGB视频；目标类别：人体、服装。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: UniVVT框架：基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的场景任务感知器（scene-task perceiver）编码源视频、目标服装和任务指令为紧凑的task-aware latent tokens；轻量语义桥（lightweight semantic bridge）对齐tokens与扩散视频生成器的条件空间；三阶段渐进训练策略（语义对齐、联合任务适应、灵活分辨率微调）。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"lightweight semantic bridge"是模型内部的一个轻量模块，用于对齐特征空间，但并非剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法。论文整体不涉及轻量化部署技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。由于模型基于MLLM和扩散模型，计算开销通常较大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提到"multiple benchmarks"和"state-of-the-art performance"。

Experiments: 摘要未提供具体实验结果数值，仅声称在多个基准上达到SOTA性能，并通过消融实验验证了三阶段训练策略的有效性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 基于MLLM和扩散模型，计算开销大，不适合资源受限场景；3. 摘要未提供定量结果和部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能相关的点是：文中"lightweight semantic bridge"的设计思路（用轻量模块对齐不同模态/任务的特征空间）在遥感多模态任务（如SAR+光学融合检测）中可能有启发，但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) synthesizes a video of a person wearing a target garment while preserving identity, motion, and scene dynamics. Dominant approaches cast VVT as mask-conditioned video inpainting and rely on separate modules for human parsing, pose estimation, and garment warping. This multi-stage design complicates deployment and, more critically, allows errors in explicit geometric priors to propagate irreversibly into the generated video. We present UniVVT, a unified end-to-end framework that reframes VVT as semantically conditioned video generation, eliminating mask, pose, and warping modules at inference. At its core, a scene-task perceiver built on a Multimodal Large Language Model jointly encodes the source video, target garment, and task instruction into compact, task-aware latent tokens, implicitly capturing what to transfer and where and how to transfer it. A lightweight semantic bridge then aligns these tokens with the conditioning space of a diffusion-based video generator, enabling coherent garment transfer. To robustly couple the heterogeneous components, we devise a three-stage progressive training strategy comprising semantic alignment, joint task adaptation, and flexible-resolution refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniVVT achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, validating implicit semantic guidance as a simple and effective alternative to fragile geometric preprocessing for end-to-end virtual try-on.

</details>


### [54] [GST-Bench: Can VLMs Develop Global Spatial Awareness from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05747)
*Qifeng Zhang,Kaixiang Huang,Heng Dong,Huang Fang,Junting Chen,Junjie Zhu,Yonghang Chen,Zhiyu Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GST-Bench（全局空间时序基准），由6,790分钟合成视频生成，含人工验证问题；GST-Bench-Local（局部空间对照）；GST-Train（全局空间推理训练集）。评价指标：VQA准确率（zero-shot得分42.68 vs 人类79.08）。

Experiments: 对22个SOTA VLMs进行综合评估，最强zero-shot模型得分42.68，远低于人类79.08。通过GST-Bench-Local对照实验发现：模型在相同任务设定下局部空间理解较强，但无法将长时序观测整合为全局一致的场景表示。

Limitations: 与本研究方向基本无关：非遥感场景、非目标检测任务、无轻量化/部署内容。摘要未说明模型架构、参数量、FLOPs等关键信息。其合成视频数据与真实遥感影像差异巨大，无法直接迁移。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点有限但存在概念性启发：(1) "多视角/长时序观测整合为全局一致场景表示"的思想，可类比遥感卫星多时相、多轨道观测的全局场景理解；(2) 其"局部理解强但全局整合弱"的发现，提示在遥感目标检测中，单帧局部检测与跨帧/跨轨道全局一致性建模之间的gap值得关注；(3) 构建基准时"人工验证问题"的质量控制思路可用于遥感检测基准构建。但整体上对轻量化在轨检测的直接借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is fundamental to embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks focus on local spatial perception from single or few viewpoints, overlooking global spatial awareness over continuous, long-horizon visual streams. To address this limitation, we introduce the Global-Spatial-Temporal Benchmark (GST-Bench), a VQA benchmark for global spatial intelligence in video understanding, comprising human-verified questions derived from 6,790 minutes of synthetically generated video. It requires models to perform accurate spatial inference from novel viewpoints unseen in the input video and to map egocentric observations onto global top-down images. A comprehensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art VLMs exposes a striking gap between models and humans: the strongest zero-shot model attains only 42.68, far below the human score of 79.08. To probe the cause of this gap, we construct GST-Bench-Local and find that models, despite strong local spatial understanding under the same task formulation, still fail to consolidate long-horizon observations into a globally consistent scene representation. We further provide GST-Train, a dataset for global spatial reasoning, as a complementary resource to facilitate future research on this challenge.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Relevance: Bayesian Evidence Acquisition for Agentic Whole-Slide Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05757)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) reasoning requires an agent to sequentially acquire visual evidence before answering a diagnostic question. Existing training-free agentic frameworks formulate this process as iterative patch retrieval based on semantic relevance to the question. However, semantic relevance does not necessarily imply diagnostic informativeness in computational pathology, where competing diagnoses often exhibit similar and overlapping morphological patterns, making many patches semantically relevant yet diagnostically non-discriminative. Consequently, relevance-based retrieval may acquire redundant observations and leave diagnostic uncertainty unresolved. We propose BEACON, a plug-and-play agentic framework that reformulates WSI reasoning as a Bayesian evidence acquisition problem. BEACON maintains a probabilistic belief over competing diagnostic hypotheses and sequentially acquires patches by maximizing expected information gain (EIG) to reduce diagnostic uncertainty. An evidence controller then determines whether to answer, acquire additional evidence, or perform higher-resolution inspection. Built entirely from off-the-shelf foundation models, BEACON requires no additional training or fine-tuning. Extensive zero-shot experiments across five WSI-VQA benchmarks demonstrate that BEACON achieves the strongest overall performance among training-free agentic frameworks while substantially improving evidence acquisition efficiency, establishing Bayesian evidence acquisition as a principled paradigm for uncertainty-aware agentic WSI reasoning. The code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BEACON

</details>


### [56] [Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05769)
*Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Knowledge distillation for image restoration typically aligns intermediate features or relation matrices between teacher and student networks as static targets, ignoring the dynamic structure of the knowledge transfer process. In this paper, we propose Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds (FoRM), which reformulates relation-based knowledge transfer as a continuous flow mapping problem on the relation manifold. Rather than regressing a constant velocity field between student and teacher relation states, FoRM learns a flow map operator $\mathcal{F}_θ(\mathbf{z}, t, s)$ that directly predicts the relation state at any target time $s$ given the current state at time $t$, enabling richer trajectory-level supervision. To ensure global self-consistency of the learned flow map, we introduce a safe semigroup consistency constraint that enforces compositional agreement using ground-truth bridge states, eliminating phantom-state error accumulation. An endpoint anchoring loss further prevents the operator from drifting away from the teacher target. Extensive experiments on five image restoration tasks, including super-resolution, deraining, denoising, deblurring, and low-light enhancement, demonstrate consistent gains over state-of-the-art distillation baselines across multiple backbone architectures, reducing training variance by approximately 50\% compared to naive flow matching distillation while achieving superior restoration quality.

</details>


### [57] [HyTBE: Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert Model for Cross-Domain Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05771)
*Aohua Li,Jin Kuang,Yubing Lu,Pingping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyTBE模型，利用双曲空间建模目标-背景关系，通过关系干预和专家适配提升红外小目标检测的跨域泛化能力。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文研究红外小目标检测（IRSTD），属于遥感/航空图像中的目标检测任务，但：1) 传感器为红外（infrared），非可见光/SAR/多光谱等遥感卫星常用模态；2) 任务为小目标检测，与遥感小目标检测有交集；3) 完全不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、星上部署、实时推理等任何轻量化或部署相关内容；4) 关注的是跨域泛化（domain generalization），而非在轨部署效率。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。论文聚焦红外小目标检测的跨域泛化，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测有部分交集（小目标检测），但完全不涉及轻量化/部署，且传感器模态为红外而非遥感卫星主流模态。可作为小目标检测和域泛化方法的参考，但对"在轨轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：红外小目标检测（Infrared Small Target Detection, IRSTD）；场景：跨域泛化（cross-domain generalization），leave-one-domain-out实验；传感器：红外（infrared）；目标类别：小目标（small target），未具体说明目标类型（如飞机、舰船等）；数据集：NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, IRSTD-1K。

Model architecture: HyTBE (Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert) 模型，包含三个核心模块：1) Target-Background Relation Intervention——对目标或背景进行选择性扰动，扩展训练中可观测的关系模式；2) Hyperbolic Relation Modeling——将多尺度视觉特征映射到Poincaré ball（双曲空间），根据特征token到目标锚点和背景锚点的相对距离刻画目标-背景关系；3) Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter——利用双曲关系表示校准多尺度视觉特征，并通过Mixture-of-Experts聚合不同关系模式的专家特定特征修正。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。模型包含MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）和双曲空间计算，计算开销可能较大，不利于星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, IRSTD-1K（均为红外小目标检测公开数据集）；实验设置：Leave-one-domain-out跨域泛化实验；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标（如IoU, mAP, F1, PD, FAR等）。

Experiments: 在三个红外小目标数据集上进行了leave-one-domain-out跨域泛化实验，HyTBE相比competitive baselines取得了更强的跨域泛化性能。摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1) 仅针对红外模态，未验证在可见光、SAR、多光谱等遥感卫星常用模态上的有效性；2) 未涉及任何轻量化或部署效率优化，MoE和双曲空间计算可能带来较大计算开销；3) 未报告参数量、推理速度等实际部署关键指标；4) 跨域泛化实验仅在三个红外数据集上进行，泛化性验证范围有限。

Ideas for my research: 1) 双曲空间（Hyperbolic space）建模目标-背景关系的思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测中，用于处理遥感图像中目标与复杂背景的区分问题；2) Target-Background Relation Intervention的数据增强策略可迁移到遥感小目标检测中，帮助模型学习更鲁棒的目标-背景关系；3) MoE Adapter的专家聚合机制可用于轻量化模型设计——在保持模型容量同时通过稀疏激活降低计算量；4) 但需注意：本文方法本身不轻量，若用于在轨部署需额外进行剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化处理。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) has achieved substantial progress under domain-consistent evaluation, yet detector performance often degrades markedly when generalizing to unseen infrared domains. Existing methods primarily improve detection by enhancing target responses and suppressing background interference. However, when trained on only a limited set of source domains, their learned decision rules are inevitably established from a restricted range of source-domain target-background relation patterns. We formulate this cross-domain failure as target-background relation shift: unseen domains may exhibit relation patterns that are not observed during training, thereby weakening the discriminative capability learned from the source domains. To address this problem, we propose HyTBE, a Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert model that expands source-domain relation patterns and adaptively adjusts visual representations using explicit relation cues. The Target-Background Relation Intervention selectively perturbs either targets or backgrounds, broadening the observable relation patterns during training while maintaining valid supervision. Subsequently, the Hyperbolic Relation Modeling maps multi-scale visual cues into a Poincaré ball and characterizes the target-background relation of each feature token according to its relative distances to the target and background anchors. The Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter further uses these hyperbolic relation representations to calibrate multi-scale visual features and aggregate expert-specific feature corrections for different relation patterns. Leave-one-domain-out experiments on NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, and IRSTD-1K demonstrate that HyTBE achieves stronger cross-domain generalization than competitive baselines.

</details>

*Zihan Zhou,Qifu Wen,Xi Zeng*

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D场景理解、缺失物体潜在表示预测；应用场景：室内3D场景（ARKitScenes）；传感器类型：深度/点云；目标类别：室内物体。不涉及遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: SR-JEPA，基于JEPA（Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture）的点云原生架构，使用EMA（指数移动平均）目标进行自监督训练，预测路径可查询。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ARKitScenes（5,953个物体）、Sr3D（8,570个支持对）；评价指标：语义身份宏准确率（43.13%）、AP（41.15）。

Experiments: 在ARKitScenes上达到43.13%语义身份宏准确率，比基线高22.18点；随机化预测路径降低9.78点；替换上下文降低21.98点。在Sr3D上达到41.15 AP。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。仅限室内3D场景，未涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。JEPA架构的自监督学习思路在理论上可迁移到遥感领域，但本文聚焦3D点云场景，与遥感图像目标检测和轻量化部署差距较大。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting latent representations of missing observations, yet many masked JEPAs are evaluated primarily through the encoders they produce. We ask what a trained predictive pathway itself infers when an entire entity is absent from a native 3D scene. We introduce SR-JEPA, a point-native JEPA for scene-scale point clouds whose original frozen predictive pathway can be queried at a supplied location. At evaluation, every point of one object is removed before encoding and replaced by the same shape-free 32-point query at its centroid. Training uses only self-contained 3D EMA targets: no reconstruction, semantic labels, language, or lifted 2D features. On 5,953 held-out ARKitScenes objects, the imputed latent reaches 43.13% semantic-identity macro accuracy, 22.18 points above the strongest floor. Randomizing the prediction path removes 9.78 points, while substituting matched donor context removes 21.98 points. On 8,570 Sr3D support pairs, the full latent reaches 41.15 AP; identity decoded from the missing-object latent, combined with anchor identity and geometry, reaches 39.37 AP, leaving an unresolved 1.78-point residual. These results reveal a queryable, compositional 3D predictive state: the model completes context-dependent entity content, which downstream computation combines with metric geometry.

</details>


### [59] [Vorch-Director: Interactive World Story Model via Noise-Aware Error Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05776)
*Lisai Zhang,Yidi Wu,Qi Liu,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Siqian Yang,Jingyuan Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vorch-Director，一种噪声级感知的残差校正策略，用于分钟级音视频自回归生成，解决训练-推理历史不匹配导致的漂移和不同步问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是音视频生成（audio-visual generation），属于多模态生成领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、在轨智能或目标检测任务。
