Task and scene: 任务：人形机器人多传感器融合里程计/状态估计（odometry & state estimation）。场景：人形机器人行走、多步态、真实世界与公开数据集。传感器：关节编码器（joint encoders）、IMU、LiDAR、相机。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无目标类别。

Model architecture: 异步-顺序混合误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波（asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter, ESIKF）。惯性数据用于预测，腿部运动学以高频率异步处理提供本体感知约束，外感知顺序更新：先LiDAR点云配准提供几何先验，再通过光度误差更新视觉分量。含多模态自适应模块（应对传感器失效）和紧凑的接触估计模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。仅提到"compact contact estimation module"（紧凑接触估计模块），但并非针对深度学习模型的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates"，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 公开数据集及真实世界多个人形机器人、多种步态和场景实验。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标（如ATE、RPE等）。

Experiments: 在公开数据集和真实世界多个人形机器人、多种步态和场景下验证，达到有竞争力的精度、效率和输出频率，对传感器退化和失效具有强鲁棒性，优于SOTA融合方法。摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。与本研究方向无关，缺乏遥感、目标检测、轻量化相关任何信息。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考其多传感器融合与误差状态滤波（ESIKF）思想，但与本课题（遥感在轨目标检测轻量化）无直接关联。若强行关联，其"多模态自适应/传感器失效鲁棒性"思想或可类比星上多源遥感数据（光学+SAR）融合的鲁棒性设计，但属于间接启发。

Abstract: This article presents a kinematic-inertial-LiDAR-visual odometry for humanoid robots, called KILVO. Tailored to the platform features, requirements, and real-world complexity, it fully utilizes the sensors commonly equipped on humanoid robots, including joint encoders, IMU, LiDAR, and camera, within an asynchronous-sequential hybrid error-state iterated Kalman filter (ESIKF). Specifically, inertial data are used for prediction, leg kinematics are processed asynchronously at a high rate and provide proprioceptive constraints, while exteroception is updated sequentially, first by registering LiDAR points for geometric priors and then by updating the visual component via photometric errors. Moreover, the framework is elaborately designed with multimodal adaptation for resilience to sensor failures. A compact contact estimation module is also developed, sharing information with state estimation without additional sensors. Extensive experiments on public datasets and in the real world across multiple humanoid robots, gait patterns, and scenarios demonstrate that KILVO achieves highly competitive accuracy, efficiency, and output rates, with strong robustness against sensor degradation and failures, making it more suitable for humanoid robots than state-of-the-art fusion methods. Our code and datasets are released on GitHub.

</details>


### [120] [JoyAI-RA 0.5: Scaling Robot Manipulation Learning via Dual Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05674)
*JoyAI-RA Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JoyAI-RA 0.5，一个面向机器人操作学习的通用视觉-语言-世界-动作（VLWA）框架，通过双动作对齐（隐式动作对齐与显式动作对齐）将人类第一视角视频、仿真和真实机器人等异构数据统一起来，实现操作能力的规模化学习。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文聚焦机器人操作（manipulation）学习，属于具身智能/机器人学习领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱等遥感场景完全无关，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可借鉴的是其"异构数据对齐"思想，但与本课题方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于机器人操作学习领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化部署，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人操作学习（manipulation learning）、视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略。场景：人类第一视角视频、仿真、真实机器人（AgiBot benchmark）。传感器：视觉（egocentric video）。目标类别：机器人操作动作，非遥感目标。摘要未说明遥感相关传感器或目标。

Model architecture: JoyAI-RA 0.5 通用 VLWA（Vision-Language-World-Action）框架，包含：隐式动作对齐（从视觉转换推断潜在动作，用于无动作标签数据）、显式动作对齐（通过规范动作表示和相机帧块相对末端执行器动作统一到物理动作空间）、潜在动作条件世界模型（latent-action-conditioned world model）、内外环强化阶段（inner-outer-loop reinforcement）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法，也未提及实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文面向机器人操作而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实世界 AgiBot benchmark（含 seen tasks 和 unseen variations）。评价指标：task score（任务成功率/得分）。摘要未说明具体数值、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Experiments: 在 AgiBot benchmark 上，JoyAI-RA 在已见任务和未见变体上均表现良好。任务得分随人类第一视角预训练数据量增加而持续提升，且在大规模下未见平台期，表明弱标注的人类经验可转化为可迁移的训练信号。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，属于机器人学习领域。2. 摘要未提供模型规模、计算开销、部署可行性等关键信息。3. 未说明在真实机器人上的泛化边界、数据规模上限、训练成本。4. 未涉及任何遥感场景验证。

Ideas for my research: 与本课题相关性极低，但可抽象借鉴两点：1) "异构数据对齐"思想——在遥感目标检测中，不同传感器（光学、SAR、高光谱）数据分布差异大，可借鉴其"隐式/显式对齐"思路处理多源遥感数据；2) "弱标注数据转化为可迁移训练信号"——遥感领域存在大量弱标注或无标注数据，可借鉴其利用弱监督信号提升模型泛化的思路。但这两点均为间接启发，不涉及轻量化核心。

Abstract: Robot data is scarce, so generalist policies need to learn from heterogeneous sources, including human egocentric video, simulation, and real robots, which differ in supervision and embodiment, with action labels missing or mutually incompatible. Human egocentric data scale best but sit farthest from robot data, and naive pooling causes negative transfer rather than knowledge sharing. We propose JoyAI-RA 0.5, a generalist Vision-Language-World-Action (VLWA) framework that couples physical world-dynamics priors with visual semantics and scales manipulation learning across such data via dual action alignment. Implicit action alignment infers latent actions from visual transitions, enabling action-free human, simulation, and robot data to guide a latent-action-conditioned world model in learning physical dynamics. Explicit alignment grounds reliable human and robot trajectories in a unified physical action space through a canonical action representation and camera-frame chunk-relative end-effector actions. An inner-outer-loop reinforcement stage then pairs efficient task adaptation with foundation-policy improvement. On a real-world AgiBot benchmark, JoyAI-RA performs strongly on both seen tasks and unseen variations. The task score improves consistently as the volume of human egocentric pretraining data increases and shows no sign of plateauing at our largest scale. This suggests that abundant but weakly labeled human experience can be converted into a transferable training signal, making human video not merely a weak auxiliary source but a primary axis along which manipulation capability can be scaled. Project page can be found at https://joyai-ra-05.github.io/.

</details>


### [121] [Nonvisual Classification of Ground-Condition by Artificial Proprioception in an Amoeba-Inspired Autonomous Walking Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05684)
*Hyoto Yamaguchi,Zenji Yatabe,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多模态传感（加速度计+足底压力传感器）与储层计算（RC）的仿生机器人地面条件非视觉分类方法，实现四足机器人步态在线切换。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。论文属于机器人学与仿生感知领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无任何直接或间接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：地面条件非视觉分类（flat/rough）；应用场景：仿生四足机器人自主行走；传感器类型：三轴加速度计、足底压力传感器；目标类别：无目标检测任务，仅分类地面平坦/粗糙。

Model architecture: 采用储层计算（Reservoir Computing, RC）作为时序信号处理框架，集成多模态传感器信号实现人工本体感知（artificial proprioception）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。RC本身属于轻量级递归神经网络范式，但论文未从模型压缩或加速角度讨论。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及在四足机器人上实现了on-site switching（现场切换），但未给出量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和规模；评价指标为分类准确率（high accuracy），但未给出具体数值。

Experiments: 在四足机器人行走动态运动条件下验证了地面条件分类的高准确性；展示了基于地面条件的步态在线切换；讨论了各传感器对分类的贡献（消融分析）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术点；2. 仅二分类（flat/rough），任务过于简单；3. 未提供量化性能指标（准确率、延迟等）；4. 未涉及任何视觉/图像处理，与遥感目标检测范式完全不同。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机器人地面感知领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Nonvisual classification of ground condition based on a multimodal sensing approach was investigated for an amoeba-inspired autonomous walking robot. To classify ground condition without image sensing and processing, we implemented artificial proprioception by integrating a three-axis accelerometer, eight foot pressure sensors, and reservoir computing (RC). Even when large fluctuations in the sensor outputs are caused by dynamic motions of a four-legged robot in walking, our system can classify the ground condition, flat or rough, with high accuracy. We demonstrate on-site switching of walking gait depending on ground condition in the robot. We also discuss the contribution of each sensor to ground condition classification.

</details>


### [122] [Hijacking Robots with a Piece of Paper: A Systematic Study of Physical Prompt Injection in VLM-Controlled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05715)
*S. M . Bhagya P. Samarakoon,M. A. Viraj J. Muthugala,W. K. R. Sachinthana,Mohan Rajesh Elara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究VLM（视觉语言模型）作为机器人规划器时，视觉场景中的对抗性文本（物理提示注入）对指令执行的安全威胁，提出攻击分类、基准测试和防御方法。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人系统中VLM的安全攻击与防御，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：VLM安全攻击与防御（物理提示注入攻击）；应用场景：机器人操控（sorting任务）；传感器类型：视觉（RGB相机）；目标类别：无目标检测相关。

Model architecture: VLM模型：GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-VL-32B。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建基准（20个攻击提示，3种物理场景布局，3种命令表述形式）；评价指标：攻击成功率（ASR），模型间攻击迁移性，推理轨迹分析（acknowledgment rate）。

Experiments: 5,670次试验，GPT-4o攻击成功率27.0%，Gemini 2.5 Flash 29.4%，Qwen3-VL-32B 5.0%。防御方法：基于提示的防御（75-100%有效，依赖模型）、两阶段验证（85-100%）、预处理文本掩码（100%）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。防御方法可能损害需要读取场景标签的任务能力。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发：VLM在安全关键场景（如卫星在轨）中部署时需考虑对抗鲁棒性，但本文不涉及遥感或轻量化。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners in robotic systems, where they translate natural-language commands into executable actions grounded in visual scene understanding. This tight coupling between perception and instruction-following introduces a new attack surface: adversarial text placed within the robot's visual field can act as an indirect prompt injection into the VLM's reasoning stack. We present a systematic study of physical prompt injection attacks against VLM-controlled sorting, introducing a four-category taxonomy, indirect signage, task redefinition, authority impersonation, and conflict injection, instantiated as a benchmark of 20 attack prompts evaluated across three physical scene layouts and three command formulations that vary in destination specificity and rule explicitness. Across 5,670 trials on three frontier VLMs (GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen3-VL-32B), attacks succeed at 27.0%, 29.4%, and 5.0% respectively, with authority-impersonating and negation attacks transferring across all three models. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals that successful compromise is nearly always conscious (99.9% acknowledgment rate), and that models defend through structurally different mechanisms, explicit rejection for Gemini, perceptual inattention for GPT-4o. We evaluate three simple mitigations: prompt-based defense (75-100% effective, model-dependent), two-stage verification (85-100%), and pre-processing text masking (100%). Our findings show that VLM-controlled manipulation is meaningfully vulnerable to human-readable physical signage, and that simple defenses substantially reduce risk, though defense choice involves trade-offs. The defenses preserve general task capabilities in our benchmark, but they may impair tasks that require reading in-scene labels.

</details>


### [123] [ATP: Anatomical Torque with Passivity-based Control Framework for Safe Upper-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05723)
*Yu Chen,Gong Chen,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于解剖学力矩与无源性控制（ATP）的上肢外骨骼辅助方法，通过强化学习肌肉控制器生成参考力矩，结合在线力矩修正与交互力矩控制器实现安全、泛化的上肢运动辅助。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是上肢外骨骼机器人控制，属于可穿戴机器人/人机交互领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：上肢外骨骼辅助控制；应用场景：可穿戴机器人/康复/助力；传感器类型：EMG（肌电信号）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。涉及强化学习肌肉控制器（unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller），以及基于无源性控制的交互力矩控制器。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或轻量化网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验方式：仿真（simulations）和真实世界实验（real-world experiments）；评价指标：力矩跟踪精度（torque tracking）、无源性（passivity）、EMG肌肉活动降低幅度（up to 48% reduction）；数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 仿真和真实实验验证了长时运动序列的准确跟踪和对实时人体运动的泛化能力。5名受试者的EMG实验表明，与重力补偿和开环辅助相比，静态和动态任务中目标肌肉活动降低，动态多关节任务中相对无外骨骼状态降低达48%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明计算资源需求、模型规模等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文属于机器人控制领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Providing assistance across diverse movements is a central objective of exoskeletons, and anatomical knowledge can enable responsive support that generalizes across tasks. However, anatomical assistance has mainly been studied for lower-limb exoskeletons, where periodic, weight-bearing motions impose lower demands on torque precision. Extending such assistance to complex, nonperiodic upper-limb movements remains challenging. This paper proposes Anatomical Torque with Passivity-Based Control (ATP) for safe upper-limb exoskeleton assistance. First, a scalable musculoskeletal simulation framework trains a unified reinforcement-learning muscle controller that generalizes across upper-limb movements and generates anatomical reference torques without complex biomechanical computations. Second, an online torque-refinement scheme adapts the reference to diverse movements, suppresses tendon-induced spikes, and incorporates a learned anomaly score for safe and comfortable assistance. Third, an interaction torque controller delivers assistance through a cable-driven compliant exoskeleton without constraining motion to predefined trajectories, while an energy tank preserves passivity with theoretical guarantees on torque tracking and system passivity. Simulations and real-world experiments show accurate tracking of long-duration motion sequences and generalization to real-time human movements. The controller achieves accurate torque tracking while preserving passivity and resumes tracking after energy-tank replenishment. An EMG study with five participants further shows reduced target-muscle activity during static and dynamic tasks compared with gravity compensation and open-loop assistance, with reductions of up to 48% relative to movement without the exoskeleton in a dynamic multi-joint task.

</details>


### [124] [Near-sensor Computing for Rapid Visuotactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05725)
*Zhengying Zhu,Ruilin Zhang,Runze Hu,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种近传感器计算框架，通过流式硬件流水线实现触觉几何重建，在低功耗和确定性延迟下实现快速机器人触觉响应。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人触觉传感器（visuotactile sensor）的近传感器计算，涉及触觉几何重建和机器人反射控制，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：触觉几何重建（dense contact geometry reconstruction）；应用场景：机器人触觉感知与快速反射控制；传感器类型：visuotactile sensor（视觉触觉传感器）；目标类别：接触几何形状。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 采用近传感器计算框架，核心为spectral Poisson solver的流式硬件流水线（fully streaming hardware pipeline）。摘要未说明具体网络结构或深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化方法。论文的"轻量化"体现在硬件层面的低功耗（347 mW）和确定性延迟设计，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 功耗：347 mW（计算核心逻辑）；工作频率：166 MHz；延迟：首个深度值输出延迟35,107 cycles（0.211 ms），全帧128×128；机器人反射闭环延迟28.3±4.9 ms vs 主机方案169.9±27.8 ms。但这是针对触觉传感器的硬件设计，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：15种接触几何形状（contact geometries）；评价指标：重建深度误差（与双精度参考相比0.17%峰值接触深度）、延迟（ms）、功耗（mW）。与遥感目标检测数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）无关。

Experiments: 在15种接触几何形状上验证重建精度，深度误差为0.17%峰值接触深度；与主机方案对比，机器人反射闭环延迟从169.9±27.8 ms降至28.3±4.9 ms。摘要未说明与其它方法的对比实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。局限性包括：仅针对触觉传感器场景，未涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型压缩等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：（1）硬件流水线设计思路（fully streaming pipeline）对星上FPGA部署的确定性延迟设计有参考价值；（2）低功耗（347 mW）硬件实现思路可启发星上处理器的功耗优化设计。但论文不涉及目标检测模型或轻量化方法，借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Visuotactile sensors reconstruct dense contact geometry from measured surface gradients, but host-based processing increases power consumption and introduces data-transfer delays and variable scheduling latency, limiting the sensing and response speed of robotic systems. To address these limitations, we implement a near-sensor computing framework that includes a spectral Poisson solver as a fully streaming hardware pipeline. The computational core logic has an estimated power consumption of 347 mW and achieves high throughput without data-dependent branching or iterative convergence, thereby providing deterministic latency. Operating at 166 MHz, the pipeline produces the first depth value of each 128x128 frame 35,107 cycles after receiving the first input pixel, corresponding to a fixed latency of 0.211 ms. Across 15 contact geometries, the reconstructed depths differ from a double-precision reference by 0.17 % of the peak contact depth. On-chip decisions based on these reconstructions close a robot protective reflex loop in 28.3 +/- 4.9 ms, compared with 169.9 +/- 27.8 ms for an equivalent host-based loop using the same actuator. These results demonstrate that near-sensor reconstruction can provide accurate, energy-efficient, and deterministic tactile geometry on timescales suitable for rapid robotic contact responses.

</details>


### [125] [In-Context VLA: Endowing Vision-Language-Action Models with Language via In-Context Post-Training and Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05738)
*Jiarui Yang,Wen Huang,Jiale Zhang,Maowei Hu,Hang Guo*

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的技术启发是"in-context post-training"中结构化上下文注入的思路，但需大幅改造才能适配遥感目标检测场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the dominant recipe for generalist manipulation, yet they are almost universally trained by behavior cloning: a policy imitates expert action chunks conditioned on a static image and a fixed instruction. A natural remedy is to inject explicit reasoning through textual chain-of-thought (CoT). We show, both empirically and analytically, that free-form textual CoT degrades low-level control: the reasoning it produces is ungrounded, its latency breaks closed-loop timing, and, crucially, the reasoning and action tokens are optimized against conflicting objectives so that the policy learns to narrate rather than to act. We argue that what a VLA needs is not the ability to generate language, but the ability to consume grounded language. To this end we introduce \textbf{\ourmethod{}}, a framework that endows a VLA with language competence through (i) in-context post-training, in which perceptual evidence is injected as structured context and the model is supervised only on actions, and (ii) an agentic tool-use interface, in which the policy queries open-vocabulary detectors, monocular depth, and a vision--language model to actively acquire task-relevant information. Rather than emitting a single templated caption, our data engine produces diverse, paraphrased, and evidence-conditioned spatial descriptions, so that the policy learns to interpret language it has never seen verbatim. Across the RoboCasa-GR1, SimplerEnv, and LIBERO simulation benchmarks, together with 8 real-world robot manipulation tasks, our method consistently achieves SOTA results in both performance and efficiency when compared with CoT-based approaches under matched configurations.

</details>


### [126] [Acoustic-driven millimetric helical robot: ultrasonic synergistic manipulation in confined fluidic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05746)
*Hanlin Wang,Xin Wang,Xinwei Wei,Jiaxu Liu,Le Wang,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Abstract: Acoustic field-driven manipulation provides a non-contact and non-invasive strategy for controlling microscale and nanoscale objects, yet its extension to millimeter-scale robots was limited by insufficient propulsion efficiency in confined biological environments. Here, a coordinated multi-acoustic-field approach is introduced, which harnesses the synergistic action of acoustic radiation forces and acoustic streaming flows to enable controlled locomotion of millimeter-scale helical robots and enhance propulsion. Multiphysics simulations captured the dynamics of millimeter-scale helical robots under combined acoustic fields, and experimental validation demonstrated their locomotion capabilities, including planar navigation, inclined climbing, and vertical motion. Semi-autonomous navigation experiments further confirmed that ultrasonic synergy substantially improved maneuverability. In vitro tests in porcine venous vessels demonstrated that coordinated acoustic fields supported both unidirectional and reciprocating motion under biologically relevant confinement. These findings provide mechanistic insight into scaling acoustic micromanipulation to the millimetre regime and support biomedical applications requiring versatile and controllable robotic mobility.

</details>


### [127] [XEWorld: Can Action-Conditioned World Models Generalize to Unseen Robot Embodiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05799)
*Yixiang Chen,Jiabing Yang,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Keji He,Peiyan Li,Kai Wang,Ziheng He,Xiangnan Wu,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are promising learned simulators for robotic manipulation, yet evaluating them exclusively on training robots fails to reveal whether they capture physical dynamics or merely memorize visual patterns. To answer whether a model can faithfully render a robot it has never seen, we introduce XEWorld, a controlled cross-embodiment testbed for world models that isolates embodiments by evaluating held-out robots within physically identical scenes. Our systematic analysis uncovers a shared architectural bottleneck: current models act primarily as 2D visual pattern matchers whose generalization is governed by visual similarity rather than physical kinematic similarity. Driven by this limitation, they struggle to translate abstract numeric joint actions into coherent visual trajectories, and fail to predict dynamic visual changes from static initial observations. Consequently, successfully rendering an unseen embodiment zero-shot strictly requires heavily grounded cues, specifically pixel-space actions and explicit spatial-temporal alignment. Even when bypassing this zero-shot barrier via few-shot adaptation, the forced appearance recovery triggers catastrophic forgetting of seen embodiments. Together, these failures expose a critical inability to apply learned physical dynamics to novel visual appearances, highlighting that achieving true cross-embodiment generalization requires architectural innovations that decouple visual appearance from underlying physical dynamics.

</details>


### [128] [Coordinated Multi-Robot Disassembly for Makespan Optimization of Large-Scale Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05830)

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构，方法基于任务规划与运动规划（ST-RRT*、RRT*）。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning for disassembly tasks requires robots to operate in confined workspaces while coordinating their motions with other robots. To tackle this problem, we propose a planning method called coordinated multi-robot disassembly (CoMuDi). CoMuDi coordinates a team of robots for disassembly tasks. The input is a team of robots, an assembly of objects, and a dependency graph. Based on this information, we create compound tasks for pick, place, and exit motions. By propagating temporal constraints, we ensure that each robot can start and end their tasks as early as possible while avoiding collisions with nearby robots. By integrating the space-time RRT* planner (ST-RRT*) into CoMuDi, we ensure that individual tasks minimize arrival time and thereby help us minimize overall makespan. We compare the performance of CoMuDi using both ST-RRT* and RRT* planners with varying time bounds, demonstrating that the combination of CoMuDi and ST-RRT* leads to a higher success rate while minimizing makespan. Finally, we evaluate CoMuDi on six assemblies with up to 49 pieces and up to 9 robots. In those scenarios, we show that CoMuDi returns robot paths that exhibit low idle times, thereby demonstrating that CoMuDi can reliably solve large-scale assemblies.

</details>


### [129] [SkillMemo: Expert-guided Skill Memory Framework for Compositional Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05970)
*Changyuan Wang,Chubin Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Runhao Li,Angyuan Ma,Ke Chao,Yinan Liang,Xiuwei Xu,Ziwei Wang,Yansong Tang,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo)框架，通过隐式分解长程轨迹为潜在原子技能并构建动态情景记忆库，提升机器人操作任务中的组合泛化能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦机器人操作（robotic manipulation）领域的visuomotor模型，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景完全无关；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。属于泛机器人学习方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作（robotic manipulation），具体为组合任务（compositional tasks）的轨迹预测与动作生成。应用场景：仿真benchmark和真实世界操作任务。传感器类型：视觉（visuomotor）。目标类别：无目标检测相关目标。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: 基于Diffusion Policy (DP)和Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型作为backbone，引入Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构进行轨迹分割，设计skill-level episodic memory架构存储可检索的key-value技能表示。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅在仿真benchmark和真实世界操作任务上进行实验。

Experiments: 在仿真benchmark和真实世界操作任务上，SkillMemo一致提升了DP和VLA backbone的性能，达到SOTA，优于π_{0.5}，并展现出对未见任务配置的强组合泛化能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 摘要未说明模型参数量、推理速度、计算开销等关键效率指标。3. 仅在机器人操作场景验证，未在遥感/卫星数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但MoE架构用于隐式分解任务技能的思想，理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测中的多尺度/多类别特征解耦场景；episodic memory机制可用于遥感时序数据中的知识复用。然而，这些借鉴点非常间接，且论文未涉及轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接帮助有限。

Abstract: Embodied visuomotor models, including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have demonstrated promising performance on robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their potential remains fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of large-scale embodied trajectory datasets, leading to insufficient compositional generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios with limited capability to capture reusable skill structures. To address this limitation, we propose Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo) framework that implicitly decomposes long-horizon demonstrations into latent atomic skills and integrates skill-level features into a dynamic episodic memory bank for solving compositional tasks. Specifically, we first introduce an expert-guided trajectory segmentation module built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, which implicitly partitions trajectories into distinct skill primitives represented by learned gating coefficients. We further design a skill-level episodic memory architecture that stores compact skill representations as retrievable key-value pairs. During inference, the memory bank retrieves the most relevant skill primitives which are subsequently fused with the model's current gating distribution, providing a robust contextual prior to refine action predictions. Extensive experiments on the simulation benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SkillMemo consistently enhances both DP and VLA backbones, achieving state-of-the-art performance and outperforming $π_{0.5}$, while exhibiting strong compositional generalization to unseen task configurations.

</details>


### [130] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

TL;DR: 提出一种面向腿足机器人的端到端本体感知里程计估计方法TRACE，利用足部感知交叉注意力机制融合IMU和腿部运动学信息，提升不可靠接触条件下的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦腿足机器人本体里程计估计，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：本体感知里程计估计（proprioceptive odometry estimation）；应用场景：腿足机器人（legged robots）在室内外复杂地形下的运动估计；传感器类型：IMU（惯性测量单元）+ 关节编码器（joint measurements）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 端到端学习架构，包含足部感知交叉注意力模块（foot-aware cross-attention module），用于自适应加权IMU和腿部运动学token；包含时间编码器（temporal encoder）和预测头（prediction head）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然涉及机器人本体计算，但未讨论边缘部署或资源受限场景下的优化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：室内外多种地形（diverse indoor and outdoor terrains）下的实测数据；评价指标：位置漂移（position drift），与基于滤波、混合方法、纯学习方法等基线对比。

Experiments: 在多种地形下与经典滤波方法、混合方法、纯学习方法对比，TRACE在位置漂移上一致降低；消融实验验证了训练目标、策略随机化、真实世界微调的有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴；摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。交叉注意力机制（cross-attention）在多模态融合方面有通用参考价值，但场景差异过大，且不涉及目标检测和轻量化。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人长程操作（long-horizon robotic manipulation）；场景：仿真机器人操作benchmark；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: HiRoC分层框架，包含高层任务规划器（high-level planner）和低层动作执行器（low-level executor），基于预训练的VLA模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多种机器人操作benchmark（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在多种机器人操作benchmark上HiRoC优于强基线方法，消融实验验证了分层后训练和各关键组件的有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，其"分层规划-执行"思想在遥感在轨任务中可能类比为"任务级调度-模型级推理"的分层架构，但与本课题核心（目标检测模型轻量化）无直接关系。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [132] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Adaptive-WAM，一种基于Wan2.2-5B视频扩散模型的多出口（multi-exit）轨迹规划器，通过质量感知的提前退出机制动态分配DiT骨干深度，避免迭代式未来视频生成，从而在自动驾驶规划任务上降低推理延迟。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于自动驾驶（autonomous driving）场景，非遥感/卫星场景；核心是轨迹规划（trajectory planning）而非目标检测；但其中的"多出口提前退出（multi-exit early exit）+ 质量感知动态深度分配"思想与轻量化/动态推理高度相关，对遥感在轨目标检测的模型轻量化有方法层面的借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 3分。论文与遥感/卫星/目标检测场景无关，但"多出口提前退出+质量感知动态深度分配"的动态推理思想对在轨目标检测轻量化有明确的方法借鉴价值，值得精读其提前退出机制与质量评分器设计，但整体优先级中等。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：自动驾驶世界模型下的轨迹规划（trajectory planning / world-action model）。场景：自动驾驶（autonomous driving），非遥感/卫星。传感器：前视视频（front-view video）。目标类别：无目标检测，输出为ego轨迹（ego trajectory）。

Model architecture: 基于Wan2.2-5B Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 骨干。在选定的DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头（trajectory diffusion heads），并引入轻量级轨迹质量评分器（trajectory-quality scorer）作为提前退出判断。采用多出口（multi-exit）结构，从缓存隐藏状态（cached hidden state）继续到更深出口。

Lightweight method: 核心轻量化/加速手段为"多出口提前退出（multi-exit early exit）+ 质量感知动态深度分配（quality-aware dynamic depth allocation）"：通过轻量轨迹质量评分器在满足质量阈值时提前终止推理，避免迭代式classifier-free denoising循环和VAE解码。属于动态推理/自适应计算（adaptive computation）范畴，非传统剪枝/量化/蒸馏。

Onboard deployability: 摘要给出A100上的端到端规划延迟：自适应路由平均170 ms，比固定block-15规划器（190 ms）低约10%，比固定全深度规划器（320 ms）低约47%。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。注意：A100为数据中心GPU，非星上/边缘硬件，且5B级模型规模对在轨部署不现实。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NAVSIM（90.8 PDMS，自适应单轨迹；固定出口变体92.6 PDMS，64 proposals）、NAVSIM v2（89.9 EPDMS）、nuScenes（迁移，0.88 m平均L2误差，0.08%碰撞率）。指标：PDMS、EPDMS、L2误差、碰撞率、端到端延迟（ms）。

Experiments: 主要实验：1) 受控研究视频去噪timestep与DiT深度对规划性能的影响，发现规划性能对视频噪声水平不敏感，且中间层即可解码出强轨迹；2) Adaptive-WAM在NAVSIM上90.8 PDMS，固定出口变体92.6 PDMS；3) NAVSIM v2上89.9 EPDMS，为对比的前视视频世界模型规划器中最佳；4) 无需目标域微调迁移到nuScenes；5) A100上自适应路由将PDMS从90.62提升到90.79，同时延迟170 ms。摘要未说明消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1) 场景为自动驾驶，非遥感/卫星，传感器与目标类别完全不同；2) 模型规模为5B级（Wan2.2-5B），对在轨部署不现实；3) 部署硬件为A100数据中心GPU，未涉及星上/边缘硬件（FPGA/NPU）；4) 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等关键部署指标；5) 未涉及目标检测任务，仅轨迹规划；6) 未说明质量评分器的训练方式与阈值设定细节。

Ideas for my research: 1) "多出口提前退出 + 质量感知动态深度分配"思想可直接迁移到遥感在轨目标检测：在检测骨干（如轻量CNN/Transformer）的不同深度设置检测头，用轻量质量评分器判断当前层输出是否满足置信度阈值，满足则提前退出，否则继续到更深层，从而在星上算力受限时动态平衡精度与延迟。2) "中间层即可解码出强结果"的发现提示：遥感小目标检测中，浅层特征可能已含足够信息，可探索浅层提前退出以降低能耗。3) 质量评分器作为轻量辅助模块，可借鉴其"缓存隐藏状态继续推理"的机制，避免重复计算。4) 动态推理（adaptive computation）可作为剪枝/量化之外的轻量化补充维度，与量化、蒸馏结合用于在轨部署。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [133] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将概率视觉地点识别(VPR)与前馈神经3D几何(FF3D)度量位姿估计相结合的自顶向下(topometric)框架，用于顺序外观定位，通过离线建图工具和在线粒子滤波实现鲁棒且精确的视觉定位。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛视觉/机器人定位领域，涉及Visual Localization、Visual Place Recognition、FF3D metric pose estimation，与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测及轻量化均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。仅有的间接联系是VPR中的图像embedding提取器可替换，可能涉及轻量特征提取，但摘要未涉及。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于视觉定位领域，无遥感、目标检测或轻量化部署内容，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：顺序外观定位(sequential appearance-based localization)、视觉地点识别(VPR)、度量位姿估计(metric pose estimation)。场景：机器人/自动驾驶视觉定位，非遥感卫星场景。传感器：普通相机图像。目标类别：无目标检测，仅定位。

Model architecture: 自顶向下(topometric)框架，包含：1) 离线建图工具，建模场景不同区域的topometric pose-appearance交互；2) 在线粒子滤波(particle filter)，结合里程计(odometry)与地点置信度(belief over places)进行FF3D推理。VPR descriptor extractor与FF3D模型保持可互换(modular)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提到VPR通过低维图像embedding实现紧凑性(compactness)以支持高效扩展，但未给出具体轻量化技术或部署细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性数据。仅提及"compactness for efficient scalability"这一概念性表述，无量化部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 在三个已知基准(three known benchmarks)上评估，但摘要未给出具体数据集名称。评价指标未明确列出，仅称"substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods"。

Experiments: 在三个基准上评估，相比现有appearance-based方法有显著提升。聚焦分析显示顺序置信度(sequential belief)可在感知混叠(perceptual aliasing)下缓解严重失败。无消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，属于视觉定位领域；2) 摘要未提供任何模型复杂度、部署性能数据；3) 未给出具体数据集与指标；4) 依赖FF3D模型与VPR提取器，其计算开销未说明；5) 面向地面机器人/车辆场景，非星上环境。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1) 模块化设计思想——descriptor extractor与度量模型可互换，类似地，在轨检测中可将backbone/检测头模块化以便替换轻量版本；2) 概率框架(particle filter)融合多源信息的思想，可类比在轨多帧时序检测中的置信度融合；3) 离线建图+在线推理的分离思路，类似星上离线训练/在线推理。但整体与遥感目标检测轻量化方向关联很弱。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene. This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [134] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IcFuzz，首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim机器人仿真器的模糊测试（fuzzing）方法，利用LLM进行语义阶段分割、多层级变异算子与多臂老虎机调度，提升代码覆盖率和bug检测能力。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1分）。论文聚焦机器人仿真器（Isaac Sim）的模糊测试与软件bug检测，属于软件测试/程序分析领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于软件测试/仿真器fuzzing领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，无需精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：仿真器模糊测试（fuzzing）与bug检测。场景：机器人仿真（embodied AI / robotics simulator），非遥感场景。传感器类型：无（不涉及遥感传感器）。目标类别：无（不涉及目标检测）。

Model architecture: IcFuzz框架包含三部分：1) LLM-based语义阶段分割（将仿真程序分解为结构化阶段，捕获上下文相关的对象语义）；2) 多层级变异算子（multi-level mutation operators），在层级粒度上系统化测试仿真器；3) 多臂老虎机（multi-armed bandit）算法自适应调度变异算子。核心是软件测试框架而非神经网络模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。其"轻量"概念与模型轻量化无关，而是指测试效率/调度优化。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：代码覆盖率（code coverage）、bug检测数量（unique crashes）、开发者确认/修复的bug数。实验设置：三轮12小时测试，约四个月持续测试。未使用遥感数据集。

Experiments: IcFuzz达到基线约190%-205%的代码覆盖率；三轮12小时测试平均检测3.7个unique crashes（基线为0）；约四个月内发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与本研究方向基本无关，无遥感、目标检测、轻量化相关内容。其方法（LLM语义分割、多臂老虎机调度）虽在软件测试领域有创新，但对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题借鉴价值有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发：LLM-based语义分割与多臂老虎机自适应调度思想，或可类比用于轻量化NAS搜索策略或剪枝策略的自适应调度，但属于牵强类比，不构成直接可复现方向。总体不建议作为本课题参考。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [135] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）作为自动驾驶零样本控制器时，其决策是否真正基于视觉输入，通过输入消融实验（盲图、重复输入、反射、非视觉基线等）发现多数VLM存在图像不变性或近乎恒定输出，最终提出一个泄漏控制的对称一致性守护器（symmetry-consensus guardian），将VLM定位为有界、选择性的危险辅助器而非整体零样本控制器。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文主题是自动驾驶场景下的VLM零样本控制与视觉接地（visual grounding）评估，属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法。它不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或在轨智能，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可借鉴的是其"输入消融/接地性验证"方法论，可用于评估遥感检测模型是否真正依赖视觉特征，但整体与本研究方向相关性弱。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为自动驾驶VLM视觉接地性评估，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何轻量化/部署技术。仅其输入消融方法论有微弱间接参考价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：VLM零样本控制（zero-shot control）与视觉接地性评估；场景：自动驾驶（两个embodiment、三个simulator）；传感器类型：视觉图像（simulator）；目标类别：纵向/横向危险（longitudinal/lateral hazards）、前车距离（lead gap）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 评估对象包括：9个直接动作模型（direct-action models）、6个结构化局部VLM（structured local VLMs）、一个探索性VLM-MPC层级结构。提出方法：post-hoc泄漏控制的对称一致性守护器（symmetry-consensus guardian），从16个校准帧中选出2个模型，冻结2-of-4危险投票机制，在原始与反射视图上运行。另有图像仅确定性正控制（image-only deterministic positive control）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。本文关注的是VLM决策的视觉接地性而非部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据：32,874次评分调用（scored calls），覆盖两个embodiment和三个simulator；16个校准帧、272个held-out帧、12折leave-one-episode-out。指标：MAE（lead gap估计0.090 m）、balanced accuracy（0.954，bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]）、committed balanced accuracy（0.973 at 0.824 coverage）、action agreement（0.934）、镜像等变（mirror equivariance）。

Experiments: 主要结果：直接控制结果多为负面——恒定SLOW策略优于脚本化几何控制器；多个模型图像不变或近乎恒定；能识别纵向危险的模型在反射下仍无法正确转换LEFT/RIGHT。无局部VLM满足联合纵向+横向接地标准。图像仅确定性正控制以0.090 m MAE和精确镜像等变确认刺激携带足够视觉信息，失败是模块化的而非普遍的。对称一致性守护器在272个held-out帧上达到0.954 balanced accuracy，12折全部恢复相同模型对和阈值；在平局时弃权可将committed balanced accuracy提升至0.973（coverage 0.824）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，场景为自动驾驶simulator。2. 不涉及任何目标检测任务或轻量化技术。3. 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算开销、部署可行性。4. 结论具有场景特异性（自动驾驶VLM），难以直接迁移到遥感在轨检测。5. 未说明VLM具体架构、训练数据、推理成本。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，其"输入消融/接地性验证"方法论对遥感在轨检测有间接借鉴价值：1) 可借鉴"盲图/重复输入/反射"等消融实验，验证遥感检测模型是否真正依赖图像特征而非数据集偏置或恒定先验，提升模型鲁棒性。2) "对称一致性守护器"的投票与弃权机制，可启发遥感旋转框检测中针对镜像/旋转等几何变换的等变性校验。3) 其"模块化失败而非普遍失败"的结论提示：在轨检测中可针对特定失效模式（如小目标、旋转目标）做模块化诊断。但整体借鉴价值有限，不涉及轻量化核心问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [136] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Prior-SG框架，利用大语言模型生成的先验图与概率对齐方法，将RGB-D传感器流转化为层次化3D场景图，用于自主移动平台的高层空间推理。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自主移动机器人平台（如室内机器人）的3D场景图生成与语义区域分割，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：层次化3D场景图生成、语义区域分割；应用场景：自主移动机器人（室内/开放平面环境）；传感器类型：RGB-D相机；目标类别：场景中的物体实例与功能区域（如房间、走廊等），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: Prior-SG框架包含：多尺度开放词汇特征融合策略构建的Instance Graph、大语言模型动态合成的Prior Graph（逻辑先验）、马尔可夫随机场优化融合视觉/几何/离散物体专家与拓扑先验。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化或部署相关方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然面向机器人平台，但未讨论星上部署或边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多种模拟住宅数据集和大规模开放平面真实环境；评价指标：语义区域分割精度（state-of-the-art），与近期基线方法对比。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和指标数值。

Experiments: 在模拟住宅数据集和真实开放平面环境中验证，Prior-SG在语义区域分割精度上达到SOTA，能鲁棒地划分无物理墙的远距离功能边界，并具备零样本本体灵活性。摘要未提供消融实验或详细对比数据。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，缺乏遥感场景、目标检测任务、轻量化方法等核心要素。摘要未说明计算开销、实时性等对部署关键的信息。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点有限。但以下思路可能有启发：（1）利用大语言模型生成先验知识指导场景理解的方法，可类比用于遥感场景中基于语义先验的轻量检测；（2）多尺度开放词汇特征融合策略，可迁移到遥感多尺度目标检测中；（3）马尔可夫随机场融合异构专家信息的思想，可用于遥感多模态数据融合。但整体相关性弱，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [137] [ErgoSurf: Ergodic Control for the Coverage of Unknown Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06208)
*Stefan Schneyer,Timo Bachmann,Maged Iskandar,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于触觉感知的在线遍历控制框架，利用高斯过程隐式曲面模型在未知表面几何上同时实现覆盖与重建。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是机器人触觉遍历控制与表面几何重建，属于机器人操作领域，与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：表面遍历覆盖与几何重建；场景：机器人接触式操作（如打磨、清洁）；传感器：触觉传感器；目标：未知表面几何。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 高斯过程隐式曲面（GPIS）模型，用于学习表面几何；热扩散类比计算势场以生成遍历轨迹。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真与真实机器人实验；重建误差作为评价指标。无遥感相关数据集。

Experiments: 仿真与真实机器人实验验证了同时遍历覆盖与在线表面几何学习的能力，重建误差接近真值。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机器人触觉控制领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: Contact-centric tasks on surfaces, ranging from inspection and cleaning to sanding and polishing, require robots to systematically cover the surface while maintaining stable contact. Ergodic control generates trajectories that spend time at a location proportional to a desired, task-specific spatial distribution, enabling efficient information gathering and coverage. However, traditional ergodic control methods rely on prior knowledge of surface geometry or require a vision sensory input to scan the geometry beforehand, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios with unknown or dynamic environments. This paper introduces a novel online ergodic control framework that achieves systematic surface coverage while simultaneously reconstructing unknown surface geometry. We employ a Gaussian Process Implicit Surface (GPIS) model that learns global surface geometry from intrinsic tactile sensing during execution. For efficient online planning, we approximate the surface locally using point clouds sampled from tangent planes at observed contact points and iteratively fit them to the Gaussian Process. This approximation simultaneously serves as the sampling domain for both the target and the coverage distributions. We employ a heat-diffusion analogy to compute potential fields that guide ergodic exploration, translating spatial coverage objectives into smooth robot trajectories. We demonstrate our framework through simulation and real-robot experiments, validating simultaneous ergodic coverage and online surface geometry learning with reconstruction errors approaching the ground truth.

</details>


### [138] [VIDP: Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy for Compliant Robot Manipulation from Diverse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06210)
*Hisham Khalil,Neil Fernandes,Thomas M. Kwok,Hsiu-Chin Lin,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP)，一种基于模仿学习的可变阻抗控制框架，通过任务参数化的方向感知混合模型从演示中提取物理一致的轨迹分布，无需力传感器即可联合预测位姿动作和任务顺应性。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1分）。论文聚焦机器人接触丰富的操作任务（contact-rich manipulation）与可变阻抗控制（variable impedance control），属于机器人学/模仿学习领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，属于机器人阻抗控制领域，无遥感、目标检测或轻量化内容，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：模仿学习驱动的可变阻抗控制（imitation learning-based variable impedance control）；应用场景：机器人接触丰富操作（contact-rich manipulation）；传感器：无力传感器（force-agnostic kinematic data）；目标类别：不适用（非检测任务）。

Model architecture: 核心框架为Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP)，采用扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）作为动作生成主干，结合Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM)提取轨迹分布，并将分布映射为刚度（stiffness）剖面，实现位姿动作与任务顺应性的联合预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法，也未讨论实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标包括任务成功率（task success rate）、交互力（interaction forces）、跟踪误差（tracking errors），并与固定阻抗（fixed-impedance）基线对比。

Experiments: 真实世界实验（real-world experiments）表明VIDP在任务成功率上显著优于固定阻抗基线，相比高刚度控制器降低了交互力，相比低刚度基线降低了跟踪误差。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：该方法面向机器人操作而非遥感场景；未涉及任何遥感数据、目标检测或模型轻量化；TP-DAMM与扩散策略的计算开销在星上/边缘资源受限环境下可能不具可行性，但摘要未提供相关数据。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可迁移的潜在思路是：论文通过Task-Parameterized模型从数据分布中提取物理一致特征，这一"分布建模+任务参数化"思想在遥感小目标检测中或可类比用于跨场景/跨传感器域适应（domain adaptation），但需自行设计轻量化实现。此外，扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）作为生成式检测/回归框架的思路，可参考其在遥感目标检测中的轻量化变体，但论文本身未提供任何遥感或轻量化证据。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise tracking and mechanical compliance, where variable impedance control can improve robustness in task success, whereas static compliance cannot adapt to varying contact constraints. Variable impedance skills can be learned from demonstrations, avoiding complex modeling, but compliance is a hidden variable in force-agnostic kinematic data. While existing methods infer compliance from trajectory variations, these variations may reflect geometric adaptation and not intentional compliance when subject to changing spatial layouts. Therefore, this letter introduces Variable Impedance Diffusion Policy (VIDP), an imitation learning-based variable impedance control framework leveraging a Task-Parameterized Directionality-Aware Mixture Model (TP-DAMM) to extract physically consistent trajectory distributions from diverse demonstrations. By mapping distributions to stiffness profiles, VIDP jointly predicts pose actions and task compliance without force sensors. Real-world experiments show that VIDP significantly outperforms fixed-impedance baselines in task success rate while reducing interaction forces with respect to high stiffness controllers and tracking errors with respect to low stiffness baselines.

</details>


### [139] [Design and Evaluation of a Touchscreen-Based Teleoperation Interface for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06219)
*Juan José García Cárdenas,Alperen Kenan,Hamidreza Raei,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani,Arash Ajoudani,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种用于核工业机器人遥操作的触摸屏界面，通过手指连续运动映射实现更自然的表面接触任务控制，与摇杆相比任务完成时间减少53.5%，认知负荷降低17.3%。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于核工业机器人遥操作界面设计（触摸屏 vs 摇杆），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人遥操作界面设计；应用场景：核工业表面接触任务（如拭子采样）；传感器类型：未涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：未涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及深度学习模型或网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 用户实验：20名参与者；任务：使用Franka Emika Panda机械臂模拟表面操作（远程跨国控制）；评价指标：任务完成时间、路径覆盖面积（in-area coverage）、超调量（overshoot）、NASA-TLX认知负荷量表（0-100）。

Experiments: 触摸屏 vs 摇杆 vs 单点击自动模式对比：触摸屏任务完成时间中位数2.50 min vs 摇杆5.38 min（-53.5%）；正弦路径覆盖面积90.7% vs 84.1%；NASA-TLX均值43 vs 52（-17.3%）；自动模式TLX最低（31）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于人机交互/机器人遥操作领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何技术交集。

Abstract: Intuitive teleoperation interfaces are crucial for the safe and effective operation of robotic manipulators in challenging environments. In the nuclear industry, surface contact tasks such as swab sampling require precise path and force tracking, obstacle avoidance, and sustained operator attention, which conventional joystick interfaces struggle to support effectively.
  This study designs and evaluates a novel touchscreen teleoperation interface that maps continuous finger movements directly to robotic manipulator motions, provides finer velocity control, and integrates control with visualization, enabling more natural, precise, and intuitive surface interaction than conventional controllers. A comparative user study with 20 participants evaluated task performance and workload using the proposed touchscreen, a conventional joystick, and a single-click autonomous mode. Tasks simulated realistic surface manipulation using a Franka Emika Panda arm, remotely controlled from another country. Kinematic, physiological, and behavioral data were recorded to comprehensively assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator trust across each control condition.
  Participants completed teleoperation tasks more efficiently and accurately with the touchscreen interface, achieving a 53.5% reduction in completion time (median: 2.50 vs. 5.38 min), higher in-area coverage on the sinusoidal path (90.7% vs. 84.1%), and lower overshoot on both path geometries compared with the joystick. Cognitive load, quantified via NASA-TLX (0-100), decreased from joystick to touchscreen (mean TLX 52 to 43; -9 points, -17.3%) and was lowest under the autonomous one-click mode (31; -21 points vs. joystick, -40.4%; -12 vs. touchscreen, -27.9%). This research presents an easy-to-implement touchscreen interface that improves performance in teleoperated surface tasks while reducing cognitive load.

</details>


### [140] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于示教学习(LfD)的框架，通过高斯混合模型/高斯混合回归(GMM/GMR)学习人类手写轨迹，使机器人运动更类人化，并通过用户感知评估验证效果。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。研究主题是机器人示教学习与类人运动生成，属于机器人学与人机交互领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人示教学习(LfD)与轨迹生成；应用场景：人机交互、机器人运动规划；传感器类型：触摸屏遥操作界面；目标类别：手写字母轨迹(52种拉丁字母大小写组合)。

Model architecture: 基于GMM(高斯混合模型)对示教轨迹进行概率建模，GMR(高斯混合回归)用于轨迹生成与泛化。扩展了传统GMM/GMR方法，引入力(force)和归一化时间(normalized time)维度，并支持非连续多段轨迹处理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建手写轨迹数据集，包含22名参与者、3,142次演示、52种拉丁字母大小写组合；评价指标：类人感知评分(0-100连续量表，50为中性中点)，总体得分71.50(SD=22.56)。

Experiments: 21名参与者的用户研究，评估生成轨迹的类人感知程度；识别出几何定位和轨迹序列为最影响感知的因素；参与者对类人机器人行为持积极态度。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人学/人机交互领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何与本研究课题相关的技术内容。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing. Building on the widely used Gaussian Mixture Model and Gaussian Mixture Regression approach for learning from demonstration, the framework is extended in this work by incorporating force and normalised time dimensions to enable richer representation of human dynamics, and adapting it to handle non-continuous, multi-segment trajectories, enabling generalisation across demonstrations. A user study with 21 participants evaluated the perceived human-likeness of the generated trajectories using a continuous scale anchored between robotic and human-like motion, normalised to 0-100 where 50 represents the neutral midpoint. The generated trajectories achieved an overall human-likeness score of 71.50 (SD=22.56), indicating that the majority of trajectories were perceived as more human-like. Participants identified geometric positioning and trajectory sequence as the most influential perceptual factors, and reported positive attitudes toward human-like robot behaviour. The datasets are released as open-source, providing a reproducible benchmark for developing and evaluating human-like robot motion methods.

</details>


### [141] [GeniWorld: A Generalizable Interactive World Model for Robotic Manipulation via Visual Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06332)
*Chenghao Gu,Hanyang Yu,Jingbo Zhang,Haitao Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Jinghe Wang,Hanglei Jin,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Generalist robot policies exhibit strong capabilities, but their robustness in complex and unseen environments remains limited. Scaling robot learning and evaluation in diverse real-world environments remains costly and challenging. Action-conditioned world models offer a promising alternative, but they often suffer from limited action controllability and poor generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To this end, we present GeniWorld, an interactive world model for robots that generalizes robustly across unseen scenarios. Building on pretrained video generative models, we use URDF-based rendering to transform numerical actions into visual action representations, enabling spatially grounded action control. By explicitly decoupling embodiment kinematics from environmental dynamics, our model mitigates scene overfitting and facilitates modeling of robot-environment interactions. To achieve closed-loop control, we construct an autoregressive video prediction model integrated with high-frequency robot kinematic control, enabling interaction with both robot policies and human teleoperators. In our experiments, even when trained solely on limited fixed-scene data, our model achieves superior in-domain performance and robust zero-shot generalization to highly randomized, unseen environments. For downstream applications, GeniWorld serves as a scalable policy evaluator that remains reliable under environmental perturbations. Furthermore, even with limited real-world demonstrations, GeniWorld generates diverse manipulation trajectories within the world model, improving downstream policy performance and robustness in complex environments.

</details>

Abstract: We present a MR safe, master-slave robot manipulator for abdominal interventions in the MRI chamber. A human operated 2+1-DoF master controller manipulator transmits motion and force to a 2+1-DoF slave manipulator via fluid transmission. Jointly, a digital master controller provides multimodal control capability beyond common split axis or mode switchable hybrid human-digital controller configurations found in previous studies. High input impedance, low-leakage, elastomeric fluid actuators are delegated to remote angulation control. Low-friction graphite piston cylinders are delegated to needle insertion axis remote actuation given the sub-newton force transparency and sub-millimeter motion transmission over bedside fluid piping lengths. The device enables real-time MRI guided interventions allowing manual, digital, hybrid, and collaborative control modes. Collaborative tasks such as assisted tissue penetration, fault-driven virtual fixture, and motion compensation through feedback control are presented in this paper. Preliminary MR scanner results demonstrate manipulator functional viability for an in-vivo pig experiment in bedside, manual control mode configuration.

</details>


### [143] [DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06374)
*Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Yangyang Zheng,Pingyue Sheng,Jiayu Dong,Ruixin Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Runze Yu,Wen Chen,Liuqing Yang,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DyPES-VLA，一种跨具身（cross-embodiment）的 Vision-Language-Action 模型，通过学习共享动力学先验（dynamics priors）和具身专属的 Mixture-of-Experts 动作头，实现异构机器人策略的通用训练，无需手动对齐异构动作格式。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2 分）。论文属于机器人操作（robot manipulation）领域的 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型，与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测场景无关，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。其相关性仅在于：VLA 中使用的 vision-language model (VLM) 与遥感多模态理解有一定技术交集，且 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 结构对模型压缩/稀疏化有一定借鉴意义，但整体属于泛视觉-语言-动作方法，与本研究方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文属于机器人操作 VLA 领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，无部署指标，仅 MoE 结构有微弱借鉴价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：跨具身机器人操作策略学习（cross-embodiment robot manipulation policy）。应用场景：仿真与真实机器人操作（LIBERO、RoboCasa-GR1、RoboTwin 2.0）。传感器类型：视觉（vision）+ 语言（language）+ 动作（action）。目标类别：机器人操作任务，非遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: DyPES-VLA 由两部分组成：(1) 共享动力学先验学习模块——在跨具身数据上用 future-prediction 目标训练 VLM，使共享 query 表示捕获物体运动、接触和交互引起的场景变化；(2) 具身专属 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 动作头——共享 attention 层捕获通用时间动作结构，具身专属的 feed-forward experts 解析不同具身的运动学约束与控制语义，直接在各自原生动作空间输出控制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS 等）。唯一相关的结构是 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 动作头，其稀疏激活特性（仅激活部分 experts）在理论上可降低推理计算量，但摘要未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。且该模型面向机器人操作而非星上/边缘部署，不具备在轨部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO、RoboCasa-GR1、RoboTwin 2.0（均为机器人操作基准）。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate），分别达到 98.0%（LIBERO）、59.25%（RoboCasa-GR1）、89.02%（RoboTwin 2.0）。

Experiments: 在仿真和真实世界评估中达到 SOTA：LIBERO 98.0%、RoboCasa-GR1 59.25%、RoboTwin 2.0 89.02%。摘要未给出消融实验、对比方法细节或计算开销对比。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。关键缺口：无任何轻量化/部署指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗）；无遥感场景验证；无目标检测任务；MoE 的稀疏激活在推理时的实际加速效果未量化。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨检测弱相关，仍有两点可借鉴：(1) MoE 动作头中"共享 attention 层 + 具身专属 experts"的设计思想，可类比到遥感多传感器（光学/SAR/高光谱）或多平台场景下共享特征提取 + 专属检测头的轻量化结构；(2) future-prediction 动力学先验学习可启发遥感时序数据（多帧卫星影像）中利用运动/变化先验增强小目标检测。但需注意其计算开销较大，直接用于星上部署需配合剪枝/量化。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robot manipulation, but training a single generalist policy for heterogeneous robot embodiments remains an open problem. Existing methods have two main limitations. First, they underuse dynamics priors shared across diverse visual and interaction data, limiting cross-embodiment transfer. Second, they require extensive manual preprocessing to convert embodiment-specific actions into a common format. To overcome these limitations, we propose DyPES-VLA, a cross-embodiment VLA that learns shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific control. First, we learn shared dynamics priors by training the vision-language model (VLM) with a future-prediction objective on cross-embodiment data, driving the shared query representation to capture object motion, contact, and interaction-induced scene changes. Second, an embodiment-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head translates these shared dynamics priors into executable controls directly in each embodiment's native action space, without manually pre-aligning heterogeneous actions into a common format. This head shares attention layers to capture common temporal action structures, while its embodiment-specific feed-forward experts resolve the unique kinematic constraints and control semantics of distinct embodiments. As a generalist policy, our \ourmethod achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-world evaluations, reaching 98.0% success on LIBERO, 59.25% on RoboCasa-GR1, and 89.02% on RoboTwin~2.0.

</details>


### [144] [$ω$-0: A Latent Predictive World Action Model for Concurrent Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06375)
*Zhe Li,Zhenzhe Zhang,Yangyang Wei,Wenjie Zhang,Xichen Yuan,Peiyuan Zhi,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Fengjie Gao,Jianfei Yang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ω-0，一个用于真实世界人形机器人并发移动-操作（loco-manipulation）的潜在预测全身世界-动作模型，通过轻量预测目标耦合潜在视觉前瞻与扩散式全身动作生成。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文面向人形机器人家庭任务（loco-manipulation），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测或遥感场景理解。虽然摘要中出现"lightweight predictive objective"（轻量预测目标），但这是指学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入作为预测目标，并非模型压缩/轻量化（pruning/quantization/distillation/NAS），与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文面向人形机器人loco-manipulation，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，仅"轻量预测目标"和"潜在预测"思想有极有限的间接借鉴价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：人形机器人并发移动-操作（concurrent loco-manipulation）；场景：真实世界家庭任务（11个家庭任务）；传感器：egocentric RGB、exocentric RGB、exocentric depth；目标类别：机器人全身动作（whole-body action），非目标检测。

Model architecture: ω-0 为潜在预测全身世界-动作模型（latent predictive whole-body world-action model）。输入为语言指令、当前视觉观测、机器人本体感知状态（proprioceptive state），直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜在表示（whole-body action latents）。采用轻量预测目标学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入（compact future observation embeddings），耦合潜在视觉前瞻（latent visual foresight）与基于扩散的全身动作生成（diffusion-based whole-body action generation）。利用基于控制器的仿真回放（controller-based simulation replay）将人类/公开视觉-运动先验接地为机器人可执行的动作潜在表示。

Lightweight method: 摘要中的"lightweight predictive objective"指学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入作为预测目标，属于预测目标设计，并非模型压缩/加速方法。未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘/星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。面向人形机器人而非星上/边缘部署，与在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ω-HOME，40+小时真实世界家庭人形数据集，含同步多视角观测、全身SMPL动作、机器人状态、动作潜在表示。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标，仅提及在11个家庭任务上与代表性imitation learning、VLA、humanoid、WAM基线对比，ω-0产生平滑的manipulate-while-moving行为并持续优于基线。

Experiments: 在11个真实世界家庭任务上验证，单个ω-0模型可产生平滑的移动-操作并发行为，并一致优于代表性imitation learning、VLA、humanoid、WAM基线。摘要未给出具体数值结果或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向基本无关；摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、推理速度等部署指标；未涉及目标检测任务；"lightweight"仅指预测目标设计而非模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：其一，"学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入作为轻量预测目标"这一思想在遥感在轨场景中可类比为用紧凑特征表示替代完整重建，降低计算开销；其二，潜在预测（latent predictive）与扩散生成结合的多模态融合思路，或可迁移到遥感多传感器（光学/SAR）特征融合；其三，controller-based simulation replay将先验接地为可执行动作的思想，可类比为将仿真遥感数据接地到真实在轨检测。但整体与目标检测和模型轻量化（压缩/加速）关联较弱。

Abstract: Humanoid household tasks often require concurrent loco-manipulation, where the robot must move, adjust posture, maintain balance, and manipulate objects as a single coordinated behavior. Yet existing humanoid policies typically decompose locomotion and manipulation, while recent world-action models remain either arm-centric or video-centered. We present $ω$-0, a latent predictive whole-body world-action model for real-world humanoid concurrent loco-manipulation. Given a language instruction, current visual observation, and robot proprioceptive state, $ω$-0 directly predicts controller-compatible whole-body action latents for real-robot execution. Rather than reconstructing future videos, $ω$-0 learns compact future observation embeddings as a lightweight predictive objective, coupling latent visual foresight with diffusion-based whole-body action generation. The model supports egocentric RGB, exocentric RGB, and exocentric depth inputs, and leverages controller-based simulation replay to ground human/public visual-motion priors into robot-executable action latents. We further collect $ω$-HOME, a 40+ hour real-world household humanoid dataset with synchronized multi-view observations, whole-body SMPL motions, robot states, and action latents. Real-world experiments on 11 household tasks demonstrate that a single $ω$-0 model can produce smooth manipulate-while-moving behaviors and consistently outperform representative imitation learning, VLA, humanoid, and WAM baselines.

</details>

### [145] [Multi-Target Micro-Motion Parameter Estimation using MIMO-FMCW Radar with Limited Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05216)
*Chandrashekhar Rai,Sanjay J. Alex,Arpan Chattopadhyay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于压缩感知的方法，利用MIMO FMCW雷达和随机稀疏阵列，从少量测量中估计旋翼无人机（如螺旋桨小无人机）的微动参数（旋转频率、桨叶长度），并用于无人机运动类型分类。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。原因：该论文聚焦于地面/近地雷达对无人机微动参数的估计与分类，属于雷达信号处理与压缩感知领域，不涉及遥感卫星图像、不涉及目标检测（object detection）、不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然涉及"UAV detection"，但这里的"detection"是雷达探测/参数估计，而非遥感图像中的目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：微动参数估计（旋转频率、桨叶长度）与无人机运动类型分类；应用场景：地面雷达对小型无人机的探测与识别；传感器类型：MIMO FMCW雷达（非光学/SAR/高光谱遥感传感器）；目标类别：小型旋翼无人机（UAV with propellers）。

Model architecture: 方法框架：基于压缩感知（compressive sensing）的微动参数估计流程，包括：1) 利用随机稀疏线性天线阵列的MIMO FMCW雷达；2) 随机LFM chirp序列实现慢时间域随机采样；3) 先估计目标距离、速度、到达角（AoA）以识别整体运动；4) 构建三维点目标响应（3D-PTR）并从总回波中减去以提取微多普勒特征；5) 使用一维正交匹配追踪（1D-OMP）算法在压缩感知框架下联合估计旋转频率和桨叶长度；6) 基于决策规则的多目标场景扩展与运动类型分类。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。文中使用的"压缩感知"（compressive sensing）是信号采样与重构技术，并非深度学习模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该方法面向地面雷达处理，未涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真数据（simulation results），未使用真实遥感/卫星数据集；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提及"accurately estimates micro-motion parameters"。

Experiments: 实验方式：仿真验证，证明该方法能在慢时间维和空间维的有限测量下准确估计微动参数，并扩展到多目标场景。未与现有方法进行定量对比实验。

Limitations: 1) 仅基于仿真数据验证，缺乏真实雷达实测数据验证；2) 未涉及遥感卫星图像目标检测任务；3) 未涉及任何深度学习模型或轻量化技术；4) 未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性；5) 压缩感知框架的计算复杂度在实时处理场景下可能较高，但摘要未讨论。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) 压缩感知思想在遥感图像稀疏表示或特征压缩方面有潜在应用，但与本课题的深度学习模型轻量化方向不同；2) 微多普勒特征分析可用于遥感SAR图像中旋转目标（如风力发电机、旋转天线）的检测，但与本课题核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: This work presents a compressive sensing-based approach for estimating the micro-motion parameters of targets with rotating components, such as small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) with propellers, using fewer measurements than conventional methods. A multiple-input-multiple-output (MIMO) frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar employing a randomly spaced sparse linear antenna array is utilized. Random sequences of linear frequency-modulated (LFM) chirps are transmitted to enable random sampling in the slow-time domain. At first, the range, velocity, and angle of arrival (AoA) of the targets are estimated to identify the bulk motion. A three-dimensional point target response (3D-PTR) is then constructed using the estimated parameters and subtracted from the total radar return to extract the micro-Doppler signatures associated with target rotation. These residual signals are processed within a compressive sensing (CS) framework using the one-dimensional orthogonal matching pursuit (1D-OMP) algorithm to jointly estimate the propellers' rotation frequencies and blade lengths with the help of a parametric dictionary. The proposed approach is also extended to a multi-target scenario. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach accurately estimates micro-motion parameters with limited measurements in the slow-time and the spatial dimensions, validating its potential for UAV detection applications. These estimated parameters are then used to distinguish among various UAV motion types using a classification framework based on a set of decision rules.

</details>

Abstract: Accurate channel estimation is essential for coherent transmission in ultra-massive multiple-input multiple-output (UM-MIMO) systems, where near-field propagation and high-dimensional spatial channels impose substantial signal processing challenges. Movable antenna architectures increase the estimation complexity further due to geometry-dependent channel variations. Existing approaches struggle to balance accuracy and complexity, motivating the use of environment-dependent propagation structures for efficient estimation. To this end, this paper proposes a unified channel map-based channel estimation framework for UM-MIMO systems, which integrates movable planar array reconfiguration and near-field spherical-wave modeling to support geometry-aware line-of-sight (LoS) estimation and efficient non-LoS (NLoS) recovery. A channel map-based LoS estimator is proposed combining coarse user position information with a Fisher information-guided antenna placement strategy. Two efficient NLoS estimation methods are also presented, including a sketch-based reduced-subspace estimator for low-complexity processing and a channel map-based estimator that leverages scatterer location information for near-optimal performance. The framework further incorporates visibility-region modeling and a structural similarity-based pilot assignment strategy for multi-user scenarios. Simulation results show that the proposed channel map-based framework improves estimation accuracy, reduces computational overhead, and enhances scalability compared with state-of-the-art benchmarks without channel maps.

</details>

*Baichuan Zhao,Qi Sun,Nan Li,Chih-Lin I*
