  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：概率预测（probabilistic prediction），输出头设计。场景：通用预测基准（representative prediction benchmarks），非遥感场景。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无具体目标类别。

Model architecture: HProbZ 输出头：将三种不确定性来源分别建模为 zonotope 的二进制（binary）、有界（bounded）、随机（stochastic）生成器，通过卷积得到闭式似然。共享有界生成器跨预测步耦合未来预测，单次前向即可精化所有剩余步的预测分布。推理时提供解析的逐模态风险与无分布多模态共形集合。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法，也未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提到"单次前向"（single forward pass）可精化所有剩余步预测，暗示计算效率上的潜在优势，但无量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称与评价指标，仅提及"representative prediction benchmarks"（代表性预测基准）和"same-encoder mixture baselines"（同编码器混合基线）作为对比。

Experiments: 在代表性预测基准上进行了实证分析，验证了相对同编码器混合基线的有效性，并强调其提供了混合预测器或凸共形预测器无法同时提供的结构性质。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。关键缺口包括：未涉及遥感/卫星场景、未涉及目标检测任务、未报告任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗）、未给出具体数据集与数值结果、未讨论与轻量化方法的结合。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨检测弱相关，但 HProbZ 的"多模态不确定性分离"思想对遥感目标检测有一定借鉴价值：遥感小目标/舰船检测中常存在类别混淆（离散模态选择）、定位漂移（有界系统误差）与噪声，可借鉴其将不确定性分解为三类生成器的思路，用于旋转框检测的定位不确定性建模。此外，其"单次前向精化多步预测"与"解析逐模态风险"在计算效率上对在轨实时推理有潜在启发，但需自行评估其计算开销是否满足星上约束。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [179] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，通过可恢复的驱逐策略（量化中间窗口+全精度高置信窗口）解决大语言模型推理中KV缓存的内存瓶颈问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦大语言模型推理中的KV缓存压缩，属于自然语言处理/大模型推理优化方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。虽然涉及量化（quantization）技术，但应用场景和问题域完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型自回归推理中的KV缓存管理；应用场景：长文本理解、检索、推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 三层KV缓存管理架构：全精度高置信窗口（high-confidence windows in full precision）+ 量化可恢复中间窗口（quantized recoverable tier）+ 删除最低置信窗口（delete lowest-confidence windows）。通过累积注意力分数动态更新窗口重要性，量化窗口可被反量化提升至全精度。

Lightweight method: 涉及量化（quantization）技术，将中间重要性窗口量化存储以节省内存，并在需要时反量化恢复。但这是针对LLM推理的KV缓存压缩，不是针对视觉模型的轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及在固定内存预算（fixed memory budget）下工作，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 长上下文理解、检索和推理基准测试（long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks）。评价指标：Future Missed Mass（未来遗漏质量）、Global LIR（全局重要性恢复率）、missed attention（遗漏注意力）、information retention（信息保持率）。

Experiments: 与代表性的驱逐策略（eviction baselines）和量化基线（quantization baselines）对比，QEvict在减少遗漏注意力和提高信息保持率方面持续改进。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 仅针对LLM推理场景，方法不可直接迁移；3. 未报告计算开销（量化/反量化带来的额外延迟）；4. 未讨论在边缘/星上部署的可行性。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴的通用思路：1. "可恢复的驱逐"（recoverable eviction）思想——在资源受限场景下，不永久丢弃信息，而是分级存储并允许恢复，这对星上模型推理中的特征缓存管理可能有启发；2. 量化+全精度分级存储策略，类似思路可用于遥感目标检测模型的多精度推理。但整体相关性极低，不建议深入跟进。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [180] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种联邦学习中基于缓存更新重用的通信压缩机制DG-FedReuse，通过年龄衰减的缓存更新和自适应Top-K数值表示来减少上行通信量。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是联邦学习中的通信效率优化问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务或模型轻量化/部署。虽然联邦学习在分布式场景中有一定关联，但本文聚焦于模拟器级别的缓存更新重用机制，未涉及遥感、目标检测或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：联邦学习通信压缩；应用场景：分布式图像分类；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：图像分类（6个数据集，具体类别未说明）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及使用随机头梯度差异代理（stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy）作为缓存重用条件。

Lightweight method: 本文方法属于通信压缩而非模型轻量化。涉及：年龄衰减缓存更新（age-decayed cached updates）、自适应per-tensor Top-K数值字段表示（adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation）、硬缓存年龄限制（hard cache-age limit）、最小新鲜客户端配额（minimum fresh-client quota）。这些是联邦学习通信优化技术，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文明确声明未建立端到端带宽减少、运行时或能耗节省、更快收敛等结论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：6个图像分类数据集（具体名称未说明）；50个虚拟客户端；Dirichlet标签异构性（α=0.5）；3个随机种子。评价指标：上行数据字段节省率（modeled update-data-field uplink saving）、测试准确率（test accuracy）。

Experiments: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的上行节省，对比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%；准确率差异为-5.29到-0.14个百分点。考虑对称密集模型下行链路敏感性后，节省降至41.68-42.71%，增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点。

Limitations: 论文明确声明：未建立无偏泛化保证、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能耗节省、更快收敛，或优于现有陈旧更新和懒惰聚合方法。结论依赖于模拟器实现和特定的计账边界。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：联邦学习中的通信压缩思想（如Top-K稀疏化、缓存重用）可类比到星地链路中模型更新的传输优化，但本文未涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务，且明确声明未验证实际带宽/能耗节省，因此直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [181] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向非光滑、超线性梯度增长、非凸势函数的子梯度驯化非调整朗之万算法（SG-TULA），并给出非渐近收敛界，在GPT-2预训练中验证了有效性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是基于Langevin动力学的采样算法及其在LLM预训练中的应用，属于机器学习/优化理论方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为概率采样与优化，应用场景为LLM预训练（GPT-2），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测等场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出SG-TULA算法（子梯度驯化非调整朗之万算法），是一种离散化Langevin扩散的采样方法，并非神经网络模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文在GPT-2预训练任务上验证，但未说明具体数据集；评价指标为Wasserstein-2距离收敛界和excess risk估计。

Experiments: 论文在GPT-2预训练中验证了SG-TULA及其boosted coordinate-wise变体，与finetuned AdamW和Muon对比，但未给出具体数值结果。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文聚焦于采样算法的理论收敛性分析，而非模型压缩或部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于优化/采样理论方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [182] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出量子启发的世界模型框架(QSWM)，使用复数表示和密度矩阵类潜变量进行预测性世界建模，在元胞自动机任务上验证。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是通用世界模型(world model)，使用量子启发的数学结构进行状态表示和预测，应用场景是元胞自动机(cellular automata)，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：世界模型(world model)预测建模；应用场景：元胞自动机(elementary cellular automata)；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: QSWM框架，包含结构化潜变量、潜转移算子、测量启发的解码映射。具体变体包括复数表示(complex-valued)和密度矩阵类(density-matrix-like)两种。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：元胞自动机(elementary cellular automata)；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称，仅提及"local predictive potential"和"long-horizon rollout"。

Experiments: 在元胞自动机上与经典基线方法对比。复数QSWM在局部预测上表现有潜力，但在长程推演(long-horizon rollout)和密度矩阵变体上存在局限性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在元胞自动机这种简单合成数据上验证，未在真实遥感图像上测试；3. 长程预测能力不足；4. 密度矩阵变体表现有限。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接借鉴价值。量子启发的表示方法(复数表示、密度矩阵)在遥感目标检测中可能作为一种新颖的特征表示思路，但论文本身未涉及检测任务，且计算开销可能更大，与轻量化目标相悖，不建议参考。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [183] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Surv-IPTB，一个基于注意力机制、用于生存分析中个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）估计的框架，将 IPTB 估计重构为二分类问题，并用区间值概率处理右删失数据。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文主题是医学/临床生存分析中的个体治疗获益概率估计，属于统计机器学习与医疗决策领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、地球观测、目标检测或任何模型压缩/部署技术。相关性评分：1 分。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文属于医学生存分析领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，无阅读价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：生存分析中的个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）估计，重构为二分类问题。应用场景：个性化医疗/临床治疗决策。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：患者个体（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: Surv-IPTB 框架：注意力机制（attention mechanism），含可学习的 query-key 变换，用于灵活、数据驱动的成对比较聚合；同时为删失样本学习软类别概率；采用区间值概率（interval-valued probabilities）表征不确定的治疗效应。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 或任何轻量化/模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息，且该模型面向医疗场景，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成数据集（synthetic datasets），包含螺旋（spiral）、钟形（bell-shaped）、圆形（circular）等复杂非线性特征空间。评价指标：摘要未明确给出具体指标名称。基线：meta-learner（T-learner 和 S-learner），配备 random survival forests、Cox proportional hazards、Beran estimators。

Experiments: 在合成数据集上验证，考察不同删失率（censoring rates）和治疗效应强度下的鲁棒性。结果显示在复杂非线性场景中优于 meta-learner 基线，而传统方法在这些场景中性能显著下降。代码已公开。

Limitations: 仅使用合成数据验证，缺乏真实临床数据评估；未报告具体指标数值、计算开销与部署信息；注意力机制的可解释性、区间值概率的统计性质等细节摘要未说明；与遥感/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 与本研究方向基本无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：其"将问题重构为二分类 + 注意力聚合 + 区间值概率处理不确定性"的建模思想，在概念层面可类比于遥感小目标检测中处理不确定/模糊标注（如旋转框角度不确定性）的思路，但迁移价值有限，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [184] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过凸学习+蒸馏将非线性动力系统转化为紧凑线性状态空间表示的端到端可证明方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于非线性动力系统的线性状态空间表示学习与蒸馏，属于系统辨识/控制理论/模仿学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：非线性动力系统建模与线性状态空间表示学习；应用场景：系统辨识、MuJoCo行为克隆；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 提出Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF)隐式谱预测器 + 谱到LDS蒸馏（spectral-to-LDS distillation）将隐式预测器转化为显式循环线性动力系统（LDS）。

Lightweight method: 涉及蒸馏（distillation）概念，但这里的蒸馏是将隐式谱预测器转化为显式线性动力系统，与模型压缩/知识蒸馏（teacher-student范式）不同。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是理论可证明性（dimension-free guarantee）而非实际部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 实验数据集：线性LDS基准测试、MuJoCo行为克隆任务；评价指标：平均预测误差（average prediction error）。

Experiments: 实验表明train-then-distill流水线产生的紧凑LDS预测器在性能上匹配或优于直接训练的基线方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 蒸馏方法针对的是动力系统表示学习，而非深度神经网络模型压缩；3. 缺乏实际部署指标；4. 仅在MuJoCo和线性LDS基准上验证，未涉及视觉任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的是"蒸馏"这一概念框架——将复杂模型转化为紧凑表示，但本文的蒸馏对象（谱预测器→LDS）与遥感目标检测中的知识蒸馏（大模型→小模型）本质不同，难以直接迁移。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [185] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需分组标注的虚假相关性样本检测方法，通过对收敛后模型施加固定输入扰动来区分"捷径样本"与"困难样本"，并用于重平衡训练以提升最差组准确率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。论文属于通用机器学习中的分布外泛化/鲁棒性研究，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对分布外泛化/虚假相关性检测有额外兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未提及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。任务为通用图像分类中的虚假相关性（spurious correlation）检测与鲁棒训练。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及"converged model"（收敛后的模型），未指定网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Waterbirds（用于最差组准确率评估）。评价指标：average accuracy（平均准确率）、worst-group accuracy（最差组准确率）。

Experiments: 在Waterbirds数据集上，所提方法将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实分组标签的上限为85.8%。摘要未说明与其他基线方法的详细对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 方法仅验证在Waterbirds数据集上，未在遥感场景下验证；3. 需要两次前向传播（一次正常推理+一次扰动推理），计算开销翻倍，不适用于资源受限的星上场景；4. 未讨论模型大小、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与本研究方向弱相关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：1. "输入扰动检测脆弱样本"的思想可用于遥感目标检测中识别模型在特定场景（如云雾遮挡、光照变化）下的失败模式；2. 无需分组标注的鲁棒训练方法可迁移到遥感场景中处理类别不平衡或域偏移问题；3. 但需注意该方法需要两次前向传播，计算开销大，不适合直接用于星上轻量化场景。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [186] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向不完全信息博弈的生成流网络框架IFlowNets，扩展了对抗流网络（AFlowNets），在博弈论应用中取得与OSMCCFR和RL方法相当或更优的性能与速度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解等视觉任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。论文属于博弈论与强化学习的交叉方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：不完全信息博弈中的策略学习。应用场景：博弈论（game theory）、不完全信息游戏。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: IFlowNets（Information Flow Networks），基于生成流网络（Generative Flow Networks）框架，扩展自Adversarial Flow Networks（AFlowNets）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 三个标准游戏环境（standard game environments）。评价指标：性能（performance）和速度（speed）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和指标细节。

Experiments: 在三个标准游戏环境中，IFlowNets与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR)和标准RL方法对比，性能与速度相当或更优。摘要未说明消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于博弈论与强化学习领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [187] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Explorative Modeling (XM)方法，通过生成K个输出并选择最接近的一个进行更新，声称"探索"是生成式预训练的第三轴，并证明该方法的本质是优化"自由度"(freedom)，而非传统的最小描述长度(MDL)。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文讨论的是生成式训练中的探索性建模理论，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是生成式训练的理论框架，未涉及任何遥感或目标检测任务场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。论文从理论层面讨论生成式训练范式，未给出具体的网络结构设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何部署相关信息。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。实验部分提到"induction experiments"和"Forward XM experiment"，但未给出标准数据集名称或评价指标。

Experiments: 实验包括：1) 理论证明XM平均损失与候选输出错过可接受区域的概率有关；2) Forward XM实验显示更大的K值增加或饱和了测量的自由度；3) 在30个案例中，自由度选择器在29个中优于验证集选择。

Limitations: 该论文是纯理论性工作，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不具备任何直接参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是生成式训练的理论基础，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题域、方法论和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [188] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoHarness-RL，让长时程LLM智能体通过离线学习harness策略，在线构建和更新外部harness状态（Belief/Progress/Experience），在ALFWorld上达到96.9%成功率。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文研究的是LLM智能体的外部执行支持（harness）策略学习，属于通用机器学习/LLM agent领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。唯一可借鉴的是其"cost-aware"策略（选择性访问外部状态以降低开销）与"harness annealing"（将重复模式内化进模型以减少外部调用）的思想，在概念上类似于将计算/存储开销从外部转移到模型内部，与轻量化中"减少冗余计算"的思路有隐喻性关联，但并非遥感或目标检测场景。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于LLM agent/harness策略学习领域。仅"cost-aware选择性访问"和"模式内化"思想有概念层面的隐喻借鉴价值，不建议作为核心参考文献，可作泛读了解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：长时程LLM智能体的harness策略学习（harness policy learning）。场景：ALFWorld交互式环境（家用任务）。传感器/目标类别：不涉及遥感传感器或目标检测，属于文本/工具交互的LLM agent。

Model architecture: 基于Qwen3-8B LLM。引入BPE（Belief, Progress, Experience）作为策略面向的harness外部状态。包含两个阶段：supervised harness fine-tuning（教基础agent harness动作空间和构建外部状态）+ cost-aware GRPO（探索协调策略，选择性读取/更新/整合外部状态）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统轻量化方法（pruning/quantization/distillation/NAS）。但包含"cost-aware"思想：通过GRPO学习选择性访问外部状态，减少harness调用频率；"harness annealing"将重复harness使用模式内化进模型策略，从频繁外部调用转向选择性外部状态访问，从而降低运行时开销。这属于"减少外部计算/存储开销"层面的策略优化，而非模型参数压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及基础模型为Qwen3-8B（8B参数规模，对星上部署而言偏大）。"cost-aware"策略和harness annealing可降低运行时外部调用开销，但未给出量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ALFWorld（交互式家用任务环境）。指标：success rate（成功率96.9%）。摘要未说明其他指标。

Experiments: 在ALFWorld上以Qwen3-8B为基座，EvoHarness-RL达到96.9%成功率。揭示两个关键动态：harness annealing（训练将重复harness使用模式内化进模型，从频繁调用转向选择性外部访问）和harness evolution（progress更新与experience整合将harness精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基底）。摘要未给出与baseline的详细对比表。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于LLM agent领域。2. 摘要未说明模型压缩/轻量化具体方法及部署指标。3. 仅在ALFWorld单一环境验证，泛化性未知。4. 8B模型规模较大，未讨论边缘/星上部署可行性。5. 未提供FLOPs、延迟、能耗等量化数据。

Ideas for my research: 1. "cost-aware"思想可迁移：在星上目标检测中，可学习"何时需要调用高精度/重模型、何时用轻量模型或跳过冗余帧"的自适应策略，类似选择性外部状态访问，降低在轨计算开销。2. "harness annealing"（将重复模式内化进模型）可类比于知识蒸馏/特征复用：将重复检测模式固化到轻量网络中，减少对重模型的依赖。3. "harness evolution"（将状态精炼为紧凑、任务自适应基底）可类比于特征压缩/通道剪枝，将冗余中间表示压缩为紧凑特征。4. 整体属于概念层面的隐喻借鉴，非直接可复现方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [189] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [190] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KV-Skill，一种将任务知识编码为外部因子化算子、冻结语言模型通过轻量接口读取的方法，支持从文本或经验中获取知识并独立部署。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的知识存储与参数高效微调方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS/边缘部署）均无直接或间接关系。属于泛自然语言处理/大模型方向，与本研究课题弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型的知识存储与参数高效微调（PEFT）。应用场景：自然语言处理（NLP）基准测试（LiveMath等）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: KV-Skill：外部因子化算子（external factorized operators），冻结的backbone语言模型通过轻量接口读取。包含两条路径：Registration（文本技能→文本派生算子+共享接口训练）和Reward learning（从任务结果直接学习紧凑潜在算子）。

Lightweight method: 论文涉及参数高效微调（PEFT），但并非传统意义上的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。其"轻量接口"和"外部算子"设计旨在实现知识模块化与可插拔，而非降低模型计算量或参数量用于边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。论文关注的是知识存储与加载的模块化，而非计算效率。

Datasets and metrics: 10个基准测试（ten benchmarks），4个backbone（来自3个模型家族）。指标：准确率（accuracy）。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath上，Registration达到77.2准确率，对比源文本技能23.4、SkillOpt 52.0、SoftSkill 64.5。在8个匹配设置中，KV-Skill在7个上优于soft prefixes、prefix tuning和LoRA。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何视觉任务；3. 未提供计算效率指标；4. 方法设计针对NLP场景，难以直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。如果强行关联，其"外部算子+冻结backbone"的思想或许可启发遥感基础模型的知识注入方式，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心目标（降低计算量、参数量、延迟以适应星上资源约束）无直接关系。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [191] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Align-RAG，一种无需训练的对齐方法，通过闭式幅度缩放和相位平移使冻结的时间序列基础模型能够动态利用检索到的上下文进行预测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是时间序列预测（Time Series Forecasting），属于时序数据建模领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱数据，也不涉及目标检测或模型压缩。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列预测（Time Series Forecasting）；应用场景：通用时序预测（非遥感）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于冻结的Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs)，如Chronos-Bolt等。核心模块是闭式幅度缩放（closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling）和整数滞后相位平移（integer-lag phase shift），无额外可学习参数。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。虽然方法本身是training-free（无需训练），但这与模型轻量化/压缩无关，而是针对检索增强预测的适配策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。方法无需训练，但推理时需检索和对齐操作，计算开销未报告。

Datasets and metrics: 7个标准时序预测数据集；评价指标：MSE（均方误差）。

Experiments: 在Chronos-Bolt上所有7个数据集平均降低MSE 3.75%；在4种不同架构的冻结TSFM上零样本MSE提升2.5%~13.7%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。方法针对时间序列预测任务设计，不适用于图像目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但"training-free对齐"的思想有一定启发：在遥感在轨场景中，如果需要对预训练模型进行域适应（如不同传感器、不同分辨率），可探索无需额外训练的闭式对齐方法，避免在轨微调带来的计算开销。但该论文本身不涉及目标检测或遥感图像，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [192] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [193] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAUGE轻量级反事实门控框架，用于不完整多模态分类中的细粒度证据调控。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦多模态分类中的模态缺失问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：不完整多模态分类；应用场景：通用多模态分类（如音频-视觉等）；传感器类型：未限定；目标类别：未限定。摘要未说明遥感或卫星相关场景。

Model architecture: GAUGE框架：包含冻结的插补器（frozen imputer）、泰勒证据分数（Taylor evidence scores）计算模块、连续门控（continuous gates）机制，以注意力logit偏置（attention-logit biases）形式实现单元级证据调制，不改变骨干网络架构。

Lightweight method: 论文标题自称"lightweight"，但摘要中未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟等具体轻量化指标。轻量性体现在：单次前向-反向传播即可计算所有反事实分数，无需对每个单元显式干预。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。未讨论星上或边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 6个基准数据集（benchmarks），具体名称摘要未说明；评价指标未明确列出。

Experiments: 在6个基准数据集上，GAUGE在多种不完整输入设置下优于强基线方法。此外进行了泰勒余项理论分析，验证一阶近似的误差界。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 轻量化仅停留在"单次前向-反向传播"的算法效率层面，缺乏具体部署指标；3. 仅针对多模态分类任务，不涉及检测任务；4. 未讨论在轨或边缘部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：泰勒展开近似反事实效应的思路可用于轻量化设计中的重要性估计；连续门控机制不改变骨干网络架构的特性对模型压缩后保持结构兼容性有参考价值。但整体与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联度低。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification. GAUGE first imputes missing modalities with a frozen imputer and encodes observed and recovered inputs uniformly as fine-grained evidence units. Rather than intervening on each unit explicitly, GAUGE scores the counterfactual effect of replacing every unit with a reference representation through prediction-aware Taylor evidence scores, all obtained in a single forward-backward pass. These scores are mapped to continuous gates, which are converted into additive attention-logit biases for unit-wise evidence modulation without altering the backbone architecture. Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that GAUGE outperforms strong baselines across diverse incomplete-input settings. Furthermore, a Taylor remainder theoretical analysis characterizes the error of the first-order approximation relative to the exact counterfactual effect, establishing GAUGE as a principled and scalable framework for fine-grained evidence control under modality incompleteness.

</details>


### [194] [Reasoning Errors Have a Region and a Direction in the Residual-Stream Trajectory of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05660)
*Hamed Damirchi,Ignacio Meza De la Jara,Damith Ranasinghe,Yuhang Liu,Javen Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种三流检测器，通过结合残差流位移（motion）与受限的位置状态（region + direction）来区分语言模型推理的正确性，在推理基准上提升12-21%的选择准确率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。它研究的是大语言模型（LLM）推理正确性检测问题，属于自然语言处理/可解释性领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或轻量化部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语言模型推理正确性检测（reasoning validity detection）；场景：LLM推理验证；传感器/数据类型：文本（语言模型内部表征）；目标类别：正确推理 vs 错误推理。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 三流检测器（three-stream detector）：(1) Motion stream：基于残差流位移（residual-stream displacement）；(2) Region stream：基于向量量化（vector quantization）的粗粒度区域读取器；(3) Direction stream：基于归一化多层状态的细粒度方向读取器。整体设计用于从语言模型内部表征中提取推理正确性信号。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。文中提到vector quantization，但这是用于特征编码而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向LLM推理验证，非边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：推理基准（reasoning benchmarks，训练时未见过的）；额外测试了事实补全（factual completion）和事实验证（fact verification）任务。指标：选择准确率（selection accuracy）。

Experiments: 在未见过的推理基准上，相比仅用位移（displacement-only）的SOTA方法提升12%准确率，相比单层探测（single-layer probing）基线提升21%。在事实补全和事实验证任务上也优于对比的所有检测器。消融实验表明motion、region、direction三个信号互补。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或部署信息。方法针对语言模型内部表征设计，无法直接迁移到视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的概念启发是"state-conditioned motion"思路——即结合特征变化（motion）与当前状态（state）来增强判别能力，但这是NLP内部表征分析的方法，与遥感目标检测的轻量化部署问题相距甚远。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly used for tasks that require verifiable reasoning, reliably distinguishing sound reasoning from flawed reasoning has become an important practical problem. Recent trajectory-based methods seek this signal in layerwise residual-stream displacements, which capture how representations change while attenuating some stable, token-specific information. However, displacement omits the state from which an update originates, whereas restoring the full state risks reintroducing shortcut-prone information. We identify this trade-off and propose a three-stream detector that combines motion with two restricted views of location. A coarse region reader based on vector quantization and a fine direction reader over normalized multi-layer states. This design restores enough state context to interpret the motion without returning to full-state probing. On reasoning benchmarks unseen during training, our method improves selection accuracy by up to 12% over the displacement-only state of the art and 21% over single-layer probing baselines. Although trained only on reasoning benchmarks, it also reads factual completion and fact verification, ahead of every detector we compare against, which places the signal on correctness rather than on a kind of reasoning. Ablations further show that motion, region, and direction provide complementary signals. These results suggest that reasoning validity is better read from state-conditioned motion than from either static states or decontextualized trajectories alone.

</details>


### [195] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于势流的通用 p-代价最优传输框架 PMOT，通过自诱导匹配损失训练 CNF 速度场，实现 p-最优传输映射的精确恢复。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛机器学习/最优传输理论方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关联。它既不是遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及模型压缩或部署。仅作为泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，对分布匹配类问题可能有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于纯最优传输理论方法，无部署信息，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：通用 p-代价最优传输（optimal transport）与连续归一化流（CNF）密度建模。场景：合成基准、高维表格数据密度建模、基于 MMD 的颜色变换。传感器类型：无（非遥感）。目标类别：无（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: PMOT 将 CNF 速度场参数化为广义 Benamou–Brenier 形式下的标量势（scalar potential），通过自诱导匹配损失沿直线桥（straight bridges）训练势梯度，并允许灵活的终端分布匹配。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等任何模型压缩或加速方法，也未讨论轻量化网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息，也未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 合成基准（synthetic benchmarks）、高维表格数据（high-dimensional tabular data）、MMD 颜色变换实验。评价指标：与 p-匹配 OT 参考的一致性、似然（likelihood）、MMD。摘要未给出具体数据集名称和数值指标。

Experiments: 主要结果：在合成基准上，PMOT 学到的 p-特定映射与对应 p-匹配 OT 参考一致；在高维表格数据上作为基于似然的密度模型具有竞争力；MMD 颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配。摘要未给出消融实验和定量对比细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断：论文聚焦理论性质（零损失精确性、Benamou–Brenier 最优性系统），未涉及遥感数据、目标检测任务、模型压缩或部署，且未报告计算开销与推理效率。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限但存在间接可能：最优传输/分布匹配思想可用于遥感域适应（如不同传感器、不同时相影像间的特征对齐），或用于知识蒸馏中教师-学生特征分布对齐。但论文本身未涉及轻量化与检测，需自行迁移。若关注遥感小样本/域偏移下的特征分布匹配，可参考其终端分布匹配与势流建模思路。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [196] [When Does Consensus Mean Correctness? Measuring the Agreement-Accuracy Coupling with Semantics-Preserving Re-Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05670)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RENDEQ，一种基于科学图表程序化重绘生成"渲染等价集"的方法，用于直接测量视觉语言模型（VLM）在扰动输入下的一致性（agreement）与正确性（correctness）之间的耦合关系，并发现一致性作为无标签可靠性信号和自训练目标存在阈值依赖与反直觉失效。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法研究，聚焦视觉语言模型（VLM）在科学图表上的可靠性评估与自训练，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或在轨智能。其核心对象是"科学图表（scientific figures）"而非遥感影像，任务也不是目标检测。唯一可借鉴的是"一致性（agreement）作为无标签可靠性信号"这一方法论思想，但与本课题的遥感目标检测与轻量化方向关联很弱。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关联，属于泛视觉语言模型可靠性研究。仅对"无标签一致性信号"这一方法论有微弱参考价值，且其反直觉结论提示该方法在目标场景可能失效。不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视觉语言模型（VLM）在扰动输入下的可靠性评估与自训练（self-training）。场景：科学图表（scientific figures）的渲染等价集（render-equivalence sets）。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。研究对象为三个开源权重视觉语言模型（three open-weight VLMs），并采用三个独立实例化（three independent instantiations）进行交叉验证。核心组件是 RENDEQ 生成器，用于从数据程序化绘制图表并重绘生成语义等价图像。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等任何模型压缩或加速技术。论文关注的是可靠性评估与自训练目标设计，而非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。论文不涉及在轨或边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：基于科学图表程序化生成的数据（RENDEQ 生成的渲染等价集），非遥感数据集。指标：一致性（agreement）与正确性（correctness）的耦合程度、准确率（accuracy）、可靠性（reliability）、平均 token 对数概率（mean token log-probability）作为对照基线。实验在三个开源 VLM 上进行，每个发现用三个独立实例化验证。

Experiments: 主要结果：1) 重绘（re-rendering）在准确率和可靠性上均优于重采样（resampling）；2) 一致性（agreement）在三个模型中的两个上优于基于证据的基线（mean token log-probability），在第三个上持平；3) 中间一个 buggy 复现被追溯到渲染管线故障；4) 差异主要集中在一个风格因子——绘图库（plotting library），其影响是次大因子的两倍多，比噪声底高一个数量级；5) 在模型自身跨渲染一致性上微调（fine-tuning）出现反转：五次复现中准确率全部下降，与自然图像上的已发表结果符号相反；6) 一致性仅在错误扩散程度高于某阈值时才认证正确性，而奖励一致性的目标恰好破坏了这种扩散性。

Limitations: 摘要未说明显式局限性，但可推断：1) 研究对象是科学图表而非遥感影像，结论的迁移性存疑；2) 一致性作为可靠性信号存在阈值依赖，且作为自训练目标会破坏错误扩散性导致准确率下降，说明该方法在目标场景中可能失效；3) 未提供任何部署相关指标；4) 与遥感目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 尽管弱相关，仍有两点方法论可借鉴：1) "一致性（agreement）作为无标签可靠性信号"的思想可用于遥感在轨场景中无标注数据的伪标签筛选——例如在自训练/半监督目标检测中，用多扰动一致性筛选高置信伪标签，但需注意论文揭示的"一致性破坏错误扩散性导致准确率下降"的反直觉风险，提示在遥感小目标检测中不能盲目用一致性作为自训练目标；2) "渲染/扰动管线故障会导致复现反转"的教训提醒在遥感数据增强或仿真数据生成中，需严格验证数据管线的一致性。但整体上该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: A model's agreement across perturbed inputs is used both as a label-free reliability signal and as a self-training target, on the premise that agreement tracks correctness. That coupling is rarely measured directly: natural-image perturbations preserve meaning only by assumption, and no exact answer key localizes errors. Scientific figures remove both obstacles, a figure is drawn from data by a program, so redrawing it yields images that are semantically equivalent by construction and share a programmatically exact answer. We build RENDEQ, a generator of such render-equivalence sets, and measure the coupling on three open-weight VLMs, checking every finding across three independent instantiations. Re-rendering beats resampling on both accuracy and reliability. Agreement beats an evidence-carrying baseline, mean token log-probability, on two of three models and ties on the third, reversing an intermediate, buggy replication traced to a rendering-pipeline failure. The dispersion behind this is concentrated in one style factor, the plotting library, more than double the next-largest factor and an order of magnitude above the noise floor. Fine-tuning on the model's own cross-render consensus inverts: accuracy falls in every one of five replication runs, the opposite sign to published results on natural images. Agreement certifies correctness only above a threshold set by how diffuse a model's errors are, and an objective that rewards agreement destroys exactly that diffuseness.

</details>

*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Abstract: Label-free reliability for vision-language models rests on invariance: perturb the input and a faithful reader's answer should not change. This has a known blind spot, a systematic misreading survives the perturbation and gets certified wrong, which we show is computable, not just real: an error is invisible to an edit exactly when the two commute, so the errors a suite cannot reach form its joint centralizer, a set that shrinks as edits are added and can be written down rather than guessed at. We act on the complementary relation, equivariance: edit a figure's data and the correct answer must change by a computable amount. Two matched edits are provably complete for affine reading errors; no suite of swap edits is complete for label permutations, and cyclic relabeling closes most of that gap. We instantiate the theory as the Equivariance-Consistency Score, a label-free, training-free detector, and release REND-EQUIV, pairing matched invariance and equivariance sets over identical data. The predicted ordering holds across three models and a hand-labeled population immune to the one circularity in how it is selected; a second invariance-family method confirms the blind spot belongs to the relation, not to any implementation; and cyclic relabeling delivers its predicted gain on a matched real sample. The same characterization explains a reported inversion of this ordering in the classifier metamorphic-testing literature: detectability is a joint property of the relation and the fault class, never of the relation alone.

</details>


### [198] [SEAM: Global consistency beyond local accuracy in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05702)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEAM框架，用于检验科学机器学习模型局部解释能否组装成全局一致的科学解释，通过层状结构定位不一致性并归因到特定通道和重叠区域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于科学机器学习中的解释一致性验证（explanation consistency），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化任何一方面。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感、卫星、目标检测相关任务。应用场景为科学机器学习中的解释一致性审计，涉及合成偏微分方程系统和Fourier神经算子（FNO）的分布外监测。

Model architecture: SEAM框架包含解释层（explanation-sheaf）结构，每个区域由状态（state）、闭包（closure）、观测通道（observation channels）和可选的合约元数据（contract metadata）表示，通过重叠比较和通道解析的障碍（channel-resolved obstruction）定位不一致性。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 19个实验涉及合成偏微分方程系统（synthetic PDE systems）和分布外Fourier神经算子（FNO）监测。摘要未说明标准目标检测数据集或遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在19个实验中，SEAM能够检测到不兼容的解释，即使局部预测准确，也能将失败归因到特定通道和重叠区域。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测和轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文未涉及任何视觉检测任务、遥感场景或模型压缩加速技术。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，几乎无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：SEAM的"局部-全局一致性审计"思路或许可类比于在轨检测模型在多个轨道/时相/传感器下的预测一致性验证，但该方向与本课题核心目标（轻量化检测）距离较远。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning commonly validates models at the level of a subdomain, a benchmark split, or an explanation for one prediction. Yet such local checks cannot establish whether the resulting explanations can be assembled into one globally admissible explanation. We introduce Scientific Explanation-Admissibility Machines (SEAM), a generator-agnostic framework that makes this local-to-global consistency question computable across regions, sensors, regimes, and model components. The finite explanation-sheaf instantiation SEAM-$Ω$ represents each region by a structured explanation with state, closure, and observation channels together with optional contract metadata; compares neighboring explanations on their overlaps; and converts disagreement into a channel-resolved obstruction. This obstruction locates inconsistency and tests competing declared accounts by restricting each repair to the revisions that one account permits. Exact feasibility refutes or retains an account; when exact repair is unavailable, residual-aware regularized records provide a separately labeled empirical attribution. The framework also separates inconsistency from non-identifiability and monitors learned generators under distribution shift. We establish theorems for minimum-cost intervention and conservation-contract detectability, together with companion results for identifiability and closure recoverability. Across nineteen experiments involving synthetic partial differential equation systems and out-of-distribution Fourier neural operator (FNO) monitoring, SEAM detects incompatible explanations even when local predictions are accurate, and attributes failures to specific channels and overlaps. SEAM adds a global explanation-consistency audit to existing solvers and learning models, testing whether their local explanations form a coherent scientific account.

</details>


### [199] [Spectral Aliasing Pretext: A novel task for Self-Supervised fault diagnosis in rotating machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05705)

Abstract: Deep learning is a new way for machinery fault diagnosis but requires extensive labeled data, a scarce resource in industrial settings. We propose Spectral Aliasing Pretext (SAP), a self-supervised learning method that pretrains models on unlabeled vibration data by exploiting spectral aliasing. We deliberately undersample signals to create folded spectrum, then train a Transformer to reconstruct the original unfolded spectrum. This pretext task forces the model to learn frequency-domain invariants characteristic of mechanical faults, without potentially destructive augmentations. Experiments on the CWRU dataset show that SAP learns stable and highly discriminative representations. In a linear probing setting, SAP quickly achieves very high classification performance with only a small fraction of labeled data and low variance. In contrast, full fine-tuning, including fully supervised training, does not lead to more stable or better results. Overall, these findings suggest that SAP combined with linear probing can be more effective and reliable than fully supervised training for fault diagnosis with limited labeled data.

</details>


### [200] [CircuitSteer: Geometrically Aligned Multi-Layer Steering via Sparse Autoencoder Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05732)
*Mehrshad Saadatinia,Parsa Razmara,Ardalan Aryashad,Ali Abbasi,Seyedarmin Azizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CircuitSteer框架，利用稀疏自编码器(SAE)识别多层语义电路，通过多点干预实现大语言模型行为控制，相比单层固定干预方法更鲁棒有效。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的行为对齐与可控性，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。属于泛机器学习/自然语言处理方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM行为控制/对齐（toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, refusal等）。应用场景：AI安全与对齐。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Sparse Autoencoder (SAE)的特征流电路构建，多层子电路识别与多点干预。涉及LLM内部表示分析。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 评估任务：toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, refusal。模型家族：两个模型家族。指标：fluency-preserving（流畅性保持）、coverage（覆盖度）。

Experiments: 与Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA)等基线方法对比，CircuitSteer是唯一在所有任务上保持流畅性的方法，在sycophancy和refusal等复杂行为上其他方法完全失败。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM对齐领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge for AI alignment. Existing steering methods, such as Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), typically rely on fixed single-layer interventions derived from aggregate activation differences. These methods impose a single intervention across semantically diverse inputs and often fail to sustain consistent behavioral changes across layers, limiting the effectiveness of the steering. In this work, we introduce CircuitSteer, a novel framework that leverages Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to identify and manipulate coherent semantic circuits distributed across multiple layers. By constructing a feature flow circuit based on feature co-activation and the geometric alignment of decoder directions, we isolate the specific multi-layer subcircuits responsible for a target behavior. We then synthesize dense steering vectors from these sparse features and apply multi-point interventions to guide the model's internal semantic trajectory. We evaluate CircuitSteer using contrastive examples across a diverse set of tasks, including toxicity, emotion-intensity, sycophancy, and refusal, spanning two model families. Across all models and datasets, CircuitSteer is the only method to consistently produce fluency-preserving interventions; competing methods either sacrifice text quality or lack coverage, failing entirely on complex behaviors like sycophancy and refusal. These results demonstrate that multi-layer circuit steering, enabled by enforcing geometric alignment among selected features, yields strictly more robust and effective behavioral control than static single-point interventions. Code is available at https://github.com/mehrshad-sdtn/CircuitSteer.

</details>


### [201] [Multivariate Time Series Forecasting needs Cross Variable Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05742)
*Kuiye Ding,Yifan Hu,Hanchen Wang,Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting presents unique challenges because future variables often co-evolve under shared system dynamics. While existing studies mainly focus on cross-variable dependencies in historical observations, dependencies among future values are much less explored. Specifically, modern forecasting models largely follow the Direct Forecasting (DF) paradigm, generating multi-step forecasts with point-wise objectives that do not explicitly constrain cross-variable structure. In this work, we show that the DF objective is mismatched in the presence of cross-variable and lagged dependencies, revealing an objective gap. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{C}ross-\textbf{V}ariable \textbf{Loss} (CvLoss), a plug-in structural regularizer that constrains forecast residuals on a cross-variable graph. CvLoss penalizes inconsistent edge-wise residual differences over forecast patches, encouraging consistency across both synchronous and asynchronous interactions. Our experiments show that CvLoss consistently improves competitive forecasting models, outperforms representative learning objectives, and is compatible with a variety of forecasting backbones.

</details>


### [202] [Equipment-centric workpiece localization in near real-time using deep learning-based vision and event-driven finite state machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05744)
*Dohyeon Kong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多静态2D摄像头的设备中心框架，通过关键点引导注意力机制的3D CNN识别抓取/释放活动，结合有限状态机推断热锻过程中工件位置，实现连续定位。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文面向工业热锻场景的工件定位，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：工业热锻场景中工件连续定位与活动识别；场景：热锻工厂；传感器：多静态2D摄像头；目标：工件位置推断、抓取/释放活动识别。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 3D CNN + 关键点引导注意力机制（keypoint-guided attention），结合事件驱动有限状态机（event-driven finite state machines）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。系统延迟为21秒，远不满足实时性要求，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：在运营热锻工厂采集的数据（非公开遥感数据集）；评价指标：事件检测准确率（100% within 33s tolerance）、平均定位误差（317.8 mm）、平均系统延迟（21秒）。

Experiments: 在真实热锻工厂中评估，事件检测准确率100%（33秒容忍窗口），平均定位误差317.8 mm，平均延迟21秒。无遥感相关对比实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或遥感检测方法。定位误差大（317.8 mm）、延迟高（21秒），不适用于遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于工业视觉定位领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交叉。

Abstract: Continuous workpiece localization is essential for traceability and process coordination in hot forging, but direct tracking is unreliable because of extreme temperatures, surface degradation, and irregular routing. This study presents an equipment-centric framework that infers workpiece locations from handling equipment observed by multiple static 2D cameras. The framework estimates floorplan-space 3D equipment coordinates and recognizes grasp and release activities. Event-driven finite state machines validate these activities as discrete handling events and continuously update workpiece states and locations. A keypoint-guided attention mechanism integrated into a 3D convolutional neural network improves activity recognition by focusing on functionally relevant equipment regions. Evaluation in an operational hot forging factory achieved 100\% event detection accuracy within a 33-second tolerance window, a mean localization error of 317.8 mm, and a mean system latency of 21 seconds. The framework connects vision-based perception with interpretable event-driven reasoning and supports visualization of workpiece transfers and quantitative analysis of equipment operations.

</details>


### [203] [Accelerating nanodrug development in continuous flow systems using informed prediction models based on low-cost surrogate nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05761)
*Kai Dahms,Eilien Heinrich,Jochen Schmid,Michael Bortz,Iryna Savych,Regina Bleul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed
