Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: The development of nanotherapeutics often involves extensive empirical optimization due to the sensitivity of nanoparticle properties, such as size and polydispersity index (PDI), to minor changes in process parameters. Factors like formulation concentration, flow rates, and mixing ratios can significantly influence clinical efficacy and therapeutic outcomes. The absence of predictive mathematical frameworks has made iterative experimental screening necessary, increasing both costs and development time.
  This study introduces and validates a predictive modeling approach based on shape constraints, aiming to enhance the estimation of nanoparticle characteristics across various process conditions. Using controlled microfluidic methods, liposomes and lipid nanoparticles were systematically prepared under varying lipid concentrations, flow rates, and aqueous-to-organic mixing ratios.
  The shape-constrained model, informed by both experimental data and expert knowledge, was subsequently validated for a pharmaceutical application using minimal empirical data. Results reveal that shape-constrained modeling facilitates accurate prediction of nanoparticle size and dispersity, reducing the need for extensive experimental workflows. This framework supports rational and efficient process development for manufacturing nanomedicine systems.

</details>


### [204] [Neuro-Symbolic Closed-Loop Control of Laser Powder Bed Fusion with an In-Loop Ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05773)
*Gisuk Hong,Jaebong Cho,Hyunbo Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）增材制造工艺的几何条件化、神经符号闭环控制架构，将本体论符号推理与统计学习耦合，通过约束感知预测控制器设定扫描目标，以消除悬垂熔渣缺陷。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1分）。论文主题为激光粉末床熔融（laser powder bed fusion）增材制造工艺控制，属于制造/材料加工领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及任何目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测或遥感场景理解。也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署技术。唯一可借鉴的是其"神经符号闭环控制"与"约束感知预测控制"的架构思想，但与应用场景和研究方向均不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为增材制造工艺控制，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何模型压缩/部署技术，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：增材制造（laser powder bed fusion, LPBF）工艺过程控制与缺陷抑制。应用场景：金属3D打印质量控制，具体为悬垂熔渣（overhang dross）与熔池深度约束。传感器类型：工艺过程信号（可观测的熔池宽度等），非遥感传感器。目标类别：工艺缺陷（dross、lack-of-fusion），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 神经符号闭环架构（neuro-symbolic closed-loop architecture）：本体论（ontology）运行在控制回路内，将符号推理（description-logic reasoner）与统计学习（Gaussian process）耦合，为约束感知预测控制器（constraint-aware predictive controller）设定目标。核心模块包括：本体论链接过程目标/约束与可观测信号、描述逻辑推理器将目标转换为每个扫描的参考值和边界、高斯过程提供几何/功率相关的深度-宽度比及其校准不确定性、每扫描路径上仅保留一个小型二次规划（quadratic program）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。其"仅在几何上下文变化时运行推理、否则保留单个小型二次规划"的机制属于计算效率优化思想，但并非面向深度学习模型的轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。仅提及"仅在几何上下文变化时运行推理"以降低计算开销，但无量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Eagar-Tsai代理模型（surrogate），校准到NIST AM-Bench基准（针对IN625镍基合金）。评价指标：dross消除情况、lack-of-fusion残余、双评分（dual scoring）下的缺陷水平、对plant mismatch的退化表现、跨合金/约束的重定向能力。无遥感数据集或目标检测指标。

Experiments: 实验验证：相比几何盲控制器（geometry-blind controller）消除了dross；在双评分下将dross保持为零，仅残留少量lack-of-fusion；在故意plant mismatch下优雅退化；可通过编辑本体数据而非代码重定向到新合金和约束。主要结论为架构可行性已建立，比率的实验校准是下一步工作。

Limitations: 与本研究方向基本无关，主要局限在于：1) 应用场景为增材制造而非遥感/卫星；2) 不涉及任何目标检测任务；3) 不涉及任何模型轻量化/部署技术；4) 摘要明确说明"比率的实验校准是下一步工作"，即核心参数尚未经过真实实验验证，仅停留在代理模型层面。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景无关，但可抽象借鉴两点思想：1) "仅在上下文变化时触发推理、否则保持轻量计算"的按需计算（compute-on-demand）机制，可类比到在轨目标检测中"仅在感兴趣区域/事件触发时运行重模型，其余时间用轻量模型"的自适应推理策略；2) 神经符号耦合与约束感知控制的思想，可类比为在轨检测中结合领域先验（如几何/物理约束）与数据驱动模型，提升小目标检测的鲁棒性。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: A geometry-conditioned, neuro-symbolic closed-loop architecture is proposed for laser powder bed fusion, in which a standards-aligned ontology operates inside the control loop and couples symbolic reasoning with statistical learning to set the targets of a constraint-aware predictive controller. The ontology links the process objectives and constraints to the signals a controller can observe, and a description-logic reasoner converts them into the references and bounds enforced on each scan. The demonstrated case is overhang dross, a quality limit on the melt pool depth, which governs quality yet cannot be measured during the build, is mapped through a geometry- and power-dependent depth-to-width ratio onto a bound on the observable width, with the ratio and its calibrated uncertainty supplied by a Gaussian process. The reasoner classifies each upcoming feature and selects the active constraints-adding a lack-of-fusion floor at overhangs, a monotone guard beyond the calibrated range, and an energy-density cap where a process window is declared while running only on changes of geometric context and otherwise leaving a single small quadratic program on the per-scan path. In an Eagar-Tsai surrogate calibrated to the NIST AM-Bench benchmark for IN625, the architecture eliminates the dross produced by a geometry-blind controller, holds dross at zero with only a small residual lack-of-fusion under dual scoring, degrades gracefully under deliberate plant mismatch, and retargets to new alloys and constraints by editing ontology data rather than code. The results establish architectural feasibility, experimental calibration of the ratio is the principal next step.

</details>


### [205] [GROM: Gradient-Free Rapid One-Shot Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05783)
*Paweł Batorski,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于闭式解析解的单次机器遗忘方法GROM，通过岭回归最小二乘直接编辑权重矩阵，无需迭代优化和反向传播，实现快速且不可逆的知识移除。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的机器遗忘(machine unlearning)问题，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接或间接关系。虽然文中提到了量化攻击(quantization attack)作为评估遗忘鲁棒性的手段，但这是作为评估遗忘效果的对抗性测试，并非模型轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器遗忘(machine unlearning)；场景：大语言模型(LLM)安全与隐私保护；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的LLM，具体架构摘要未说明；方法作用于选定的权重矩阵，通过闭式解析解进行权重编辑。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。文中提及LoRA作为参数高效微调方法进行对比，以及量化攻击(quantization attack)作为评估遗忘鲁棒性的手段，但这些并非本文提出的轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。本文方法GROM的优势在于计算效率（秒级完成权重编辑，无需反向传播和迭代），但这是针对LLM遗忘任务的效率，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News, WMDP；评价指标：forgetting-utility trade-off（遗忘-效用权衡）；实验设置摘要未详细说明。

Experiments: GROM在TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News, WMDP上达到SOTA的遗忘-效用权衡；计算开销显著低于基于梯度的微调方法；能够抵抗低比特量化攻击（即量化后不会恢复被遗忘的知识）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 方法针对LLM遗忘设计，不适用于目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何遥感场景或目标检测相关实验。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：文中提到"量化攻击"可以恢复梯度方法中看似被移除的知识，这从侧面说明量化操作对模型权重信息的敏感性——在遥感目标检测模型轻量化（量化）时，需注意量化对检测精度的潜在影响。但这一信息过于间接，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has become a critical capability for safely removing specific, sensitive knowledge from large language models (LLMs). Current state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on iterative, training-time unlearning via fine-tuning. However, even when utilizing parameter-efficient dimensionality reduction techniques like LoRA, gradient-based optimization remains computationally expensive and lacks explicit analytical formulations. It can also leave the targeted knowledge merely hidden rather than removed, to the point that simply quantizing the unlearned model restores much of what it was supposed to have erased. To resolve this, we propose a novel one-shot unlearning approach, abandoning iterative optimization in favor of a direct, exact analytical solution. We frame the unlearning process as a ridge-regularized least-squares optimization problem, deriving a closed-form additive update for targeted weight matrices. This update forces the selected layer to suppress unwanted content while strictly preserving its behavior on retained data. Computed from gradient-free forward passes alone, with no backpropagation and no iteration to convergence, GROM applies the weight edit in mere seconds, which makes it orders of magnitude faster than traditional fine-tuning. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that GROM achieves state-of-the-art forgetting-utility trade-offs on TOFU-5%, TOFU-10%, MUSE-Books, MUSE-News and WMDP, significantly reducing computational overhead without sacrificing overall model performance. Because the update removes the targeted content from the weights instead of masking it, GROM also withstands the low-bit quantization attack that recovers much of the content a gradient-based baseline had appeared to forget. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/GROM.

</details>


### [206] [Predicting Task Difficulty Without Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05797)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究如何在不执行仿真rollout的情况下，从任务描述中预先估计智能体任务难度（ex ante difficulty prediction），并跨17个agentic benchmark验证token级熵等预测信号，用于校准评测基准和构建渐进式训练课程。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文主题是LLM/智能体（agent）任务的难度预测，属于泛机器学习/泛AI领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及模型压缩或边缘/星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为智能体任务难度预测，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法，仅作泛读了解即可。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：智能体任务难度预测（ex ante difficulty prediction）。应用场景：coding、mathematics、machine learning、web navigation、function calling等17个agentic benchmark。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及使用token-level entropy作为预测信号，以及AUC作为评估指标，未给出具体网络结构或模型设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：17个agentic benchmarks，涵盖coding、mathematics、machine learning、web navigation、function calling等领域。评价指标：AUC（并指出AUC可能掩盖较差的难度估计）、token-level entropy作为预测信号、expected与observed difficulty之间的残差。

Experiments: 摘要仅概述性说明：证明AUC会掩盖较差的难度估计，识别token-level entropy为有用预测信号，并展示expected与observed difficulty残差可暴露环境缺陷（如contamination和infeasibility）。未给出具体数值结果或对比方法细节。

Limitations: 与本研究方向基本无关。摘要聚焦于智能体任务难度预测，属于LLM/agent评测领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交集。摘要未说明具体模型架构、实验数值、部署信息等。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。唯一可能间接相关的点是：其"expected vs observed difficulty残差可暴露数据缺陷（contamination）"的思想，可类比到遥感目标检测中检测训练数据质量/标注噪声的排查；以及"token-level entropy作为预测信号"的思路，或可迁移到遥感小目标检测中衡量特征不确定性。但整体关联度很低，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Task difficulty dictates an agent's likelihood of success, and estimating it without rollouts means forecasting this directly from a task description before executing costly simulations in stateful environments. Reliable estimates would therefore allow environment designers to calibrate evaluation benchmarks and construct progressive training curricula. This becomes increasingly important as agents move into long-horizon domains, where empirical trial-and-error is a severe computational bottleneck. Prior work on early prediction is limited to static tasks or isolated coding environments, often relying on narrow features and inaccurate evaluation metrics. We study \textit{ex ante} difficulty prediction across 17 agentic benchmarks spanning coding, mathematics, machine learning, web navigation, function calling, and other domains. We show that AUC can mask poor difficulty estimates, identify token-level entropy as a useful predictive signal, and show how residuals between expected and observed difficulty can expose hidden environment flaws such as contamination and infeasibility.

</details>


### [207] [Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Reinforcement Learning for Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05989)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OG-SPR算法，通过结合潜在空间自预测和观测空间预测的双重辅助目标，提升视觉强化学习的样本效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视觉强化学习（visual RL）连续控制；应用场景：DeepMind Control Suite中的28个视觉控制任务（如dog, humanoid）；传感器类型：仿真环境像素输入；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: OG-SPR框架：基于model-free visual RL，包含共享表征网络 + 两个轻量适配器（lightweight adapters）分别用于多步潜在自预测和下一观测预测。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到"lightweight adapters"用于潜在自预测，但这是针对RL表征学习的轻量模块设计，并非模型压缩/剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化方法。与遥感模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DeepMind Control Suite的28个视觉控制任务；评价指标：aggregate performance（聚合性能），具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在28个视觉控制任务上，OG-SPR优于state-of-the-art的自预测和观测预测RL方法，在dog和humanoid等困难任务上提升尤为显著。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 仅在仿真环境验证，未涉及真实遥感数据；3. 未讨论模型部署效率；4. 轻量适配器仅针对RL表征学习，不适用于目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但"双重预测目标结合"的思想（潜在空间预测+观测空间预测）在遥感自监督表征学习中可能有启发，例如结合遥感图像重建和特征空间对比学习来提升小目标检测性能。不过需要大幅改造才能适配遥感目标检测场景。

Abstract: Sample-efficient policy learning from pixels is a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Recent dynamics-based representation learning methods have significantly improved the sample efficiency of model-free visual RL by learning dynamics-aware representations through auxiliary prediction performed either in latent space (self-prediction) or observation space (observation prediction). However, state-of-the-art methods from both categories still struggle on challenging visual control tasks when training data is limited. We posit that relying on either predictive objective alone may be insufficient. In contrast, observation prediction grounds learned representations in observation-level dynamics, but does not directly regularize the temporal predictability of latent representations over extended horizons. In this paper, we propose Observation-Grounded Self-Predictive Representations (OG-SPR), a model-free visual RL algorithm for continuous control that learns representations that are both temporally predictive in latent space and grounded in observation-level dynamics. OG-SPR incorporates two core auxiliary objectives: multi-step latent self-prediction and next-observation prediction. We empirically show that directly imposing latent self-prediction on the shared representation may over-constrain it and does not necessarily improve performance. To address this issue, OG-SPR introduces two lightweight adapters for latent self-prediction, allowing the shared representation to benefit from temporally predictive signals without being forced to directly satisfy the self-prediction objective. Experiments on 28 visual control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite show that OG-SPR improves aggregate performance over state-of-the-art self-predictive and observation-predictive RL methods, with particularly pronounced gains in challenging domains such as dog and humanoid.

</details>


### [208] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于 learning-to-rank 的框架，用于在 GPU 上执行张量网络收缩前选择高效的 contraction plan，通过从 GPU 测量数据训练梯度提升排序器来预测最优收缩方案。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。该论文属于量子计算/张量网络收缩领域，核心是量子算法仿真中的 contraction plan 选择优化，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。属于泛机器学习（learning-to-rank）方法，但应用场景是量子电路仿真，与本研究方向弱相关（1分）。

Reading priority: 1 分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于量子计算仿真领域，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：量子电路经典仿真中的张量网络收缩计划选择（contraction plan selection）。场景：量子算法开发与验证、GPU 计算。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 采用 learning-to-rank 框架，将每个 contraction plan 表示为从成对收缩序列中提取的结构特征，使用梯度提升排序器（gradient-boosted rankers），训练目标采用 listwise 和 pairwise 两种排序损失。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等模型压缩方法。其优化对象是张量网络收缩计划的选择，而非神经网络模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文关注 GPU 上的计算效率，涉及并行性、归约结构、内存流量、收缩几何等，但面向的是量子仿真 GPU 计算，而非星上/边缘部署。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多种电路族（diverse circuit families），包含 in-distribution 和 circuit-family-shift 测试集；还研究了 backend shift（两种 GPU 架构）。评价指标：排序决策质量（decision quality），与 random 和 MinFill 基线对比。

Experiments: 在多种电路族上评估，listwise 模型整体决策质量最强；跨 GPU 架构（backend shift）时排序保持基本稳定但非完全稳定，源训练模型在第二设备上无需重训仍保持有用决策质量。

Limitations: 性能部分依赖后端（backend dependent），跨 GPU 排序并非完全稳定；论文面向量子仿真而非遥感/目标检测，无任何遥感或轻量化相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的泛化思路：learning-to-rank 思想可用于在轨目标检测中为不同硬件/部署后端自动选择最优推理配置或轻量化策略，但需自行迁移到遥感场景。整体与课题方向关联度很低。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [209] [Evidential Rule Learning for Interpretable Classification with Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05859)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释的快速证据规则学习（FERL）方法，用于学习可解释、准确的模糊规则模型，其输出具有证据性质，支持置信度、可信度和弃权决策。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关系。论文聚焦于可解释分类中的证据规则学习，面向通用表格数据集，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可解释分类（interpretable classification），支持证据输出和弃权决策。应用场景：通用表格数据分类。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: FERL（Fast Evidential Rule Learning），基于模糊规则（fuzzy rule）的模型，输出为证据性结果（belief, plausibility, abstention），单次确定性前向传播完成，无需辅助头、留出集或重复推理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然FERL声称"fast"，但未给出具体速度指标或与星上/边缘部署相关的量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：30个表格数据集（tabular-dataset benchmark）、CUB、AwA2。评价指标：准确率（accuracy）、utility-discounted accuracy（u65/u80）、set coverage、AUROC、AUPR-Out、novel-class rejection。

Experiments: 在30个表格数据集上，FERL比SOTA规则学习器平均准确率高+2.6%；在credal分类器中取得最佳utility-discounted accuracy（u65/u80=0.80/0.83 vs 0.79/0.80），set coverage更高（0.92 vs ≤0.82）；在表格近OOD检测上匹配专用检测器（AUROC 77.7 vs 77.4）；在CUB和AwA2上概念瓶颈评估中与最强专用检测器差距在2.3 AUROC以内。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对表格数据，未涉及图像/遥感图像；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 未报告计算开销指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，可借鉴点极少。若强行关联，其"单次确定性前向传播"的设计理念（无需辅助头或重复推理）在轻量化推理效率设计上可能有启发，但方向差异过大，不建议深入参考。

Abstract: Interpretable classification often requires more than accurate predictions for real-life deployment: models should be transparent about the evidence behind their decisions and abstain when they cannot decide reliably. We introduce Fast Evidential Rule Learning (FERL), a method that learns interpretable, accurate fuzzy rule models whose outputs are evidential. Unlike post-hoc calibration, FERL's belief, plausibility, and abstention capabilities arise directly from the fuzzy memberships in a single deterministic pass, with no auxiliary head, held-out set, or repeated inference. Our theoretical analysis further shows that FERL is Lipschitz stable, which means that its evidential outputs vary smoothly with the input. Against state-of-the-art rule learners, FERL is statistically significantly more accurate across a 30 tabular-dataset benchmark ($+2.6\%$ average accuracy over the second best). Its native set predictions attain the best utility-discounted accuracy among credal classifiers ($u_{65}/u_{80}=0.80/0.83$ vs.\ $0.79/0.80$ for the naive credal classifier), at higher set coverage ($0.92$ vs.\ $\le0.82$). FERL also matches dedicated out-of-distribution detectors on tabular near-OOD detection ($77.7$ vs.\ $77.4$ AUROC for the strongest baseline). Under detector-class-disjoint concept-bottleneck evaluation, its it is within $2.3$ AUROC points of the strongest dedicated detector on both CUB and AwA2, while attaining the best AwA2 AUPR-Out ($68.3$) and novel-class rejection ($57.2$), while being able to name which attributes are anomalous.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond Feature Importance: A Comparative Analysis of Pattern Detection Methods in Cluster Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05880)
*Benjamin Connor,Anna Jurek-Loughrey,Lu Bai,Muhammad Fahim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦于聚类结果的可解释性分析，对比了随机森林、LIME和PCA三种后验分析方法在检测聚类中预定义模式的能力，发现现有方法均无法一致地检测所有模式类型，指出模式级聚类解释工具存在关键缺口。

Research relevance: 1分——与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是聚类可解释性（explainable clustering），属于无监督学习与可解释机器学习范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分——与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星相关任务。应用场景为医疗健康等高维数据分析，任务为聚类结果中的模式检测与可解释性分析，不涉及目标检测或遥感场景理解。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明目标检测模型架构。文中提及的模型为Random Forest surrogate model、LIME、PCA，均为可解释性/后验分析方法，非目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用自建合成数据集（synthetic datasets），其中注入预定义模式用于受控评估。评价指标未明确说明。

Experiments: 对比了Random Forest + permutation feature importance、LIME、PCA三种方法在合成数据集上的模式检测能力，结果显示每种方法都能恢复相关特征，但无一能一致检测所有注入的模式类型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、压缩方法或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无监督学习可解释性方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Interpreting clustering outcomes remains a fundamental challenge in data analysis, particularly in domains such as healthcare where meaningful patterns must be extracted from high-dimensional data. While numerous explainability techniques exist, they are primarily designed to assess feature importance or provide local instance-level explanations rather than to identify structured patterns present within clusters. This work presents a comparative evaluation of commonly used post-hoc analysis methods for pattern detection in clustering results. To enable controlled evaluation, we introduce a suite of synthetic datasets in which predefined patterns are systematically injected. Three widely used techniques are evaluated: a Random Forest surrogate model with permutation feature importance, LIME (Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations), and principal component analysis. Results demonstrate that although each method can successfully recover relevant features, none consistently detects all injected pattern types. These findings high- light a critical gap between existing explainability tools and the requirements of pattern-level cluster interpretation, motivating the development of dedicated pattern detection methodologies.

</details>


### [211] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FALM-PINN优化框架，通过交替Levenberg-Marquardt训练解耦表示学习与系数拟合，并引入Fourier增强基以缓解PINN在高频/多尺度/强非线性PDE求解中的谱偏差与表示-系数耦合问题。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于物理信息神经网络(PINN)求解偏微分方程(PDE)的优化算法，属于科学计算/数值方法领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于科学计算领域，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：求解偏微分方程(PDE)，包括高频、多尺度、强非线性问题。场景：科学计算/数值模拟。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: FALM-PINN框架：上层问题学习Fourier增强基(basis)以丰富潜空间高频成分；下层问题用Levenberg-Marquardt算法在该基上拟合投影系数，求解非线性最小二乘问题。对线性PDE退化为单步凸优化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法，其"轻量"体现在优化算法层面而非模型部署层面。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息，且该方向与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个挑战性高频和非线性PDE数值算例。评价指标：相对L2误差(relative L2 error)。

Experiments: 在多个高频和非线性PDE算例上，FALM-PINN的相对L2误差比SOTA基线低最多两个数量级。论文证明了两种情况下交替训练方案的全局收敛性。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。但可推断：论文仅针对PDE求解，未涉及任何视觉/遥感任务；未提供部署相关指标；未与目标检测或轻量化方法对比。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的间接思路是：其"解耦表示学习与系数拟合"的交替优化思想，在理论上或可类比于知识蒸馏中教师-学生解耦训练，或轻量化中分离特征提取与分类头的联合优化，但关联牵强，不构成直接可复现方向。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [212] [CohortHijack: Robustness of Single Cell Annotation to Companion Cell Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05900)
*Arash Vashagh,Yasmin Vashagh*

Abstract: Many single-cell annotation tools refine an initial cell label using nearby cells or cluster-level voting. We study whether this refinement can be manipulated without changing the target cell. We introduce CohortHijack, a robustness audit that removes selected non-target cells from the query cohort while preserving the target expression profile, base prediction, and trained model. We evaluate random and structured removal methods, together with greedy, multi-start, and beam search, on PBMC3K and Paul15 using logistic regression and calibrated linear SVM classifiers. Structured removal was consistently stronger than random removal on Paul15. Multi-start search changed 24.33% of linear-SVM targets and 19.67% of logistic-regression targets while removing a small fraction of the cohort and keeping mean collateral changes below 0.4%. Ablations confirmed that the effect disappeared when neighborhood refinement was disabled. We also evaluated CellTypist majority voting, where independent predictions remained unchanged across all evaluations, but refined labels changed after small companion-cell removals. These findings identify query cohort composition as a target-preserving attack surface in single-cell annotation.

</details>


### [213] [BioM-JEPA: joint-embedding prediction of graph-connected gene blocks in single cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05928)
*Yuhao Wang,Zelin Zang,Yuxuan Liu,Zhen Lei,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Single-cell transcriptomes are sparse observations of coordinated biological programmes, yet most self-supervised models learn by reconstructing individual genes. Here we present BioM-JEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that instead predicts aggregate representations of graph-connected gene blocks defined by protein-association and corpus-derived coexpression evidence. A student network infers each target-block representation from the remaining genes in a cell, while a slowly updated teacher supplies the corresponding target from the full observed gene set. Under the reported extraction procedure, block-level prediction produced embeddings with higher effective rank and weaker association with detected-gene depth in the tested diagnostics than token-prediction, random-block and reconstruction controls. Across CellBench tasks, frozen BioM-JEPA embeddings retained expression, pathway and neighbourhood information and achieved the lowest aggregate perturbation-response error among the evaluated models. Representation diagnostics were also consistent with canonical pancreatic programmes and compositional relationships between genetic perturbations. Linear attention avoids constructing a quadratic gene-by-gene attention matrix; in a matched one-epoch hPancreas experiment at batch size 8, BioM-JEPA provided 5.75-fold higher fine-tuning throughput and 3.76-fold higher held-out embedding throughput than scFoundation. Together, these results support graph-connected gene blocks as useful prediction units for JEPA-style representation learning in single-cell biology.

</details>


### [214] [How Far Do Simple Transformations Translate Across Text Embedding Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05980)
*Sid Ali Hamideche,Louis Adrien Dufrene,Quentin Lampin,Guillaume Larue*

Abstract: We investigate whether simple transformations can translate representations across heterogeneous text embedding models. Understanding how independently trained models organize semantic information is an enabler for AI-to-AI latent communication without decoding into human-readable text. Focusing on lightweight translators such as linear mappings, we test the literature hypothesis of latent universality in a realistic text setting beyond simplified benchmarks. Across nine embedding models differing in architecture, pooling strategy, and training objective, we evaluate compatibility using CKA, downstream transfer, fidelity, and retrieval. Simple translators recover meaningful shared structure and support transfer for some compatible pairs, but fail sharply for others. Compatibility depends jointly on architecture, training objective, pooling, and data distribution. Overall, the results show that heterogeneous embedding spaces are not universally related by simple mappings as often suggested in some literature.

</details>


### [215] [THBKG: A Temporal Biomedical Knowledge Graph for Decision-Aligned Clinical Advancement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05982)
*Pui Chung Siu,Claudia Cabrera,Mani Mudaliar,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：预测靶点-疾病对能否从 Phase II 推进到 Phase III（药物研发决策预测）。场景：生物医学知识图谱、临床试验。传感器/目标类别：不适用（非遥感）。

Model architecture: 基于时间异质生物医学知识图谱 THBKG 的图传播（graph propagation）模型，以及路径可解释器（path-based explainer）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS 等任何模型压缩或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：THBKG，110,396 个实体、11.1M 条边、19 种关系类型，每条边带证据变化年份。评价指标：相对成功率（relative success，top ten pairs 达 4.3–4.5）、决策对齐基准（decision-aligned benchmark）、五到六倍于随机（chance）的排序性能。

Experiments: 图传播模型在决策对齐协议下优于所有直接证据参考方法；增益集中在 72.8% 无直接靶点-疾病证据的样本上，编码器仍能五到六倍于随机水平排序，通过传播中间生物学信息恢复信号。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与本研究方向无关，无遥感、目标检测、轻量化相关内容。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点有限。唯一可能有启发的是"按历史时点重建证据/时间感知图传播"的思想，若类比到遥感在轨场景，可考虑时间序列遥感影像中按历史时刻重建目标状态，但与本课题（在轨目标检测轻量化）关联很弱。

Abstract: Inadequate target--disease linkage accounts for 40--50\% of Phase~II efficacy failures, so anticipating which programmes will advance would let sponsors back the hypotheses most likely to reach patients. What a programme can be judged on is the evidence that supported its linkage \emph{when it entered the clinic}. No existing biomedical knowledge graph allows that evidence profile to be assembled as of a past date. We present the Temporal Heterogeneous Biomedical Knowledge Graph (THBKG), which describes and predicts therapeutic target--disease links through time: 110,396 entities and 11.1M edges across nineteen relation types, each edge carrying the year its evidence changed, so a pair's profile can be recovered as it stood when its own decision fell due. On this graph we define a decision-aligned benchmark that predicts, for a target--disease pair entering Phase~II, whether it advances to Phase~III on evidence datable before that decision. Graph propagation over the THBKG outranks every direct-evidence reference scored under the same decision-aligned protocol, reaching a relative success of 4.3--4.5 at the top ten pairs per therapeutic area. The gain concentrates on the 72.8\% of pairs with no direct target--disease evidence at their decision point, where a direct-edge model has nothing to read: the encoders still rank five- to sixfold above chance, recovering the signal by propagating over the intervening biology. Adapting a path-based explainer to the decision-time subgraph decomposes each prediction into the evidence landscape behind the hypothesis for explainable prediction. We release the THBKG as a continually updated substrate for studying therapeutic target hypotheses by retrospective validation.

</details>


### [216] [A Unified Risk View of Uncertainty: Posterior Risk for Disentanglement and Evaluation Beyond Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05995)
*Frieder Wizgall,Georg Tirpitz,Moritz Seiler,Kerstin Ritter,Bálint Mucsányi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于点态后验风险的不确定性统一定义，并构建了半合成基准数据集，用于直接评估认知不确定性和偶然不确定性的解耦质量。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于不确定性估计的理论定义与基准构建，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于纯机器学习/贝叶斯深度学习方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未描述具体模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 论文提出使用半合成数据集（real covariates + known generative processes）构建基准，用于直接计算oracle epistemic和aleatoric uncertainty。评价指标为不确定性解耦质量，而非检测精度或速度。

Experiments: 实验发现：准确预测并不保证可靠的不确定性解耦；不同方法在不确定性对齐质量上存在实际差异，且对数据集和建模选择敏感。

Limitations: 论文完全不涉及遥感目标检测或模型轻量化，无法直接提供相关借鉴。其不确定性估计方法若未来应用于遥感检测，可能有助于提升模型在轨部署时的可靠性评估，但摘要中未给出任何具体实现。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可借鉴的点是：在轨遥感目标检测模型部署时，不确定性估计（尤其是认知不确定性）可用于判断模型在轨遇到未见分布数据时的可靠性，但本文未给出任何轻量化或遥感场景下的具体方案。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimates are critical in safety-sensitive applications, where understanding the sources of predictive uncertainty is essential. This often requires disentangling epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty, yet these uncertainty types are not defined consistently across the literature, making it difficult to assess whether a method produces accurate uncertainty estimates. Evaluation is further complicated by the fact that ground-truth epistemic uncertainty is typically unavailable. Existing benchmarks therefore mostly rely on proxy tasks such as out-of-distribution detection, which do not provide complete ground-truth uncertainty targets and offer limited insight into the structure and quality of uncertainty estimates. We propose a unified definition of uncertainty as pointwise posterior risk, the expected loss of a predictor under the distribution of plausible ground-truth functions given the data. This view combines Bayesian uncertainty over functions with estimator-dependent deviations from the posterior mean, capturing effects such as misspecification and optimization error. This formulation constitutes the foundation of a theory-backed benchmark that enables direct computation of oracle epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty using semi-synthetic datasets with real covariates and known generative processes. By avoiding proxy evaluations, the benchmark enables fine-grained analysis of uncertainty estimates. Empirically, we find that accurate prediction does not guarantee reliable uncertainty disentanglement. The benchmark reveals practically useful differences between methods, identifying approaches with meaningful alignment to oracle uncertainty targets while exposing sensitivity to datasets and modeling choices.

</details>


### [217] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Agree with Themselves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06004)
*Christian Klötergens,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Tom Hanika*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) 的联合分布一致性，提出边际化一致性与因子分解一致性两个要求，并发现所有被评估的 TFM 在分类和回归任务上均违反这两个要求。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文研究对象是表格数据基础模型（Tabular Foundation Models），属于泛机器学习/概率建模范畴，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于泛机器学习概率建模研究，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：表格数据预测（tabular prediction）与概率联合分布一致性评估。应用场景：通用表格数据，非遥感/卫星场景。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 研究对象为 Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs)，其结构为 transformer，通过预训练先验近似贝叶斯后验预测分布，采用自回归方式将单变量预测器转换为多变量预测器（采样一个目标并加入特征）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评估覆盖分类（classification）和回归（regression）任务，跨多个数据集。评价指标为边际化一致性（marginalization consistency）与因子分解一致性（factorization consistency）。

Experiments: 主要实验发现：所有被评估的 TFM 在分类和回归任务的所有数据集上均违反边际化一致性与因子分解一致性两个要求。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体数据集、模型规模、计算开销等细节。论文聚焦于概率一致性理论分析，未涉及任何遥感或部署场景，也未提供轻量化相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的是其"一致性验证"的评估思路——即在设计轻量化遥感目标检测模型时，可考虑对模型在旋转框/小目标等不同预测分支间的一致性进行验证，但与本课题核心（遥感在轨检测+轻量化）关联很弱。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) are currently the best approach to tabular prediction problems. They are constructed as transformers that approximate the Bayesian posterior predictive distribution based on a pre-training prior. These univariate predictors can be converted into multivariate ones autoregressively by sampling one target and adding it to the features.
  However, the faithfulness of the resulting joint has not been investigated. Furthermore, TFMs cannot be evaluated against the posterior itself, at least not on real-world datasets, because the ground-truth distribution is unknown. We therefore propose asking a different question: could a model's predictions result from any joint distribution? To answer this question, we pose two requirements that any such model must satisfy. The first is marginalization consistency, which demands that marginalized conditionals are equal to directly predicted marginals. The second is factorization consistency, which demands that different factorization orders result in equal joint distributions. Every TFM that we evaluate violates both of these requirements for both classification and regression across all datasets.

</details>


### [218] [ProDVI: Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06015)
*Xinwei Liu,Junyuan Liang,Jianting Zhang,Wuhui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) is notoriously sample inefficient. One contributing factor is that RL agents are typically initialized from scratch, forcing them to acquire task-relevant knowledge through online interaction. Existing approaches obtain informative initializations through pre-collected datasets, high-fidelity simulators, or meta-learning over related tasks, but these prerequisites may be difficult to access or even unavailable. In this paper, we propose Programmatic Dynamics Priors for Value Network Initialization (ProDVI), a framework that leverages the commonsense and domain knowledge encoded in large language models to initialize RL agents without relying on these resources. Specifically, ProDVI prompts a code-generating language model to produce executable Python functions that encode coarse hypotheses about environment dynamics. These functions are then used to generate synthetic transitions. Based on these transitions, we construct an auxiliary dynamics prediction objective to pretrain the state-action encoder of the value network in an actor-critic framework. The learned representation provides dynamics-aware inductive biases before online RL begins. Importantly, the generated programs are used only for representation pretraining and are not required to faithfully simulate the target environment. While the generated programs may be inaccurate, their induced initialization can be corrected through online learning from real transitions and rewards. Experiments on OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control Suite tasks show that ProDVI can effectively improve the sample efficiency of model-free RL algorithms.

</details>


### [219] [BioKD: Selective Physiology-to-Video Knowledge Distillation via Reliability Gate for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06023)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Hongjun Liu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于生理信号到视频的知识蒸馏框架BioKD，用于视频情感识别，通过可靠性感知门控机制抑制不可靠教师监督带来的负迁移。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是视频情感识别（video-based emotion recognition），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测任务；虽然使用了知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）这一轻量化技术，但蒸馏目的是跨模态知识迁移（生理→视频），而非模型压缩或轻量化部署，且不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等轻量化核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。仅当对知识蒸馏中教师可靠性建模的通用方法感兴趣时，可作为旁路参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视频情感识别（valence/arousal recognition）；场景：情感计算、人机交互；传感器：RGB视频（学生模型输入）、生理信号（教师模型特权信息）；目标类别：情感维度（效价、唤醒度）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 教师-学生知识蒸馏框架：教师模型以生理信号为输入，学生模型以视频为输入。包含样本级可靠性感知门控机制（sample-wise reliability-aware gating mechanism）和渐进式蒸馏策略（progressive distillation strategy）。摘要未说明具体网络结构（如CNN/Transformer等）。

Lightweight method: 使用了知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation），但目的是跨模态知识迁移而非模型轻量化。蒸馏后推理时仅需视频输入，无需生理传感器，降低了部署复杂度，但未涉及参数量减少、FLOPs降低、推理加速等轻量化核心指标。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"introduces no additional inference-time overhead relative to the same video student architecture"，即推理开销与原始视频学生模型相同，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DEAP、AMIGOS；评价指标：valence/arousal识别准确率（如DEAP trial-wise arousal 68.01%，subject-wise 65.29%）；评估协议：trial-wise和subject-wise。与遥感目标检测常用数据集（DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、SSDD等）和指标（mAP、FPS等）无关。

Experiments: 在DEAP和AMIGOS上优于代表性基线方法；消融实验验证了可靠性感知门控机制和渐进式蒸馏策略的有效性；对比了熵加权策略，证明显式建模监督可靠性的重要性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 知识蒸馏方法虽可借鉴，但本文蒸馏目的是跨模态知识迁移而非模型压缩，轻量化价值有限；3. 未提供任何计算效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. 仅在情感识别数据集上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 1. 可靠性感知门控机制（reliability-aware gating）的设计思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测的知识蒸馏中——例如当教师模型（大模型）对某些困难样本（如小目标、遮挡目标）预测不可靠时，自适应降低蒸馏权重，避免负迁移；2. 渐进式蒸馏策略（progressive distillation）可迁移到遥感模型轻量化训练中，逐步增加蒸馏强度以稳定训练；3. 但整体而言，本文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化方向关联度极低，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: To address the limitations of video-based emotion recognition under ambiguous or socially masked behavioral cues, as well as the poor deployability of physiological signals, this paper proposes a reliability-aware physiology-to-video knowledge distillation framework, termed BioKD. The proposed framework leverages physiological signals as privileged information during training to guide a video-based student model in learning deep affective representations, while relying solely on non-intrusive video inputs at inference time. To cope with the high noise and instability of physiological teacher supervision caused by inter-subject variability, signal artifacts, and temporal inconsistency, BioKD incorporates a sample-wise reliability-aware gating mechanism together with a progressive distillation strategy. By adaptively regulating the strength of knowledge transfer, the framework suppresses negative transfer induced by unreliable physiological supervision and enables more stable cross-modal distillation. Experiments on DEAP and AMIGOS show that BioKD consistently outperforms representative baselines under both trial-wise and subject-wise evaluation protocols for valence and arousal recognition. For example, BioKD achieves 68.01\% on DEAP (trial-wise arousal) and 65.29\% under the more challenging subject-wise setting, demonstrating improved performance under a subject-independent evaluation setting. Further analyses show that BioKD effectively mitigates overconfident teacher errors and outperforms an entropy-only weighting strategy, confirming the importance of explicitly modeling supervision reliability. In addition, BioKD introduces no additional inference-time overhead relative to the same video student architecture and removes the need for physiological sensing and multimodal synchronization.

</details>


### [220] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AutoThread，一种基于物理信息神经算子(PINO)和排队论模型的混合自适应线程调优方法，用于加速强化学习推理中的仿真瓶颈。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无任何关联。论文聚焦于强化学习推理中的仿真端多线程调度优化，属于系统/高性能计算方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习推理中的仿真瓶颈加速；应用场景：simulation-in-the-loop决策系统；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 使用Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO)作为线程数预测器，结合有限源M/M/1排队模型进行约束和引导预测。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。论文方法属于系统层面的线程调度优化，与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：加速比(speedup)、吞吐量(throughput)、执行时间减少百分比。

Experiments: 与静态策略相比平均加速18.4%；吞吐量分别达到XGBoost和Reinforcer的1.7倍和1.8倍；相比最先进方法执行时间最多减少83.8%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文解决的是RL推理中的仿真端多线程调度问题，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心技术。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [221] [Dynamic Graph Prompting via Topology-Routed Mixed-Curvature Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06031)
*Quanxin Wang,Xuanting Xie,Bingheng Li,Xingtong Yu,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CurvPrompt，一种面向动态图的拓扑引导几何提示框架，通过曲率多样化的黎曼专家库和拓扑感知门控路由，解决预训练模型在标签稀缺下游任务中的几何欠适配问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是动态图上的少样本链接预测和节点分类任务，属于图神经网络/时序图学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：动态图上的少样本链接预测和节点分类；应用场景：时序图学习；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: CurvPrompt框架包含：(1) 曲率多样化的黎曼专家库（curvature-diverse Riemannian experts），每个专家配有一个可学习提示；(2) 拓扑感知门控（topology-aware gate），动态将每个节点-时间实例路由到稀疏专家子集，构建个性化混合曲率表示；(3) 预训练阶段使用软路由（soft routing）构建连续拓扑-几何映射，下游适应阶段转为硬Top-K路由（hard Top-K routing）和均匀权重。

Lightweight method: 论文涉及轻量级提示（lightweight prompts）和参数高效微调，但这是针对动态图任务的提示学习方法，与模型剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等遥感轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 四个基准数据集（摘要未具体命名），评价指标为少样本链接预测和节点分类性能。摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在四个基准数据集上，CurvPrompt在少样本链接预测任务上显著提升，在节点分类任务上表现强且一致。摘要未提供与具体基线方法的详细对比结果。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 不涉及任何目标检测任务；(3) 不涉及任何模型轻量化或部署技术；(4) 实验仅在图学习基准上进行，未在遥感数据上验证。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：提示学习（prompting）作为一种参数高效微调思路，在遥感基础模型微调中可能有类似应用，但本文聚焦动态图场景，与遥感目标检测的视觉特征差异巨大，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Dynamic graph prompting freezes a pre-trained temporal backbone and adapts it to label-scarce downstream tasks using lightweight prompts. However, existing methods operate within a single, fixed embedding space. In this work, we reveal that temporal shifts in local clustering and degree heterogeneity actively reorganize the edge curvature spectrum---indicating that the optimal representation geometry dynamically evolves with local topology over time. We formalize this unaddressed mismatch as geometry under-adaptation. To overcome this limitation, we propose CurvPrompt, a topology-routed geometry prompting framework for dynamic graphs. Instead of relying on a single space, CurvPrompt maintains a bank of curvature-diverse Riemannian experts, each paired with a learnable prompt. A topology-aware gate dynamically routes each node--time instance to a sparse subset of experts, constructing a personalized mixed-curvature representation. To ensure parameter efficiency and training stability under extreme label scarcity, CurvPrompt employs soft routing during pre-training to build a continuous topology--geometry mapping, and transitions to hard Top-K routing with uniform weights during downstream adaptation. Extensive experiments across four benchmark datasets show that CurvPrompt significantly advances few-shot link prediction while delivering strong, consistent performance on node classification tasks, validating the necessity of geometry-adaptive prompting.

</details>


### [222] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [223] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [224] [Is Self-Pretraining really useful to improve diagnosis in medical Time Series?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06122)

Abstract: Inspired by recent evidence that transformer architectures benefit from Self-PreTraining (SPT) on long-context benchmarks, we investigate whether similar gains extend to multimodal, multivariate, and even simple univariate medical time series. Our objective is to assess the impact of SPT on the performance and scalability of transformer-based models across diverse medical applications, particularly under limited data conditions. We evaluate transformer architectures on three representative medical time-series tasks: rehabilitation robotics (Camargo dataset), stress detection (Non-EEG Stress), and Parkinson's disease detection (Gait Parkinson's Disease). Models are trained either from scratch or through SPT using four masking-based objectives designed to promote temporal and cross-modal representation learning, and we systematically vary model depth to examine how capacity interacts with pre-training benefits. Across datasets and configurations, SPT consistently improves classification accuracy by 0-6 percentage points depending on masking strategy, dataset and architecture, with gains observed not only in multivariate settings but also when models are restricted to simple univariate inputs. The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [226] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SkillTFM，一种无需训练的表格基础模型适配系统，通过门控技能演化替代参数更新来应对分布偏移和特征语义异质性问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于表格数据（tabular data）的基础模型适配问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：表格数据基础模型（Tabular Foundation Model）的适配与泛化。应用场景：科学、工业、金融、医疗、公共服务等领域的表格数据预测与决策。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: SkillTFM系统，核心组件为可验证可扩展的技能库（skill bank），包含边界证据识别（boundary evidence identification）和门控技能演化（gated skill evolution）。不涉及CNN/Transformer等视觉模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文提出的"training-free"是指无需微调（fine-tuning）即可适配，而非模型轻量化意义上的无训练压缩。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且论文面向表格数据而非视觉/遥感模型，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：模拟边界设置（simulated boundary settings）和真实世界电价预测（real-world electricity-price forecasting）。评价指标：AUC（Area Under the Curve）。

Experiments: SkillTFM在模拟边界设置和真实电价预测任务上，AUC提升0.128-0.142，非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898。在多个TFM骨干网络上验证了有效性和泛化性。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/视觉领域，与本研究方向无直接关联。2. 仅针对表格数据，无法直接迁移到遥感图像目标检测任务。3. 未提供任何模型轻量化或部署相关的指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但"training-free适配"的思路（即在不更新参数的情况下提升模型泛化能力）在概念上可启发思考：在轨遥感目标检测模型是否可以通过某种无需重训的机制来适应不同轨道/光照/传感器条件下的分布偏移？不过该论文的具体方法（边界证据识别+门控技能演化）高度依赖表格数据的特征语义结构，难以直接迁移到图像检测任务。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [227] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对二值神经网络（BNN）的后训练早停机制：利用训练集上运行累加和的行为特征，在累加过程中尽早预测最终符号，从而跳过后续无意义的累加项，在不重训任何参数的前提下减少运算量。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛视觉/泛机器学习范畴，核心是二值神经网络（BNN）的推理加速，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱等遥感场景，也未针对目标检测任务。但其"减少累加运算、降低推理开销"的思想与轻量化/边缘部署方向有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向相关性较弱，属于泛视觉的BNN推理加速方法。但其"减少累加运算"的早停思想对低功耗星上推理有一定借鉴价值，可作为轻量化方法的补充参考，优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像分类（CIFAR-10上的VGG11）。场景：通用视觉任务，非遥感场景。传感器类型：摘要未说明（非遥感）。目标类别：摘要未说明（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 基于二值神经网络（BNN），具体为VGG11。核心思想：二值激活下点积变为符号控制的加减法，但运算量不变；利用运行累加和（running partial sum）的漂移特性，在累加过程中提前预测最终符号，实现早停（early-stopping）。

Lightweight method: 后训练早停机制（post-training early-stopping）：在训练集上刻画运行累加和的行为，据此尽早预测最终符号，跳过后续累加项。不重训任何模型参数。属于推理阶段的计算剪枝/加速方法，与剪枝、量化、蒸馏等传统压缩方法不同，是一种基于符号可预测性的运算跳过策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅给出运算量减少比例：在VGG11/CIFAR-10上，最深卷积层可移除86.6%的累加项（精度下降0.37点）；同时用于三个最深卷积层时，可减少全网络25%的算术运算（精度下降1.36点）。该机制面向低功耗、小footprint的约束部署场景，但未给出星上/边缘部署的具体可行性数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR-10。模型：VGG11。指标：累加项移除比例（%）、全网络算术运算减少比例（%）、精度下降（accuracy drop，以百分点计）。

Experiments: 在VGG11/CIFAR-10上验证：最深卷积层移除86.6%累加项，精度下降0.37点；三个最深卷积层同时使用时，减少25%全网络算术运算，精度下降1.36点。摘要未说明与其他方法的对比实验或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对二值激活网络（BNN），适用范围有限；2. 仅在CIFAR-10/VGG11上验证，未在遥感、大尺度、目标检测等场景验证；3. 未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署指标；4. 理想化权重排序（idealized ordering of weights）假设在实际硬件上可能难以实现；5. 精度下降在同时用于多个卷积层时较明显（1.36点）；6. 未涉及遥感目标检测、旋转框、小目标等任务。

Ideas for my research: 1. 该"运行累加和符号可预测性"思想可迁移到遥感目标检测骨干网络的推理加速，尤其对二值化/低比特量化的检测网络，可减少卷积累加运算；2. 可结合知识蒸馏或量化，在星上低功耗推理中进一步压缩运算；3. 早停机制可作为轻量化检测网络的后训练加速插件，与剪枝、量化正交组合；4. 需在遥感数据集（如DOTA、DIOR、SAR舰船检测）上验证其有效性，并评估对旋转框/小目标检测精度的影响；5. 需补充FLOPs、延迟、能耗等星上部署指标。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [228] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGA框架，用依存句法解析器替代人工偏好标注，通过delta-DPO和复合奖励函数优化低资源语言的语法质量，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦自然语言处理中的低资源语言语法对齐，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，完全不相关，无需阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [229] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [231] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>

Experiments: MetaboLLM在四个骨干家族上优于对应基础模型和医学适配模型，覆盖代谢组学知识、关系与描述任务，并迁移到外部公共基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在stress hyperglycemia预测（AUC 0.8616）和postmenopausal hormone-regimen分类（AUC 0.8123）上取得最高AUC，优于传统模型、替代图构建方式，以及由未适配或非检索LLM配置生成的图。模型解释产生了生物学上有意义的发现。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与遥感在轨目标检测弱相关，但可借鉴其"领域专用LLM + 结构化图表示"的范式：将领域知识（如遥感目标检测中的场景语义、目标关系）组织为结构化表示用于下游预测。此外，其"持续预训练+监督微调+检索增强"的领域适配流程，可类比为遥感检测模型在特定传感器/场景数据上的领域适配策略。但整体借鉴价值有限，因不涉及目标检测与轻量化。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [233] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [234] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>
