Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [340] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可执行事件轨迹的参数化视频基准测试方法，用于诊断视频事件计数任务中时间推理的失败模式。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用视频事件计数任务的基准测试与诊断方法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频事件计数（event counting）；应用场景：通用视频理解（bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, categorical state transitions）；传感器类型：通用视频；目标类别：事件（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，评估对象为Gemini 3.6 Flash等通用视频理解模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2,190段可控参数化视频（bouncing-ball, visual blinks, categorical state transitions）；评价指标：80%可靠性阈值下的正确计数率、事件恢复率（18.1%）、序列一致性（3.7%）、最终答案准确率等。

Experiments: 主要发现：事件表征类型决定模型能否初步访问证据；高计数-高频率场景下仅0.2%最终计数正确，事件恢复率仅18.1%；提高采样率虽将Bounce Ball准确率从19.6%提升至29.3%，但序列一致性仅3.7%；不同提示策略增益有限。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是通用视频事件计数诊断，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [341] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Nimblemind多智能体系统(nMAS)，用于心力衰竭电子健康记录(EHR)特征的自动化工程，通过证据链接和评分标准生成结构化及聚合特征，提升表型分类AUROC。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1分）。论文聚焦于临床医学领域的电子健康记录(EHR)特征工程与心力衰竭表型分类，属于医疗AI/生物医学信息学方向，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感任务；也不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等轻量化与部署技术。唯一可借鉴的是其"多智能体系统+证据链接+评分标准"的自动化pipeline思想，但与本课题方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于医疗AI/EHR特征工程方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化课题基本无关，不涉及任何遥感、目标检测或模型压缩技术，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：EHR特征工程与心力衰竭(HF)表型分类（HFrEF/HFpEF phenotyping）。应用场景：临床研究、医疗AI。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：心力衰竭患者表型。数据来源：9张EHR源表、500条模拟患者记录。

Model architecture: Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS)：证据链接、基于评分标准(rubric-grounded)的自动化特征工程pipeline，包含多智能体协作、受限LLM审计模块。生成132个结构化特征和70个rubric评分的聚合特征。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：500条模拟(dummy)患者记录，来自9张EHR源表。评价指标：held-out AUROC（HFrEF从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910）；独立LLM基于rubric对证据支持和方法学合理性的评分（达到最高分的81.5%）。

Experiments: 主要结果：加入聚合特征后，HFrEF表型分类AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910；LLM审计评分达81.5%。验证了自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。局限：仅单机构队列评估，需外部验证。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关，属于医疗AI领域。2. 评估仅限单机构模拟数据，缺乏外部验证。3. 摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度、部署资源等任何与轻量化相关的信息。4. 未涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 尽管领域不同，但可借鉴其"多智能体系统+证据链接+评分标准(rubric)"的自动化pipeline思想，类比到遥感在轨目标检测中可考虑：1) 将多智能体协作思想用于遥感小目标检测的自动化特征/模块设计；2) 用"证据链接+可审计"思路增强在轨检测模型的可解释性与可追溯性；3) 用rubric评分机制对轻量化模型（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）的精度-效率权衡进行结构化评估。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>

### [342] [A Foundational EDM2-Based Generative Model for High-Resolution Synthetic Fetal Ultrasound Imaging from Open Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05471)
*Harvey Mannering,Yilin Zhang,Ziao Liu,Zhiwu Huang,Jacqueline Matthew,Miguel Xochicale*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 基于EDM2扩散架构的高分辨率胎儿超声图像合成框架，用于缓解产前超声数据集稀缺、隐私受限和标注困难的问题，并提升下游胎儿平面分类精度。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1分）。论文属于医学超声影像生成领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能均无关联；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测或遥感场景理解；也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等。唯一可借鉴的是其"合成数据增强下游任务"的思路，但场景完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于医学超声生成领域，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：高分辨率胎儿超声图像合成（generative synthesis）及下游胎儿平面分类（fetal plane classification）。场景：产前超声医学影像。传感器类型：超声（ultrasound），非遥感传感器。目标类别：六种解剖类别（six anatomical classes）。

Model architecture: 基于EDM2扩散架构（diffusion architecture）的生成框架，生成512x512分辨率图像，覆盖六个解剖类别。摘要未说明具体网络模块细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性信息。扩散模型通常计算开销大，不适合星上/边缘部署，但摘要未明确讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个公开胎儿超声数据集（multiple public datasets）。评价指标：FID（图像质量）、下游胎儿平面分类准确率（ensemble accuracy 93.36%）、临床专家真实感评分（mean realism score 2.67/5，100张图像，10年以上经验专家）。

Experiments: 主要结果：相比仅用真实数据训练，微调后集成准确率达93.36%，超越真实数据训练；FID更低（图像质量更好）。临床评估显示真实图像评分高于合成图像，合成图像存在平滑、斑点不规则、解剖不一致等伪影。

Limitations: 合成图像存在平滑、斑点不规则、解剖不一致等伪影；临床专家对合成图像真实感评分偏低（2.67/5）；与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关，无可直接迁移的检测或压缩方法。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴的仅有一点：利用生成式方法合成数据来增强下游任务（如遥感小目标样本稀缺时可用扩散模型合成遥感图像/目标样本）。但需注意：扩散模型计算开销大，与在轨轻量化目标相悖，更适合离线数据增强而非星上部署。整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound imaging is key for assessing fetal health, but AI progress is limited by scarce, privacy-restricted, and hard-to-annotate datasets. We propose a high-resolution fetal ultrasound synthesis framework based on the EDM2 diffusion architecture, trained on multiple public datasets to generate 512x512 images across six anatomical classes. Our method achieved improved image quality with lower FID scores and enhanced downstream fetal plane classification, reaching 93.36% ensemble accuracy after fine-tuning, surpassing real-data-only training. Clinical evaluation by an experienced fetal ultrasound specialist (10+ years) on 100 images yielded a mean realism score of 2.67/5, with real images rated higher than synthetic. Artefacts included smoothing, speckle irregularities, and anatomical inconsistencies. Code, data, models and other resources to reproduce this work are available at https://github.com/xfetus/fetal-ultrasound-edm2.

</details>


### [343] [Hyperspectral Calibration Detection: A Novel Concept For Change Detection With Unsupervised Incremental Safe Pseudo-Labeling Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06028)
*Chia-Hsiang Lin,Shih-Min Hsu,Ching-Yun Liang,Jocelyn Chanussot,Jhih-Yan Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种全无监督的高光谱变化检测算法HyperLUCID，结合轻量模型，适用于星上边缘计算场景，无需标注即可实现高效变化检测。

Research relevance: 4分。论文明确涉及遥感（高光谱）、卫星（onboard edge computing）、轻量化（lightweight model），但任务类型是变化检测（change detection）而非目标检测（object detection），与课题核心方向“目标检测”存在任务差异。不过其轻量化设计和星上部署思路有较强借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 3分。论文与遥感卫星在轨部署强相关，轻量化思路有借鉴价值，但任务类型为变化检测而非目标检测，与课题核心方向存在偏差。建议作为参考阅读，重点关注其星上无监督学习和轻量模型设计思路，而非直接复现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高光谱变化检测（Hyperspectral Change Detection, HCD）；应用场景：星上边缘计算（onboard edge computing）、土地覆盖监测（land cover monitoring）；传感器类型：高光谱（hyperspectral）；目标类别：变化/未变化像素（changed/unchanged pixels），非具体目标类别（如舰船/飞机/车辆）。

Model architecture: HyperLUCID算法：基于迭代增广训练集（iteratively augmented training set）安全收集未变化像素样本，学习迭代精化的光谱校准函数（iteratively refined spectrum calibration function），补偿双时相图像采集条件差异，通过分析校准光谱检测变化像素。整体为无监督循环校准与增量检测框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要提及"lightweight model"，但未具体说明轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等具体技术）。轻量性体现在计算效率上：比大多数基准HCD方法快1-2个数量级（around 1 to 2 orders of magnitude faster）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要明确提及适用于星上边缘计算（onboard edge computing）和即时检测响应（immediate detection responses）。计算效率高（比基准方法快1-2个数量级），但未提供具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个真实基准HCD数据集（several real benchmark HCD datasets），具体名称摘要未说明；评价指标：总体精度（Overall Accuracy, OA），达到93.6%-97.9%。

Experiments: 在多个真实HCD基准数据集上达到SOTA结果（OA 93.6%-97.9%），计算效率比大多数基准HCD方法快1-2个数量级。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 任务为变化检测而非目标检测，与课题核心方向存在任务差异；2. 轻量化方法具体技术细节（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）未说明；3. 缺乏参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化部署指标；4. 仅在高光谱数据上验证，未涉及多光谱/SAR/光学遥感图像；5. 无监督方法在复杂场景下的鲁棒性需进一步验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. 星上无监督/零标注学习思路：可借鉴其"零标签需求"（zero-label requirement）思想，探索在轨目标检测中无需地面标注的自适应学习方法；2. 轻量模型+星上部署的框架设计思路：其"轻量模型+星上边缘计算"的定位与课题高度一致；3. 迭代校准策略：可借鉴其迭代增广训练集和光谱校准的思路，用于在轨模型自适应更新；4. 计算效率提升思路：其比基准方法快1-2个数量级的效率提升思路值得借鉴，但需将变化检测思路迁移到目标检测任务。

Abstract: Hyperspectral change detection (HCD) has found numerous key applications, such as land cover monitoring. The majority of benchmark HCD algorithms are semi-supervised methods, and some of them can even achieve very low sample labeling rates. However, in some practical scenarios, such as those requiring immediate detection responses for onboard edge computing, we need to achieve the zero-label requirement as ground-truth labeling would not be available onboard for newly acquired images. In this work, we propose a fully unsupervised HCD algorithm, together with a lightweight model, quite suitable for onboard detection missions. Based on an iteratively augmented training set that safely collects some unchanged pixel samples, we learn an iteratively refined spectrum calibration function that eventually compensates the variability of acquisition conditions (often observed in bitemporal images), thereby making the changed pixels easily detectable by analyzing the calibrated spectra. The proposed hyperspectral looping unsupervised calibration and incremental detection (HyperLUCID) algorithm is not only computationally efficient (around 1 to 2 orders of magnitude faster than most benchmark HCD methods), but has also achieved state-of-the-art results (around 93.6% to 97.9% overall accuracy) on several real benchmark HCD datasets. Source codes: https://github.com/IHCLab/HyperLUCID.

</details>
